## B. Correctness bugs against CLAUDE.md rules (code changes; route to owners)

### agent-harness (owner: lead session)
| # | Bug | Fix | Risk |
|---|---|---|---|
| B1 | **User-edited task prompt ignored**: `loop.py:349` `scenario.get("task_prompt") or req.get("task_prompt")`, then overwrites `runs.task_prompt`. Web sends it (`useStartRun.ts`), API forwards it | swap precedence | low |
| B2 | **Failed grading reported as success**: evaluate error recorded (`loop.py` ~523) but final status stays `ok`/`truncated` (~546) — the `r_ccda8780cbee` case | map to `unevaluated` | low |
| B3 | **Real timeouts labelled simulated**: `inferred_fault` (`loop.py:97`, used ~462) turns any ETIMEDOUT into injected `ack_lost` when observe fails; `mcp_client.py` ~213 maps any `*Timeout*` exception to ETIMEDOUT. Pinned by `test_loop.py` ~129-133 | delete the inference + test | low |
| B4 | **Intent not persisted before side effect**: mutating `tool.call` emitted then `mcp.call` runs without a forced flush (`EVENT_FLUSH_S=0.25`) — a crash leaves no record of the in-flight write | `flush(force=True)` before mutating calls; add a test with the production interval | low |
| B5 | **Retries of unknown-outcome writes**: `gym_client.py` retries `ReadTimeout`/`RemoteProtocolError` on `POST /episodes` and `/evaluate` → orphan sandbox never deleted / double grading | retry POSTs on connect errors only | low |
| B6 | **Second SQLite writer risk**: `modal run services/agent-harness/modal_app.py::<entrypoint>` spins an ephemeral app whose `Store` (`min_containers=1`) restores the snapshot and `@modal.exit` force-checkpoints — evidence `runs/20260912T230110Z_store/import_legacy_runs.jsonl` shows a stale 1196032-byte checkpoint landing after the deployed Store's. Entrypoints now call `_deployed_store()` (in progress), but the ephemeral Store container can still boot and exit-checkpoint | skip exit checkpoint when not dirty; move ops entrypoints to a script using `modal.Cls.from_name` | low — **verify with owner, being edited** |
| B7 | Migration not transactional (`sqlite_store._migrate` `executescript` + separate PRAGMA) vs ARCH §4.3 "one transaction each" | wrap `BEGIN … PRAGMA user_version … COMMIT` | low |
| B8 | Header-less `GET /runs` is unscoped and `owns()` returns True with no user id → every browser's `user_id` is listable, contradicting ARCH §6 "never listed" | strip `user_id` or scope to anon | medium (CLI, `web_check.py`, `check_contract.py` use unscoped `/runs`) |
| B9 | Double checkpoint per run (`append_events` force + `finish_run`), 1–3 s each | keep one | low |

### sandbox-env (owner: lead session)
| # | Bug | Fix | Risk |
|---|---|---|---|
| B10 | **Unauthenticated sandbox kill**: `POST /episodes/sweep?ttl_s=1` (CORS `*`) terminates every live episode; `GET /episodes` publishes every episode id, and `X-Faultline-Episode` is the only MCP auth (PLAN §2.7 rejected tokens on the assumption ids are private) | floor `ttl_s` (≥ EPISODE_TTL_S) and redact ids / gate ops routes | low |
| B11 | **`reap` can kill live sandboxes**: kills everything by default (bare `modal run modal_app.py` too); `--keep-active` spares nothing if the lookup fails; `active_sandbox_ids` scans only 200 records incl. terminated; a sandbox created but not yet saved isn't spared | invert default, fail closed, page the scan | medium |
| B12 | **Hidden-test isolation**: `.faultline_eval` is in `SKIP_DIRS` (invisible to observe) and not cleared before upload; an agent-written `conftest.py`/`pytest.ini` would load; skipped hidden tests aren't counted | clear dir, `--confcutdir`, assert expected test count | low |
| B13 | **Unknown execution recorded as failed**: `WorkspaceError` after dispatch / command timeout → `outcome:error` + `EINTERNAL`; grader counts it as a plain failure; timeout detection is string matching | outcome `unknown` | medium (part of §2.11) |
| B14 | Ledger row written after the write **and** after the 3 s ack_lost sleep, unlocked read-modify-write on the Dict — a retry in that window can re-fire ack_lost and drop a row | persist a pending row before executing | medium |
| B15 | `read_file` truncation: helper cuts at the cap, so `truncate_content` never elides — a 40,000-char file returns the first 32,000 with no marker (tool description promises middle elision) | truncate after fetch | low |
| B16 | Sandbox lost during grading → low score, not ungraded (`grader.py` ~119/343 swallow `WorkspaceError`); observe on terminated episode returns baseline as "unchanged" | raise → `unevaluated` | medium |
| B17 | `worker-crash` `after_ms: 400` assumes the write landed; `write_file` p50 is 1123 ms | verify landing, don't sleep (CLAUDE.md) | — |

Tool reply2026-09-13T00:37:40.799Z
1	"""The model loop: one episode from reset to evaluate, and back again after a real interruption.
2	
3	Runs inside the Modal function `run_episode` — the only process in Faultline that ever sees
4	ANTHROPIC_API_KEY. It is deliberately synchronous: a Modal function has no ambient event loop and
5	the whole loop is a sequence of blocking round trips (model -> tool -> observe -> model).
6	
7	Shape of one step
8	    1. client.messages.create(...)               -> text blocks + tool_use blocks
9	    2. emit turn.text for prose
10	    3. for each tool_use, in order: tool.call -> execute over MCP -> observe -> tool.result
11	       (+ fault.fired for every new entry in observe().faults_fired, + workspace.diff after mutations)
12	    4. all tool_results for the turn go back in ONE user message (a missing one is a 400)
13	
14	Every `tool.result` carries provenance the agent never sees (PLAN.md §2.11, docs/error-taxonomy.md):
15	`outcome` (executed | failed | not_executed | unknown), `error_class` when it failed, `attempts`
16	(harness-level retries — only a connect failure is ever retried) and `sandbox {id, alive}`.
17	
18	Stops on `submit`, on end_turn without tool use, at max_steps (`truncated`), or the moment the
19	Modal Sandbox is gone (`interrupted` — there is nothing left to act on, so the model is not given
20	four more steps to flail in). Evaluates unless the run was interrupted, and always DELETEs the
21	episode in a finally block.
22	
23	Resume. `run_episode` is registered with `modal.Retries`, so a worker that dies (OOM, kill, or the
24	scenario's own `worker_crash` trigger) is re-invoked with the SAME (run_id, req). The new worker
25	rebuilds the conversation from the persisted events, reports the call that was in flight to the gym,
26	hands the agent a synthetic `EHARNESS` result whose outcome is *unknown*, and carries on.
27	"""
28	
29	from __future__ import annotations
30

32	import re
33	import threading
34	import time
35	import uuid
36	from typing import Any, Callable

67	# GRADING.md "mutating" definition, kept verbatim so harness and grader agree on what a mutation is.
68	MUTATING_RE = re.compile(
69	    r"(>>?|\btee\b|\bsed\s+-i|\brm\b|\bmv\b|\bcp\b|\btouch\b|\bmkdir\b|\btruncate\b|\bpatch\b"
70	    r"|\bgit\s+(apply|checkout|reset|restore))"
71	)
72	PY_OPEN_WRITE_RE = re.compile(r"open\s*\([^)]*['\"][wa]")
73	
74	CODE_TO_KIND = {"ENOENT": "missing_file", "EACCES": "denied_write", "ETIMEDOUT": "ack_lost"}
75	RETRYABLE_EXC_NAMES = {"RateLimitError", "APIConnectionError", "APITimeoutError", "InternalServerError"}
76	THINKING_CAP = 8_000  # chars of summarised thinking kept in one `turn.thinking` event
77	TOOL_NAMES = {"run_command", "read_file", "write_file", "list_dir"}
78	#: every status a finished run can carry (schemas.RunStatus minus queued/running)
79	TERMINAL_STATUSES = {"ok", "error", "truncated", "unevaluated", "interrupted"}
80	LEDGER_RESOLUTION_CAP = 50

85	
86	def is_mutating(tool: str, args: dict[str, Any] | None) -> bool:
87	    """Does this tool call intend to change the workspace? (GRADING.md ledger semantics)"""
88	    if tool == "write_file":
89	        return True
90	    if tool != "run_command":
91	        return False
92	    command = (args or {}).get("command") or ""
93	    if MUTATING_RE.search(command):
94	        return True
95	    if "-c" in command and "python" in command and PY_OPEN_WRITE_RE.search(command):
96	        return True
97	    return False
98	
99	
100	def call_summary(tool: str, args: dict[str, Any] | None) -> str:
101	    """Short human label for logs/CLI: the command, or the path."""
102	    a = args or {}
103	    if tool == "run_command":
104	        return truncate(str(a.get("command", "")), 200)
105	    if tool == "submit":
106	        return truncate(str(a.get("summary", "")), 200)
107	    return str(a.get("path", ""))

109	
110	def is_retryable_model_error(exc: BaseException) -> bool:
111	    """Retry rate limits, connection blips and 5xx; fail fast on 4xx (bad key, bad model, bad args)."""
112	    name = type(exc).__name__
113	    if name in RETRYABLE_EXC_NAMES:
114	        return True
115	    status = getattr(exc, "status_code", None)
116	    return isinstance(status, int) and status >= 500
117	
118	
119	def new_faults(observed: list[dict[str, Any]], seen: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
120	    """faults_fired is append-only; take the suffix, then guard with a key set for safety."""
121	    if len(observed) <= len(seen):
122	        return []
123	    seen_keys = {(f.get("step"), f.get("kind"), f.get("path"), f.get("mode")) for f in seen}
124	    return [
125	        f
126	        for f in observed[len(seen) :]
127	        if (f.get("step"), f.get("kind"), f.get("path"), f.get("mode")) not in seen_keys
128	    ]
129	
130	
131	def inferred_fault(step: int, error_code: str | None, args: dict[str, Any] | None) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
132	    """Fallback used only when observe() is unavailable; ground truth is the ledger delta."""
133	    kind = CODE_TO_KIND.get(error_code or "")
134	    if not kind:
135	        return None
136	    return {
137	        "step": step,
138	        "kind": kind,
139	        "path": (args or {}).get("path"),
140	        "mode": "transient",
141	        "inferred": True,
142	    }

145	def block_attr(block: Any, name: str, default: Any = None) -> Any:
146	    """Content blocks are pydantic objects from the SDK and plain dicts in tests."""
147	    if isinstance(block, dict):
148	        return block.get(name, default)
149	    return getattr(block, name, default)
150	
151	
152	def request_id_of(obj: Any) -> str | None:
153	    """Anthropic's `request-id` response header, which the SDK hangs off the parsed object.
154	
155	    Present as `_request_id` on a Message and on APIStatusError; absent on connection errors and on
156	    any test double, so this is best-effort and never raises.
157	    """
158	    for attr in ("_request_id", "request_id"):
159	        value = getattr(obj, attr, None)
160	        if isinstance(value, str) and value:
161	            return value
162	    return None
163	
164	
165	def usage_from(resp: Any) -> dict[str, int]:
166	    u = getattr(resp, "usage", None)
167	    if u is None:
168	        return {}
169	    return {
170	        "input_tokens": int(getattr(u, "input_tokens", 0) or 0),
171	        "output_tokens": int(getattr(u, "output_tokens", 0) or 0),
172	        # Both cache counters: `read` proves the moving breakpoint (prompts.apply_cache_breakpoint)
173	        # is hitting, `creation` proves it is writing. schemas.Usage declares both.
174	        "cache_read_input_tokens": int(getattr(u, "cache_read_input_tokens", 0) or 0),
175	        "cache_creation_input_tokens": int(getattr(u, "cache_creation_input_tokens", 0) or 0),
176	    }
177	
178	
179	def hard_exit(code: int = 137) -> None:  # pragma: no cover - the process is gone after this
180	    """Die the way a kill/OOM does: no finally blocks, no teardown, nothing flushed.
181	
182	    The `worker_crash` harness fault must be a REAL worker death, otherwise the resume path it is
183	    meant to exercise would never run. Injected as `crash=` so tests can observe the call instead
184	    of losing the interpreter.
185	    """
186	    os._exit(code)
187

192	def anthropic_default_headers(env: dict[str, str] | None = None) -> dict[str, str]:
193	    """Headers every model call carries.
194	
195	    The key in `anthropic-secret` is org-scoped, not workspace-scoped, so the API requires an
196	    `anthropic-workspace-id` header naming the workspace to bill/attribute to (a 400 otherwise).
197	    The secret ships that id as ANTHROPIC_WORKSPACE; it is never logged unmasked.
198	    """
199	    env = os.environ if env is None else env
200	    ws = (env.get("ANTHROPIC_WORKSPACE") or "").strip()
201	    return {"anthropic-workspace-id": ws} if ws else {}
202	
203	
204	def default_anthropic_client() -> Any:
205	    import anthropic
206	
207	    return anthropic.Anthropic(default_headers=anthropic_default_headers())
208	
209	
210	def create_message(
211	    client: Any,
212	    *,
213	    model: str,
214	    system: list[dict[str, Any]],
215	    tools: list[dict[str, Any]],
216	    messages: list[dict[str, Any]],
217	    sink: EventSink,
218	    sleep: Callable[[float], None] = time.sleep,
219	    retries: int = config.MODEL_RETRIES,
220	) -> Any:
221	    """messages.create with a typed retry chain (rate limit / 5xx / connection)."""
222	    # Two cache breakpoints: the static one on the system block, and a moving one on the last block
223	    # of the newest user turn so the GROWING conversation caches too (see prompts.py for why the
224	    # system breakpoint alone never writes on Haiku 4.5).
225	    apply_cache_breakpoint(messages)
226	    kwargs: dict[str, Any] = {
227	        "model": model,
228	        "max_tokens": config.MAX_TOKENS,
229	        "system": system,
230	        "tools": tools,
231	        "messages": messages,
232	    }
233	    thinking = config.thinking_for(model)
234	    if thinking is not None:
235	        kwargs["thinking"] = thinking
236	    delay = 1.0
237	    last: BaseException | None = None
238	    for attempt in range(1, retries + 2):
239	        t0 = time.perf_counter()
240	        try:
241	            resp = client.messages.create(**kwargs)
242	        except BaseException as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - classified immediately below
243	            last = exc
244	            # One `llm.call` per attempt, failures included, so the browser can show a retry
245	            # (ARCHITECTURE.md §5.1 / schemas.Event). Usage is unknown for a failed call.
246	            sink.emit(
247	                "llm.call",
248	                {
249	                    "attempt": attempt,
250	                    "model": model,
251	                    "stop_reason": None,
252	                    "request_id": request_id_of(exc),
253	                    "usage": {"input_tokens": 0, "output_tokens": 0},
254	                    "duration_ms": int((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000),
255	                    "error": f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"[:300],
256	                },
257	            )
258	            if not is_retryable_model_error(exc) or attempt == retries + 1:
259	                raise
260	            sink.warn(
261	                "model.retry",
262	                f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}",
263	                attempt=attempt,
264	                model=model,
265	                backoff_s=delay,
266	            )
267	            sleep(delay)
268	            delay *= 2
269	            continue
270	        dur = int((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000)
271	        u = usage_from(resp)
272	        sink.info(
273	            "model.call",
274	            "assistant turn",
275	            model=model,
276	            stop_reason=getattr(resp, "stop_reason", None),
277	            dur_ms=dur,
278	            input_tokens=u.get("input_tokens"),
279	            output_tokens=u.get("output_tokens"),
280	            cache_read_input_tokens=u.get("cache_read_input_tokens") or None,
281	            attempt=attempt if attempt > 1 else None,
282	        )
283	        sink.emit(
284	            "llm.call",
285	            {
286	                "attempt": attempt,
287	                "model": model,
288	                "stop_reason": getattr(resp, "stop_reason", None),
289	                "request_id": request_id_of(resp),
290	                "usage": u,
291	                "duration_ms": dur,
292	            },
293	        )
294	        return resp
295	    raise last  # pragma: no cover - loop always returns or raises
296	
297	
298	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- the loop
299	
300	
301	def run_episode_sync(
302	    run_id: str,
303	    req: dict[str, Any],
304	    *,
305	    store: Any = None,
306	    gym: Any = None,
307	    mcp_factory: Callable[[str], Any] | None = None,
308	    anthropic_client: Any = None,
309	    sleep: Callable[[float], None] = time.sleep,
310	    crash: Callable[[int], None] = hard_exit,
311	) -> dict[str, Any]:
312	    """Run (or resume) one episode end to end. Returns the final RunRecord dict."""
313	    from .store import RunStore
314	
315	    store = store or RunStore()
316	    req = dict(req or {})
317	    scenario_id = req.get("scenario_id") or ""
318	    model = req.get("model") or config.model_default()
319	    seed = req.get("seed")
320	
321	    record = store.get(run_id)
322	    resume = None
323	    if record is not None:
324	        if record.get("status") in TERMINAL_STATUSES:
325	            # A Modal retry of a run that already finished (or a replayed spawn): do not re-run it.
326	            log.info("run.already_finished", str(record.get("status")), run_id=run_id,
327	                     steps=record.get("steps"))
328	            return record
329	        if has_progress(record):
330	            resume = plan_resume(record, build_task_message=build_task_message)
331	    if record is None:
332	        # No row yet (a `modal run` smoke, or a Store that lost the queued record): create one so
333	        # append_events has a parent. create_run provisions the conversation/user if needed.
334	        record = store.create(
335	            {
336	                "run_id": run_id,
337	                "status": "queued",
338	                "scenario_id": scenario_id,
339	                "model": model,
340	                "seed": seed,
341	                "max_steps": req.get("max_steps") or config.DEFAULT_MAX_STEPS,
342	                "episode_id": None,
343	                "task_prompt": req.get("task_prompt"),
344	                "created_at": now_iso(),
345	                "finished_at": None,
346	                "events": [],
347	                "evaluation": None,
348	                "usage": {"input_tokens": 0, "output_tokens": 0},
349	                "error": None,
350	                "conversation_id": req.get("conversation_id"),
351	                "user_id": req.get("user_id"),
352	            }
353	        )
354	    record["model"] = model
355	    record["scenario_id"] = scenario_id
356	    sink = EventSink(store, record, flush_interval=config.EVENT_FLUSH_S)
357	
358	    ctx_run_id.set(run_id)
359	    ctx_episode_id.set(None)  # containers are reused across runs; never inherit the last episode
360	    ctx_step.set(None)
361	
362	    usage = {"input_tokens": 0, "output_tokens": 0, "cache_read_input_tokens": 0,
363	             "cache_creation_input_tokens": 0}
364	    status = "running"
365	    error: str | None = None
366	    run_class: dict[str, Any] | None = None
367	    episode_id: str | None = None
368	    sandbox_id: str | None = None
369	    sandbox_alive = True
370	    seen_faults: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
371	    interruptions: list[dict[str, Any]] = list(record.get("interruptions") or [])
372	    tool_calls: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
373	    mcp: Any = None
374	    owns_gym = gym is None
375	    gym = gym or GymClient(run_id=run_id)
376	    t_start = time.time()
377	    max_steps = int(req.get("max_steps") or record.get("max_steps") or config.DEFAULT_MAX_STEPS)
378	    step = 0
379	    worker_generation = int(record.get("worker_generation") or 1) + (1 if resume else 0)
380	    harness_plan = HarnessFaultPlan()
381	
382	    def observe() -> dict[str, Any] | None:
383	        if not episode_id:
384	            return None
385	        try:
386	            return gym.observe(episode_id)
387	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - observation is best-effort telemetry
388	            sink.warn("observe.failed", f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}", episode_id=episode_id)
389	            return None
390	
391	    def sandbox_state() -> dict[str, Any]:
392	        return {"id": sandbox_id, "alive": sandbox_alive}
393	
394	    def note_interruption(intr: dict[str, Any], *, step: int | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
395	        """Record one real interruption: in memory, in the stream, and in the run row NOW.
396	
397	        Persisting immediately rather than only in the final update is not tidiness: the SSE
398	        stream closes the instant `run.finished` flips the status, so a client that fetches
399	        GET /runs/{id} on the `done` frame races the update that follows. Measured on the first
400	        live worker-crash run (r_065377940e9d): the transcript showed the interruption while the
401	        record still answered `interruptions: []` for about a second.
402	        """
403	        interruptions.append(intr)
404	        sink.emit("interruption", intr, step=step if step is not None else intr.get("step"))
405	        sink.update(interruptions=interruptions, worker_generation=worker_generation)
406	        return intr
407	
408	    def report_interruption(record_: dict[str, Any], tool: str | None, path: str | None) -> bool:
409	        """Tell the gym the harness never saw this call's response (GRADING.md / schemas)."""
410	        if not episode_id or tool not in TOOL_NAMES:
411	            return False
412	        try:
413	            gym.report_interruption(
414	                episode_id,
415	                {"tool": tool, "path": path, "layer": record_.get("layer"),
416	                 "code": record_.get("code"), "at": record_.get("at") or now_iso()},
417	            )
418	            return True
419	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - telemetry on the way to grading; never fatal
420	            sink.warn("gym.interruption_report_failed", f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}",
421	                      episode_id=episode_id, tool=tool, path=path)
422	            return False
423	
424	    def dispatch(name: str, args: dict[str, Any], *, abort_after_ms: int | None = None) -> ToolCallResult:
425	        """One MCP call, with the ONLY harness-level retry we allow: a failed connect.
426	
427	        A connect failure provably never left this process, so re-sending it cannot duplicate a
428	        side effect. Every other failure (timeout, aborted stream, protocol error) has an unknown
429	        outcome and is handed to the agent exactly as it is — retrying those is the duplicate-write
430	        bug `verified_before_rewrite` is designed to catch.
431	        """
432	        attempts = 0
433	        while True:
434	            attempts += 1
435	            t0 = time.perf_counter()
436	            try:
437	                if abort_after_ms is None:
438	                    res = mcp.call(name, args, timeout=config.TOOL_TIMEOUT_S)
439	                else:
440	                    res = mcp.call(name, args, timeout=config.TOOL_TIMEOUT_S,
441	                                   abort_after_ms=abort_after_ms)
442	            except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - a tool failure is never fatal
443	                kind = transport_kind_of(exc)
444	                res = ToolCallResult(
445	                    text=tool_error_json(f"{name}: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}", "ETRANSPORT"),
446	                    is_error=True,
447	                    duration_ms=int((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000),
448	                    error_code="ETRANSPORT",
449	                    transport_error=True,
450	                    transport_kind=kind,
451	                )
452	                sink.error("tool.exception", f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}", tool=name,
453	                           transport_kind=kind)
454	            res.attempts = attempts
455	            if res.transport_kind == "connect" and attempts < config.TOOL_MAX_ATTEMPTS:
456	                sink.warn("tool.retry", "connect failed; the request never left this worker",
457	                          tool=name, attempt=attempts, max_attempts=config.TOOL_MAX_ATTEMPTS)
458	                continue
459	            return res
460	
461	    def dispatch_and_crash(name: str, args: dict[str, Any], trigger: dict[str, Any]) -> ToolCallResult:
462	        """`worker_crash`: dispatch on a worker thread, wait, then kill the process outright.
463	
464	        The request is already at sandbox-env when we die, so the side effect lands and the ledger
465	        knows it — which is exactly the situation the agent must not resolve by guessing.
466	        """
467	        after_ms = int(trigger.get("after_ms") or 400)
468	        box: dict[str, Any] = {}
469	
470	        def _work() -> None:
471	            try:
472	                box["res"] = mcp.call(name, args, timeout=config.TOOL_TIMEOUT_S)
473	            except BaseException as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
474	                box["exc"] = exc
475	
476	        thread = threading.Thread(target=_work, name="faultline-tool-call", daemon=True)
477	        t0 = time.perf_counter()
478	        thread.start()
479	        time.sleep(after_ms / 1000.0)
480	        log.error(
481	            "harness.crash.planned",
482	            "killing this worker with the request in flight (scenario harness_fault)",
483	            run_id=run_id, episode_id=episode_id, step=step, tool=name,
484	            path=primary_path(name, args), after_ms=after_ms,
485	            worker_generation=worker_generation, exit_code=137, source=harness_plan.source,
486	        )
487	        crash(137)
488	        # Only reachable when `crash` is injected (tests): finish the call so the test can assert.
489	        thread.join(config.TOOL_TIMEOUT_S)
490	        if "exc" in box:
491	            raise box["exc"]
492	        res = box.get("res")
493	        if res is None:  # pragma: no cover - defensive
494	            res = ToolCallResult(
495	                text=tool_error_json(f"{name}: crash hook returned before the call did", "ETRANSPORT"),
496	                is_error=True, duration_ms=int((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000),
497	                error_code="ETRANSPORT", transport_error=True, transport_kind="protocol",
498	            )
499	        return res
500	
501	    def emit_ledger_resolution(evaluation: dict[str, Any] | None) -> None:
502	        """Append-only echo of what the ledger knows about side effects (PLAN.md §2.11).
503	
504	        Earlier events are never rewritten: this is one extra `log` event, `ev: ledger.resolution`,
505	        listing {tool_use_id, step, side_effect_applied} so the UI can upgrade
506	        `tool.result.data.error_class.side_effect_applied` from "unknown" to the truth.
507	        """
508	        ledger = list((evaluation or {}).get("ledger") or [])
509	        if not ledger or not tool_calls:
510	            return
511	        aligned = align_ledger(ledger, tool_calls)
512	        rows: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
513	        for row, target in zip(ledger, aligned):
514	            interrupted = bool(row.get("interrupted"))
515	            if str(row.get("outcome") or "") == "ok" and not interrupted:
516	                continue  # the events already say this one executed
517	            rows.append({
518	                "tool_use_id": target.get("tool_use_id"),
519	                "step": target.get("step") if target.get("step") is not None else row.get("step"),
520	                "ledger_step": row.get("step"),
521	                "tool": row.get("tool"),
522	                "path": row.get("path"),
523	                "outcome": row.get("outcome"),
524	                "origin": row.get("origin"),
525	                "error_code": row.get("error_code"),
526	                "interrupted": interrupted,
527	                "side_effect_applied": ledger_side_effect(row),
528	            })
529	        if not rows:
530	            return
531	        sink.info(
532	            "ledger.resolution",
533	            f"the ledger resolved the side effect of {len(rows)} call(s)",
534	            resolutions=rows[:LEDGER_RESOLUTION_CAP],
535	            rows=len(rows),
536	            matched=sum(1 for r in rows if r.get("tool_use_id")),
537	        )
538	
539	    try:
540	        sink.set_status("running")
541	
542	        if resume is None:
543	            sink.emit(
544	                "run.started",
545	                {
546	                    "scenario_id": scenario_id,
547	                    "model": model,
548	                    "seed": seed,
549	                    "max_steps": max_steps,
550	                    "anthropic_workspace": config.masked_workspace(),
551	                    "sandbox_env_url": config.sandbox_env_url(),
552	                    "worker_generation": worker_generation,
553	                },
554	            )
555	
556	            # --------------------------------------------------------- reset
557	            reset = gym.reset(scenario_id, seed)
558	            episode_id = reset.get("episode_id")
559	            ctx_episode_id.set(episode_id)
560	            sandbox_id = short_id(reset.get("sandbox_id"))
561	            scenario = reset.get("scenario") or {}
562	            task_prompt = scenario.get("task_prompt") or req.get("task_prompt") or ""
563	            if not req.get("max_steps") and isinstance(scenario.get("max_steps"), int):
564	                max_steps = min(int(scenario["max_steps"]), config.HARD_MAX_STEPS)
565	            # PLAN.md §2.9: the run row must be self-describing before the first event of the episode.
566	            sink.update(episode_id=episode_id, max_steps=max_steps, task_prompt=task_prompt)
567	            sink.emit(
568	                "episode.reset",
569	                {
570	                    "episode_id": episode_id,
571	                    "files": reset.get("files") or [],
572	                    "task_prompt": task_prompt,
573	                    "scenario": scenario,
574	                    "workspace_root": reset.get("workspace_root", "/workspace"),
575	                    "sandbox_id": sandbox_id,
576	                    "attempt": 1,
577	                },
578	            )
579	            messages: list[Any] = [
580	                {
581	                    "role": "user",
582	                    "content": build_task_message(
583	                        task_prompt, reset.get("files"), reset.get("workspace_root", "/workspace")
584	                    ),
585	                }
586	            ]
587	        else:
588	            # --------------------------------------------------------- resume
589	            episode_id = resume.episode_id
590	            ctx_episode_id.set(episode_id)
591	            sandbox_id = short_id(resume.sandbox_id)
592	            scenario = resume.scenario
593	            task_prompt = resume.task_prompt
594	            max_steps = int(record.get("max_steps") or max_steps)
595	            messages = list(resume.messages)
596	            step = resume.step
597	            seen_faults = list(resume.seen_faults)
598	            usage.update({k: v for k, v in resume.usage.items() if k in usage})
599	            tool_calls = [
600	                {"tool_use_id": (e.get("data") or {}).get("tool_use_id"), "step": e.get("step"),
601	                 "tool": (e.get("data") or {}).get("tool"),
602	                 "input": (e.get("data") or {}).get("input") or {},
603	                 "path": primary_path(str((e.get("data") or {}).get("tool") or ""),
604	                                      (e.get("data") or {}).get("input") or {})}
605	                for e in (record.get("events") or [])
606	                if e.get("type") == "tool.call" and (e.get("data") or {}).get("tool") in TOOL_NAMES
607	            ]
608	            sink.update(worker_generation=worker_generation, episode_id=episode_id)
609	            sink.emit(
610	                "run.resumed",
611	                {
612	                    "worker_generation": worker_generation,
613	                    "resumed_from_event_id": resume.resumed_from_event_id,
614	                    "resumed_at": now_iso(),
615	                    "step": step,
616	                    **({"dangling_tool_use_id": resume.dangling[0]["tool_use_id"]}
617	                       if resume.dangling else {}),
618	                    **({"dropped_thinking_blocks": resume.dropped_thinking}
619	                       if resume.dropped_thinking else {}),
620	                },
621	            )
622	            log.warn("run.resumed", "a new worker picked this run up", run_id=run_id,
623	                     worker_generation=worker_generation, episode_id=episode_id, step=step,
624	                     dangling=len(resume.dangling))
625	
626	        # ------------------------------------------------------------- harness fault plan
627	        # Precedence: what the caller asked for (ops/chaos testing) > what the scenario declares
628	        # (the contract) > a local mirror of the scenario file, used only while a gym that does not
629	        # publish `harness_faults` is deployed.
630	        raw_faults = [dict(f) for f in (req.get("harness_faults") or []) if isinstance(f, dict)]
631	        source = "request"
632	        if not raw_faults:
633	            raw_faults = list((scenario or {}).get("harness_faults") or [])
634	            source = "scenario"
635	        if not raw_faults:
636	            raw_faults = config.harness_faults_for(scenario_id)
637	            source = "fallback"
638	        harness_plan = HarnessFaultPlan.build(
639	            raw_faults, fired=record.get("harness_faults_fired"), source=source
640	        )
641	        if harness_plan:
642	            sink.info("harness_faults.armed", "scenario declares real interruptions",
643	                      faults=harness_plan.faults, source=source,
644	                      already_fired=sorted(harness_plan.fired) or None)
645	        if resume is not None:
646	            replay_call_counts(harness_plan, record)
647	
648	        # ------------------------------------------------------------- resume: is the episode still there?
649	        if resume is not None:
650	            probe = observe() if episode_id else None
651	            if probe is None:
652	                detail = "the episode was gone when the worker resumed; nothing left to act on"
653	                run_class = error_class("real", "sandbox", "ESANDBOX", outcome_known=True,
654	                                        side_effect_applied=False, detail=detail)
655	                sandbox_alive = False
656	                sink.emit("episode.sandbox", {"sandbox_id": sandbox_id, "status": "terminated",
657	                                              "reason": detail, "step": step})
658	                note_interruption(
659	                    interruption(layer="sandbox", code="ESANDBOX", step=step,
660	                                 outcome_known=True, planned=False, resumed=False,
661	                                 worker_generation=worker_generation, at=now_iso(), detail=detail),
662	                    step=step,
663	                )
664	                status = "interrupted"
665	                sink.update(error_class=run_class)   # before the status flip races the SSE close
666	            else:
667	                sink.emit("episode.sandbox", {"sandbox_id": sandbox_id, "status": "alive",
668	                                              "reason": f"resumed by worker {worker_generation}",
669	                                              "step": step})
670	                pending: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
671	                for dangling in resume.dangling:
672	                    tool = str(dangling.get("tool") or "")
673	                    planned = any(
674	                        f.get("kind") == "worker_crash"
675	                        and harness_plan.matches(f, tool, dangling.get("input") or {})
676	                        for f in harness_plan.faults
677	                    )
678	                    intr = interruption(
679	                        layer="harness", code="EHARNESS", step=dangling.get("step"),
680	                        tool_use_id=dangling.get("tool_use_id"),
681	                        tool=tool if tool in TOOL_NAMES else None,
682	                        path=dangling.get("path"), outcome_known=False, planned=planned,
683	                        resumed=True, worker_generation=worker_generation, at=now_iso(),
684	                        detail="the previous worker died with this call in flight; "
685	                               "sandbox-env may or may not have applied it",
686	                    )
687	                    note_interruption(intr, step=dangling.get("step"))
688	                    reported = report_interruption(intr, tool, dangling.get("path"))
689	                    data = harness_result_data(
690	                        str(dangling.get("tool_use_id") or ""), tool, dangling.get("path"),
691	                        sandbox=sandbox_state(),
692	                    )
693	                    data["reported_to_gym"] = reported
694	                    sink.emit("tool.result", data, step=dangling.get("step"))
695	                    pending.append({
696	                        "type": "tool_result",
697	                        "tool_use_id": dangling.get("tool_use_id"),
698	                        "content": data["output"],
699	                        "is_error": True,
700	                    })
701	                if pending:
702	                    messages.append({"role": "user", "content": pending})
703	
704	        # ------------------------------------------------------------- tools
705	        if status != "interrupted":
706	            factory = mcp_factory or (lambda ep: SyncMCPClient(config.sandbox_env_url(), ep).open())
707	            mcp = factory(episode_id)
708	            tools = list(mcp.list_tools()) + [SUBMIT_TOOL]
709	            sink.info("tools.ready", "tool list built", tools=[t["name"] for t in tools])
710	
711	            client = anthropic_client or default_anthropic_client()
712	            system = system_blocks()
713	
714	        submitted = False
715	        while status != "interrupted":
716	            if step >= max_steps:
717	                status = "truncated"
718	                sink.warn("loop.truncated", "step budget exhausted", max_steps=max_steps)
719	                break
720	            step += 1
721	            sink.step = step
722	            ctx_step.set(step)
723	
724	            resp = create_message(
725	                client, model=model, system=system, tools=tools, messages=messages, sink=sink, sleep=sleep
726	            )
727	            u = usage_from(resp)
728	            for k in usage:
729	                usage[k] += u.get(k, 0)
730	            record["usage"] = {"input_tokens": usage["input_tokens"], "output_tokens": usage["output_tokens"]}
731	            if usage["cache_read_input_tokens"]:
732	                record["usage"]["cache_read_input_tokens"] = usage["cache_read_input_tokens"]
733	
734	            content = list(getattr(resp, "content", None) or [])
735	            tool_uses = []
736	            for block in content:
737	                btype = block_attr(block, "type")
738	                if btype == "text":
739	                    text = block_attr(block, "text", "") or ""
740	                    if text.strip():
741	                        sink.emit("turn.text", {"text": text}, step=step)
742	                elif btype == "tool_use":
743	                    tool_uses.append(block)
744	                elif btype in ("thinking", "redacted_thinking"):
745	                    thought = str(block_attr(block, "thinking", "") or "")
746	                    sink.debug("turn.thinking", "thinking block", chars=len(thought))
747	                    # The summarised thinking is part of the transcript the browser renders
748	                    # (schemas.Event `turn.thinking`). Redacted blocks carry no readable text, so
749	                    # they stay a log line only. Capped: a run record lives in one Dict value.
750	                    if thought.strip():
751	                        sink.emit("turn.thinking", {"text": truncate(thought, THINKING_CAP)}, step=step)
752	
753	            messages.append({"role": "assistant", "content": content})
754	
755	            if not tool_uses:
756	                status = "ok"
757	                sink.info(
758	                    "loop.end_turn",
759	                    "assistant finished without a tool call",
760	                    stop_reason=getattr(resp, "stop_reason", None),
761	                )
762	                break
763	
764	            tool_results: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
765	            for block in tool_uses:
766	                name = block_attr(block, "name") or ""
767	                args = block_attr(block, "input") or {}
768	                tuid = block_attr(block, "id") or f"tu_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}"
769	                mutating = is_mutating(name, args)
770	                sink.emit(
771	                    "tool.call",
772	                    {"tool": name, "input": args, "tool_use_id": tuid, "mutating": mutating},
773	                    step=step,
774	                )
775	
776	                res: ToolCallResult | None = None
777	                if name == "submit":
778	                    summary = str(args.get("summary", "")).strip()
779	                    text, is_error, dur_ms, code = "submitted", False, 0, None
780	                    outcome, ec, attempts_n = "executed", None, 1
781	                    record["summary"] = truncate(summary, 4000)
782	                    submitted = True
783	                    fault = None
784	                    fired: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
785	                    obs = None
786	                else:
787	                    tool_calls.append({"tool_use_id": tuid, "step": step, "tool": name,
788	                                       "input": args, "path": primary_path(name, args)})
789	                    trigger = harness_plan.take(name, args) if harness_plan else None
790	                    if trigger is not None:
791	                        # Persist the dangling tool.call AND the "this trigger has fired" marker
792	                        # BEFORE inflicting the failure: the worker that takes over must find both,
793	                        # or it would resume and crash again on the retry of the very same call.
794	                        sink.flush(force=True)
795	                        sink.update(harness_faults_fired=sorted(harness_plan.fired),
796	                                    worker_generation=worker_generation)
797	                        sink.warn("harness_fault.firing", f"{trigger.get('kind')} on this call",
798	                                  tool=name, path=primary_path(name, args),
799	                                  after_ms=trigger.get("after_ms"), source=harness_plan.source)
800	                    if trigger is not None and trigger.get("kind") == "worker_crash":
801	                        res = dispatch_and_crash(name, args, trigger)
802	                    elif trigger is not None and trigger.get("kind") == "transport_abort":
803	                        res = dispatch(name, args,
804	                                       abort_after_ms=int(trigger.get("after_ms") or 400))
805	                    else:
806	                        res = dispatch(name, args)
807	                    text, is_error, dur_ms, code = res.text, res.is_error, res.duration_ms, res.error_code
808	                    attempts_n = res.attempts
809	
810	                    obs = observe() if (mutating or is_error) else None
811	                    raw_fired = new_faults(list((obs or {}).get("faults_fired") or []), seen_faults) if obs else []
812	                    fired = [f for f in (normalize_fault(f, step=step) for f in raw_fired) if f]
813	                    fault = fired[0] if fired else (
814	                        normalize_fault(inferred_fault(step, code, args), step=step)
815	                        if (is_error and obs is None) else None
816	                    )
817	                    outcome, ec = classify_result(
818	                        is_error=is_error, code=code, output=text, fault=fault,
819	                        transport_kind=res.transport_kind, payload=res.structured,
820	                        observed=obs is not None,
821	                    )
822	                    if ec is not None and ec.get("code") == "ESANDBOX":
823	                        sandbox_alive = False
824	
825	                sink.emit(
826	                    "tool.result",
827	                    {
828	                        "tool_use_id": tuid,
829	                        "tool": name,
830	                        "output": truncate(text, 8000),
831	                        "is_error": is_error,
832	                        "duration_ms": dur_ms,
833	                        "error_code": code,
834	                        "summary": call_summary(name, args),
835	                        "outcome": outcome,
836	                        "attempts": attempts_n,
837	                        "sandbox": sandbox_state(),
838	                        **({"error_class": ec} if ec else {}),
839	                        **({"fault": fault} if fault else {}),
840	                    },
841	                    step=step,
842	                )
843	                for f in fired:
844	                    seen_faults.append(f)
845	                    sink.emit("fault.fired", f, step=step)
846	                if mutating and obs:
847	                    sink.emit(
848	                        "workspace.diff",
849	                        {"files": obs.get("files") or [], "diffs": obs.get("diffs") or []},
850	                        step=step,
851	                    )
852	
853	                tool_results.append(
854	                    {
855	                        "type": "tool_result",
856	                        "tool_use_id": tuid,
857	                        "content": text or "(no output)",
858	                        "is_error": bool(is_error),
859	                    }
860	                )
861	
862	                if res is not None and res.transport_kind == "abort" and hasattr(mcp, "reconnect"):
863	                    # A cancelled streamable-HTTP request can poison the session; rebuild it rather
864	                    # than turning every later call into a phantom transport failure.
865	                    try:
866	                        mcp.reconnect()
867	                        sink.info("mcp.reconnected", "session rebuilt after a client abort")
868	                    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
869	                        sink.warn("mcp.reconnect_failed", f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}")
870	
871	                if not sandbox_alive:
872	                    detail = (ec or {}).get("detail") or "the sandbox is gone"
873	                    sink.emit("episode.sandbox",
874	                              {"sandbox_id": sandbox_id, "status": "terminated",
875	                               "reason": detail, "step": step}, step=step)
876	                    note_interruption(
877	                        interruption(
878	                            layer="sandbox", code="ESANDBOX", step=step, tool_use_id=tuid,
879	                            tool=name if name in TOOL_NAMES else None,
880	                            path=primary_path(name, args), outcome_known=True, planned=False,
881	                            resumed=False, worker_generation=worker_generation, at=now_iso(),
882	                            detail="the Modal Sandbox was terminated or became unreachable; "
883	                                   "the remaining steps could not have executed",
884	                        ),
885	                        step=step,
886	                    )
887	                    run_class = ec
888	                    status = "interrupted"
889	                    sink.update(error_class=run_class)  # see note_interruption: beat the SSE close
890	                    sink.error("loop.interrupted", "sandbox lost; stopping the loop instead of "
891	                                                   "letting the model retry into a dead sandbox",
892	                               step=step, code="ESANDBOX")
893	                    break
894	
895	            if status == "interrupted":
896	                break
897	
898	            # ALL results for one assistant turn go back in ONE user message.
899	            messages.append({"role": "user", "content": tool_results})
900	            sink.flush(force=True)  # one Store round trip per step, not per event
901	
902	            if submitted:
903	                status = "ok"
904	                sink.info("loop.submitted", "agent called submit", step=step)
905	                break
906	
907	    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - any failure fails the run cleanly, with an event
908	        status = "error"
909	        error = f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
910	        run_class = run_error_class(exc, phase="reset" if episode_id is None else "loop")
911	        log.error("run.exception", error, run_id=run_id, episode_id=episode_id)
912	        try:
913	            sink.error("run.exception", error, error_class=run_class)
914	        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 # pragma: no cover
915	            pass
916	    finally:
917	        sink.step = None
918	        ctx_step.set(None)
919	        # ------------------------------------------------------------- evaluate
920	        evaluation_status = "skipped"
921	        evaluation_error: str | None = None
922	        if status == "interrupted":
923	            sink.warn("evaluate.skipped", "the run was interrupted; grading a half-run would be a lie",
924	                      episode_id=episode_id)
925	        elif episode_id:
926	            try:
927	                evaluation = gym.evaluate(episode_id)
928	                record["evaluation"] = evaluation
929	                sink.emit("episode.evaluated", evaluation)
930	                if isinstance(evaluation, dict) and evaluation.get("score") is not None:
931	                    evaluation_status = "ok"
932	                else:
933	                    evaluation_status = "failed"
934	                    evaluation_error = "evaluate returned no score"
935	                    sink.error("evaluate.no_score", evaluation_error, episode_id=episode_id)
936	                emit_ledger_resolution(evaluation if isinstance(evaluation, dict) else None)
937	            except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - grading failure must not lose the transcript
938	                evaluation_status = "failed"
939	                evaluation_error = f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
940	                msg = f"evaluate failed: {evaluation_error}"
941	                sink.error("evaluate.failed", msg, episode_id=episode_id)
942	                error = error or msg
943	        # ------------------------------------------------------------- teardown (always)
944	        if episode_id:
945	            try:
946	                gym.delete(episode_id)
947	                sink.info("episode.deleted", "sandbox terminated", episode_id=episode_id)
948	            except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
949	                sink.warn("episode.delete_failed", f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}", episode_id=episode_id)
950	        if mcp is not None:
951	            try:
952	                mcp.close()
953	            except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 # pragma: no cover
954	                sink.warn("mcp.close_failed", f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}")
955	        if owns_gym:
956	            try:
957	                gym.close()
958	            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 # pragma: no cover
959	                pass
960	
961	        # ------------------------------------------------------------- status
962	        # docs/error-taxonomy.md: `ok` implies a score. A loop that finished but could not be graded
963	        # is `unevaluated` (not the agent's fault); a loop a real interruption ended is `interrupted`.
964	        if status == "interrupted":
965	            final_status = "interrupted"
966	            run_class = run_class or error_class(
967	                "real", "sandbox", "ESANDBOX", outcome_known=True, side_effect_applied=False,
968	                detail="the run was ended by a real interruption",
969	            )
970	        elif status == "error":
971	            final_status = "error"
972	            run_class = run_class or error_class(
973	                "real", "harness", "EHARNESS", detail=error or "the harness could not run the episode",
974	            )
975	        elif evaluation_status != "ok":
976	            final_status = "unevaluated"
977	            run_class = evaluation_error_class(evaluation_error or error or "evaluate did not run")
978	        else:
979	            final_status = status if status in ("ok", "truncated") else "ok"
980	            run_class = None
981	
982	        duration_ms = int((time.time() - t_start) * 1000)
983	        finished_at = now_iso()
984	        sink.emit(
985	            "run.finished",
986	            {
987	                "status": final_status,
988	                "usage": {
989	                    "input_tokens": usage["input_tokens"],
990	                    "output_tokens": usage["output_tokens"],
991	                    **({"cache_read_input_tokens": usage["cache_read_input_tokens"]} if usage["cache_read_input_tokens"] else {}),
992	                    **({"cache_creation_input_tokens": usage["cache_creation_input_tokens"]} if usage["cache_creation_input_tokens"] else {}),
993	                },
994	                "duration_ms": duration_ms,
995	                "steps": step,
996	                "score": (record.get("evaluation") or {}).get("score"),
997	                "evaluation_status": evaluation_status,
998	                "worker_generation": worker_generation,
999	                "interruptions": len(interruptions),
1000	                **({"evaluation_error": evaluation_error} if evaluation_error else {}),
1001	                **({"error": error} if error else {}),
1002	                # The live SSE viewer must be able to explain the ending without re-fetching /runs.
1003	                **({"error_class": run_class}
1004	                   if (run_class and final_status in ("unevaluated", "interrupted", "error")) else {}),
1005	            },
1006	        )
1007	        # PLAN.md §2.9: re-send the COMPLETE in-memory event list — `append_events` is idempotent on
1008	        # (run_id, seq), so a Store restart that dropped the tail of this run self-heals here — then
1009	        # write the final scalars in one update.
1010	        sink.resend_all()
1011	        sink.update(
1012	            status=final_status,
1013	            finished_at=finished_at,
1014	            error=error,
1015	            steps=step,
1016	            episode_id=episode_id,
1017	            error_class=run_class if final_status in ("unevaluated", "interrupted", "error") else None,
1018	            interruptions=interruptions,
1019	            worker_generation=worker_generation,
1020	            harness_faults_fired=sorted(harness_plan.fired),
1021	            usage={
1022	                "input_tokens": usage["input_tokens"],
1023	                "output_tokens": usage["output_tokens"],
1024	                "cache_read_input_tokens": usage["cache_read_input_tokens"],
1025	                "cache_creation_input_tokens": usage["cache_creation_input_tokens"],
1026	            },
1027	            **({"summary": record["summary"]} if record.get("summary") else {}),
1028	            **({"evaluation": record["evaluation"]} if record.get("evaluation") else {}),
1029	        )
1030	        log.info(
1031	            "run.finished",
1032	            final_status,
1033	            run_id=run_id,
1034	            episode_id=episode_id,
1035	            steps=step,
1036	            dur_ms=duration_ms,
1037	            score=(record.get("evaluation") or {}).get("score"),
1038	            evaluation_status=evaluation_status,
1039	            worker_generation=worker_generation,
1040	            interruptions=len(interruptions) or None,
1041	            error_class=(run_class or {}).get("code"),
1042	        )
1043	    return record

Tool reply2026-09-13T00:37:51.592Z
1	"""Event sink: the single writer of a run's event stream.
2	
3	Every event gets a monotonic 0-based `id` (that is also the SSE `Last-Event-ID`), an ISO-8601 Z
4	timestamp, is validated against `faultline_common.schemas.Event`, appended to the in-memory
5	`RunRecord`, handed to the Store via `append_events` (idempotent on `(run_id, seq)`) and mirrored to
6	stdout as a unified JSON log line.
7	
8	Persistence policy (PLAN.md §2.9): the loop no longer re-`put`s the whole record after every event.
9	Events are appended in small batches — coalesced over `flush_interval` seconds so a 150-event run
10	costs a few dozen round trips instead of 150 — and the sink re-sends its COMPLETE in-memory list at
11	`run.finished`, which back-fills anything a Store restart dropped. A failed flush is never fatal: the
12	batch stays pending and the final re-send closes the gap.
13	
14	`sink.log(...)` is the other direction: a unified log line that is ALSO mirrored into the stream as
15	a `log` event so the browser timeline can show harness-side detail (bounded to 500 chars/value).
16	"""
17	
18	from __future__ import annotations
19	
20	import time
21	from datetime import datetime, timezone
22	from typing import Any, Callable
23	
24	from faultline_common.log import get_logger, truncate
25	from faultline_common.schemas import Event
26	
27	from . import config
28	from .store import RunStore
29	
30	_log = get_logger(config.SVC)
31	
32	TERMINAL_STATUSES = {"ok", "error", "truncated", "unevaluated", "interrupted"}
33	
34	
35	def now_iso() -> str:
36	    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="milliseconds").replace("+00:00", "Z")
37	
38	
39	def _bound(value: Any, cap: int = config.LOG_VALUE_CAP) -> Any:
40	    """Bound one payload value so mirrored log events can never blow up the record."""
41	    if isinstance(value, str):
42	        return truncate(value, cap)
43	    if isinstance(value, dict):
44	        return {k: _bound(v, cap) for k, v in list(value.items())[:50]}
45	    if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
46	        return [_bound(v, cap) for v in list(value)[:50]]
47	    return value
48	
49	
50	class EventSink:
51	    """Owns the RunRecord for the lifetime of a run; appends events to the Store as they happen."""
52	
53	    def __init__(self, store: RunStore, record: dict[str, Any], *,
54	                 flush_interval: float = 0.0, clock: Callable[[], float] = time.monotonic):
55	        self.store = store
56	        self.record = record
57	        self.run_id: str = record["run_id"]
58	        self.step: int | None = None
59	        self.flush_interval = float(flush_interval)
60	        self._clock = clock
61	        self._pending: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
62	        self._last_flush: float | None = None
63	        self.flush_failures = 0
64	        record.setdefault("events", [])
65	
66	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ core
67	    def emit(self, type: str, data: dict[str, Any] | None = None, step: int | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
68	        ev = Event(
69	            id=len(self.record["events"]),
70	            ts=now_iso(),
71	            run_id=self.run_id,
72	            type=type,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
73	            step=self.step if step is None else step,
74	            data=data or {},
75	        ).model_dump()
76	        self.record["events"].append(ev)
77	        self._pending.append(ev)
78	        self.flush()
79	        if type != "log":
80	            _log.info(
81	                type,
82	                "event",
83	                run_id=self.run_id,
84	                step=ev["step"],
85	                event_id=ev["id"],
86	                keys=sorted((data or {}).keys()) or None,
87	            )
88	        return ev
89	
90	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ persistence
91	    def flush(self, force: bool = False) -> int:
92	        """Send pending events to the Store. Returns how many were sent (0 = coalesced or failed)."""
93	        if not self._pending:
94	            return 0
95	        now = self._clock()
96	        if not force and self._last_flush is not None and (now - self._last_flush) < self.flush_interval:
97	            return 0
98	        batch = list(self._pending)
99	        try:
100	            self.store.append_events(self.run_id, batch)
101	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - a store blip must not kill the episode
102	            self.flush_failures += 1
103	            _log.warn("store.append_failed", f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}", run_id=self.run_id,
104	                      pending=len(batch), failures=self.flush_failures)
105	            return 0
106	        self._pending = self._pending[len(batch):]
107	        self._last_flush = now
108	        return len(batch)
109	
110	    def persist(self) -> None:
111	        """Compatibility alias: force the pending batch out now."""
112	        self.flush(force=True)
113	
114	    def resend_all(self) -> int:
115	        """Re-send the COMPLETE in-memory event list (idempotent) so a Store restart self-heals."""
116	        events = list(self.record.get("events") or [])
117	        try:
118	            result = self.store.append_events(self.run_id, events)
119	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
120	            _log.error("store.resend_failed", f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}", run_id=self.run_id,
121	                       events=len(events))
122	            return 0
123	        self._pending = []
124	        self._last_flush = self._clock()
125	        appended = int((result or {}).get("appended") or 0) if isinstance(result, dict) else 0
126	        _log.info("store.resend", "full event list re-sent", run_id=self.run_id,
127	                  events=len(events), backfilled=appended or None)
128	        return appended
129	
130	    def update(self, **fields: Any) -> None:
131	        """Merge scalar fields into the record and the run row (status, usage, evaluation, …)."""
132	        for k, v in fields.items():
133	            self.record[k] = v
134	        self.flush(force=True)
135	        try:
136	            self.store.update(self.run_id, **fields)
137	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
138	            _log.warn("store.update_failed", f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}", run_id=self.run_id,
139	                      keys=sorted(fields.keys()))
140	
141	    def set_status(self, status: str, **fields: Any) -> None:
142	        self.update(status=status, **fields)
143	        _log.info("run.status", status, run_id=self.run_id, **{k: _bound(v) for k, v in fields.items()})
144	
145	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ unified log mirror
146	    def log(self, lvl: str, ev: str, msg: str = "", **kw: Any) -> None:
147	        """Unified JSON log line + a bounded `log` event in the stream (PLAN.md §2.3/§2.5)."""
148	        bounded = {k: _bound(v) for k, v in kw.items() if v is not None}
149	        # callers may pass their own step/run_id; they win, and must not collide with ours
150	        step = bounded.pop("step", self.step)
151	        run_id = bounded.pop("run_id", self.run_id)
152	        rec = getattr(_log, lvl if lvl in {"debug", "info", "warn", "error"} else "info")(
153	            ev, msg, run_id=run_id, step=step, **bounded
154	        )
155	        if not rec:  # filtered out by LOG_LEVEL
156	            return
157	        self.emit("log", {"svc": config.SVC, "lvl": lvl, "ev": ev, "msg": msg, **bounded})
158	
159	    def debug(self, ev: str, msg: str = "", **kw: Any) -> None:
160	        self.log("debug", ev, msg, **kw)
161	
162	    def info(self, ev: str, msg: str = "", **kw: Any) -> None:
163	        self.log("info", ev, msg, **kw)
164	
165	    def warn(self, ev: str, msg: str = "", **kw: Any) -> None:
166	        self.log("warn", ev, msg, **kw)
167	
168	    def error(self, ev: str, msg: str = "", **kw: Any) -> None:
169	        self.log("error", ev, msg, **kw)
170

1	"""Run/conversation persistence — a thin client over the single-writer SQLite `Store`.
2	
3	`RunStore` keeps the small verb set `api.py`, `events.py` and `loop.py` already use
4	(`get / create / append_events / update / list_runs`) so the migration off the Modal Dict
5	`faultline-runs` was a backend swap, not a rewrite (PLAN.md §2.9, ARCHITECTURE.md §4.3). It also
6	passes the identity/conversation verbs straight through.
7	
8	Two backends, same surface:
9	
10	  * `SqliteStore`         — in-process. Used by every test (temp dir or `:memory:`) and by a local
11	                            `uvicorn harness.api:app` run.
12	  * `ModalStoreClient`    — `Store().<method>.remote(...)` on the `@app.cls(max_containers=1)`
13	                            defined in `modal_app.py`. Used in the deployed harness, where the
14	                            database must live in exactly one process.
15	
16	Nothing else may open the database file: single writer is what makes SQLite safe here.
17	"""
18	
19	from __future__ import annotations
20	
21	import threading
22	from typing import Any, Callable
23	
24	from faultline_common.log import get_logger
25	
26	from . import config
27	from .sqlite_store import (  # re-exported: callers import these from harness.store
28	    ANON_USER_ID,
29	    TERMINAL_STATUSES,
30	    SqliteStore,
31	    new_id,
32	    now_iso,
33	    valid_user_id,
34	)
35	
36	log = get_logger(config.SVC)
37	
38	__all__ = [
39	    "ANON_USER_ID",
40	    "TERMINAL_STATUSES",
41	    "ModalStoreClient",
42	    "RunStore",
43	    "SqliteStore",
44	    "memory_store",
45	    "new_id",
46	    "now_iso",
47	    "valid_user_id",
48	]
49	
50	# Every verb the Store exposes; `ModalStoreClient` forwards each one to the Modal class.
51	STORE_METHODS = (
52	    "upsert_user",
53	    "list_conversations",
54	    "create_conversation",
55	    "get_conversation",
56	    "update_conversation",
57	    "delete_conversation",
58	    "create_run",
59	    "append_events",
60	    "finish_run",
61	    "get_run",
62	    "list_runs",
63	    "events_after",
64	    "checkpoint",
65	    "health",
66	)

68	
69	def memory_store() -> SqliteStore:
70	    """A fresh private database (tests, and any caller that wants a throwaway store)."""
71	    return SqliteStore(":memory:").open()
72	
73	
74	class ModalStoreClient:
75	    """Forwards every Store verb to the single-container Modal class.
76	
77	    `cls_handle` is the decorated class from `modal_app` when we are running inside the app (no
78	    lookup, no dependency on what is currently deployed); otherwise the class is resolved by name.
79	    """
80	
81	    def __init__(self, cls_handle: Any = None, *, app_name: str = config.APP_NAME,
82	                 class_name: str = "Store"):
83	        self._cls_handle = cls_handle
84	        self._app_name = app_name
85	        self._class_name = class_name
86	        self._instance: Any = None
87	        self._lock = threading.Lock()
88	
89	    def _obj(self) -> Any:
90	        with self._lock:
91	            if self._instance is None:
92	                handle = self._cls_handle
93	                if handle is None:
94	                    import modal
95	
96	                    handle = modal.Cls.from_name(
97	                        self._app_name, self._class_name,
98	                        environment_name=config.modal_environment(),
99	                    )
100	                self._instance = handle()
101	            return self._instance
102	
103	    def __getattr__(self, name: str) -> Callable[..., Any]:
104	        if name not in STORE_METHODS:
105	            raise AttributeError(name)
106	
107	        def call(*args: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> Any:
108	            return getattr(self._obj(), name).remote(*args, **kwargs)
109	
110	        return call
111	
112	
113	class RunStore:
114	    """The verbs the rest of the harness calls. Backend-agnostic; never opens a file itself."""
115	
116	    def __init__(self, backend: Any = None):
117	        self._backend = backend
118	
119	    @property
120	    def backend(self) -> Any:
121	        if self._backend is None:
122	            self._backend = ModalStoreClient()
123	        return self._backend
124	
125	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ runs
126	    def get(self, run_id: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
127	        record = self.backend.get_run(run_id)
128	        return record if isinstance(record, dict) else None
129	
130	    def create(self, record: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
131	        return self.backend.create_run(dict(record))
132	
133	    def append_events(self, run_id: str, events: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> dict[str, Any]:
134	        """Idempotent on (run_id, seq); returns {run_id, appended, count} — NOT the whole record."""
135	        return self.backend.append_events(run_id, list(events or []))
136	
137	    def update(self, run_id: str, **fields: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
138	        """Merge scalar fields (status, finished_at, evaluation, usage, …) into the run row."""
139	        return self.backend.finish_run(run_id, dict(fields))
140	
141	    def finish_run(self, run_id: str, **fields: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
142	        return self.update(run_id, **fields)
143	
144	    def list_runs(self, limit: int = 50, user_id: str | None = None) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
145	        return list(self.backend.list_runs(limit, user_id) or [])
146	
147	    def events_after(self, run_id: str, after: int = -1, limit: int = 2000) -> dict[str, Any]:
148	        return self.backend.events_after(run_id, after, limit)
149	
150	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ identity + conversations
151	    def upsert_user(self, user_id: str, user_agent: str | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
152	        return self.backend.upsert_user(user_id, user_agent)
153	
154	    def list_conversations(self, user_id: str, limit: int = 50) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
155	        return list(self.backend.list_conversations(user_id, limit) or [])
156	
157	    def create_conversation(self, user_id: str, scenario_id: str, title: str | None = None,
158	                            user_agent: str | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
159	        return self.backend.create_conversation(user_id, scenario_id, title, user_agent)
160	
161	    def get_conversation(self, conversation_id: str, user_id: str | None = None) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
162	        return self.backend.get_conversation(conversation_id, user_id)
163	
164	    def update_conversation(self, conversation_id: str, user_id: str | None = None, *,
165	                            title: str | None = None, archived: bool | None = None) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
166	        return self.backend.update_conversation(conversation_id, user_id, title=title, archived=archived)
167	
168	    def delete_conversation(self, conversation_id: str, user_id: str | None = None) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
169	        return self.backend.delete_conversation(conversation_id, user_id)
170	
171	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ ops
172	    def checkpoint(self, force: bool = False) -> dict[str, Any]:
173	        return self.backend.checkpoint(force)
174	
175	    def health(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
176	        return self.backend.health()
