Tool reply2026-09-13T00:37:57.487Z
1	"""SQLite engine behind the harness `Store` (PLAN.md §2.9, ARCHITECTURE.md §4).
2	
3	This module is plain Python: no Modal, no network, no FastAPI. `modal_app.Store` is a thin
4	`@app.cls(max_containers=1)` wrapper that owns one instance of `SqliteStore` per container and
5	exposes each method as a `@modal.method`, so everything here is unit-testable on a temp dir.
6	
7	Shape (ARCHITECTURE §4.3):
8	  * the hot database lives on the container's own disk (`/tmp/faultline.sqlite3`) with ordinary
9	    SQLite settings — WAL, synchronous=NORMAL, foreign_keys=ON — so none of the FUSE questions a
10	    Modal Volume raises (locking, mmap, a background commit capturing a half-written file) apply;
11	  * a checkpoint is `VACUUM INTO <snapshot>.tmp` → `os.replace` → `volume.commit()`. Only complete
12	    files are ever renamed into place, so a snapshot is always openable;
13	  * `@modal.enter` restores the snapshot into /tmp and runs migrations (`PRAGMA user_version` gates
14	    `migrations/000N_*.sql`);
15	  * every write marks the DB dirty; `checkpoint()` is called after each `run.finished`, every
16	    `checkpoint_interval_s` while dirty (background thread), and from `@modal.exit`.
17	
18	`events` is the source of truth; `messages` / `blocks` / `llm_calls` are a projection written in the
19	SAME transaction as the event insert (§4.5), and `append_events` is idempotent on `(run_id, seq)` —
20	`run_episode` re-sends its whole in-memory event list at `run.finished`, which back-fills anything a
21	Store restart lost, and the projection is skipped for rows that were already there.
22	"""
23	
24	from __future__ import annotations
25	
26	import contextlib
27	import json
28	import os
29	import pathlib
30	import re
31	import secrets
32	import shutil
33	import sqlite3
34	import threading
35	import time
36	from datetime import datetime, timezone
37	from typing import Any, Callable, Iterator
38	
39	from faultline_common.log import get_logger
40	
41	from . import config
42	
43	log = get_logger(config.SVC)
44	
45	# `Image.add_local_python_source` ships only the package's .py files — verified on Modal 1.5.5: the
46	# container had harness/*.py and NO harness/migrations/. So modal_app.py copies the directory in
47	# explicitly (outside the package, where the python-source mount cannot shadow it) and points here.
48	MIGRATIONS_DIR = pathlib.Path(
49	    os.environ.get("FAULTLINE_MIGRATIONS_DIR") or (pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "migrations")
50	)
51	
52	# Identity is scoping, not authentication (PLAN.md §1 non-goal / §2.9.1): `u_` + a uuid4.
53	USER_ID_RE = re.compile(r"^u_[0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}$")
54	# Requests that carry no X-Faultline-User (the CLI, smoke scripts) are still persisted, under one
55	# well-known anonymous id, so a run always has a conversation and the FKs hold.
56	ANON_USER_ID = "u_00000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000000"
57	
58	TERMINAL_STATUSES = {"ok", "error", "truncated", "unevaluated", "interrupted"}
59	
60	# Columns of `runs` that finish_run()/create_run() may set directly.
61	RUN_COLUMNS = {
62	    "conversation_id", "user_id", "scenario_id", "model", "seed", "max_steps", "task_prompt",
63	    "episode_id", "status", "score", "error", "created_at", "started_at", "finished_at",
64	    "steps", "summary", "input_tokens", "output_tokens",
65	}
66	# Anything else a caller passes (usage detail, error_class, interruptions, worker_generation …)
67	# is preserved verbatim in `extra_json` and merged back into the record on read, so a RunRecord
68	# never loses a field this store does not know about.
69	_RESERVED = RUN_COLUMNS | {"run_id", "id", "evaluation", "events", "usage"}
70	
71	
72	def now_iso() -> str:
73	    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="milliseconds").replace("+00:00", "Z")
74	
75	
76	def new_id(prefix: str) -> str:
77	    """`<prefix>_<hex ms timestamp><12 hex random>` — sortable, no extra dependency (§4.3)."""
78	    return f"{prefix}_{int(time.time() * 1000):x}{secrets.token_hex(6)}"
79	
80	
81	def valid_user_id(value: str | None) -> bool:
82	    return bool(value) and bool(USER_ID_RE.match(value or ""))
83	
84	
85	def _json(value: Any) -> str | None:
86	    return None if value is None else json.dumps(value, default=str)
87	
88	
89	def _unjson(value: Any) -> Any:
90	    if not value:
91	        return None
92	    try:
93	        return json.loads(value)
94	    except (TypeError, ValueError):  # pragma: no cover - only a corrupted row gets here
95	        return None
96	
97	
98	class SqliteStore:
99	    """The single writer. One connection, one lock; every op is sub-millisecond on local disk."""
100	
101	    def __init__(
102	        self,
103	        db_path: str | os.PathLike[str],
104	        *,
105	        snapshot_path: str | os.PathLike[str] | None = None,
106	        commit: Callable[[], None] | None = None,
107	        reload: Callable[[], None] | None = None,
108	        checkpoint_interval_s: float = 15.0,
109	    ):
110	        self.db_path = str(db_path)
111	        self.snapshot_path = str(snapshot_path) if snapshot_path else None
112	        self._commit_volume = commit
113	        self._reload_volume = reload
114	        self.checkpoint_interval_s = checkpoint_interval_s
115	        self._lock = threading.RLock()
116	        self._ckpt_lock = threading.Lock()
117	        self._conn: sqlite3.Connection | None = None
118	        self._dirty = False
119	        self._last_checkpoint_at: str | None = None
120	        self._last_checkpoint_error: str | None = None
121	        self._restored_from_snapshot = False
122	        self._stop = threading.Event()
123	        self._thread: threading.Thread | None = None
124	
125	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ lifecycle
126	    def open(self) -> "SqliteStore":
127	        with self._lock:
128	            if self._conn is not None:
129	                return self
130	            if self.db_path not in (":memory:", ""):
131	                pathlib.Path(self.db_path).parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
132	            self._restore()
133	            self._conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path, check_same_thread=False, isolation_level=None)
134	            self._conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
135	            if self.db_path not in (":memory:", ""):
136	                self._conn.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL")
137	            self._conn.execute("PRAGMA synchronous=NORMAL")
138	            self._conn.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON")
139	            self._conn.execute("PRAGMA busy_timeout=5000")
140	            applied = self._migrate()
141	            self._assert_schema()
142	            log.info(
143	                "store.open",
144	                "sqlite ready",
145	                db=self.db_path,
146	                snapshot=self.snapshot_path,
147	                restored=self._restored_from_snapshot,
148	                user_version=self.user_version(),
149	                migrations_applied=applied or None,
150	            )
151	        return self
152	
153	    def _restore(self) -> None:
154	        """Copy the Volume snapshot onto local disk, unless a hot DB is already there."""
155	        if not self.snapshot_path or self.db_path in (":memory:", ""):
156	            return
157	        if os.path.exists(self.db_path):
158	            return
159	        if self._reload_volume is not None:
160	            try:
161	                self._reload_volume()
162	            except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - a fresh Volume has nothing to reload
163	                log.warn("store.reload_failed", f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}")
164	        if not os.path.exists(self.snapshot_path):
165	            log.info("store.restore", "no snapshot on the volume; starting empty",
166	                     snapshot=self.snapshot_path)
167	            return
168	        pathlib.Path(self.db_path).parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
169	        shutil.copyfile(self.snapshot_path, self.db_path)
170	        self._restored_from_snapshot = True
171	        log.info("store.restore", "snapshot copied to local disk", snapshot=self.snapshot_path,
172	                 db=self.db_path, bytes=os.path.getsize(self.db_path))
173	
174	    def _migrate(self) -> list[str]:
175	        version = self.user_version()
176	        applied: list[str] = []
177	        for path in sorted(MIGRATIONS_DIR.glob("[0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9]_*.sql")):
178	            n = int(path.name.split("_", 1)[0])
179	            if n <= version:
180	                continue
181	            assert self._conn is not None
182	            self._conn.executescript(path.read_text())
183	            self._conn.execute(f"PRAGMA user_version = {n}")
184	            applied.append(path.name)
185	        return applied
186	
187	    def _assert_schema(self) -> None:
188	        """Fail at @modal.enter, not on the first request.
189	
190	        A missing migrations directory used to leave an EMPTY database that answered every call with
191	        `no such table` 500s; crashing here makes that a container-start failure instead.
192	        """
193	        assert self._conn is not None
194	        tables = {r[0] for r in self._conn.execute("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'")}
195	        missing = {"users", "conversations", "runs", "events", "messages", "blocks", "llm_calls"} - tables
196	        if missing:
197	            raise RuntimeError(
198	                f"store schema is incomplete: missing {sorted(missing)}; "
199	                f"migrations dir {MIGRATIONS_DIR} contains "
200	                f"{sorted(p.name for p in MIGRATIONS_DIR.glob('*')) if MIGRATIONS_DIR.is_dir() else 'NOTHING (dir absent)'}"
201	            )
202	
203	    def user_version(self) -> int:
204	        assert self._conn is not None
205	        return int(self._conn.execute("PRAGMA user_version").fetchone()[0])
206	
207	    def close(self) -> None:
208	        self.stop_autocheckpoint()
209	        with self._lock:
210	            if self._conn is not None:
211	                self._conn.close()
212	                self._conn = None
213	
214	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ plumbing
215	    @property
216	    def conn(self) -> sqlite3.Connection:
217	        if self._conn is None:
218	            self.open()
219	        assert self._conn is not None
220	        return self._conn
221	
222	    @contextlib.contextmanager
223	    def _tx(self) -> Iterator[sqlite3.Cursor]:
224	        with self._lock:
225	            conn = self.conn
226	            cur = conn.cursor()
227	            cur.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
228	            try:
229	                yield cur
230	                conn.commit()
231	                self._dirty = True
232	            except BaseException:
233	                conn.rollback()
234	                raise
235	            finally:
236	                cur.close()
237	
238	    def _query(self, sql: str, params: tuple[Any, ...] = ()) -> list[sqlite3.Row]:
239	        with self._lock:
240	            return list(self.conn.execute(sql, params).fetchall())
241	
242	    def _one(self, sql: str, params: tuple[Any, ...] = ()) -> sqlite3.Row | None:
243	        rows = self._query(sql, params)
244	        return rows[0] if rows else None
245	
246	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ users
247	    def upsert_user(self, user_id: str, user_agent: str | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
248	        ts = now_iso()
249	        with self._tx() as cur:
250	            cur.execute(
251	                "INSERT INTO users (id, created_at, last_seen_at, user_agent) VALUES (?,?,?,?) "
252	                "ON CONFLICT(id) DO UPDATE SET last_seen_at=excluded.last_seen_at, "
253	                "user_agent=COALESCE(excluded.user_agent, users.user_agent)",
254	                (user_id, ts, ts, user_agent),
255	            )
256	        row = self._one("SELECT * FROM users WHERE id=?", (user_id,))
257	        n = self._one("SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM conversations WHERE user_id=? AND archived_at IS NULL",
258	                      (user_id,))
259	        assert row is not None
260	        return {**dict(row), "conversations": int(n["n"] if n else 0)}
261	
262	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ conversations
263	    def create_conversation(self, user_id: str, scenario_id: str, title: str | None = None,
264	                            user_agent: str | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
265	        self.upsert_user(user_id, user_agent)
266	        cid = new_id("c")
267	        ts = now_iso()
268	        with self._tx() as cur:
269	            cur.execute(
270	                "INSERT INTO conversations (id, user_id, scenario_id, title, created_at, updated_at) "
271	                "VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?)",
272	                (cid, user_id, scenario_id, (title or scenario_id).strip()[:200] or scenario_id, ts, ts),
273	            )
274	        log.info("conversation.created", "conversation created", conversation_id=cid,
275	                 user_id=user_id, scenario_id=scenario_id)
276	        return self._conversation(cid) or {}
277	
278	    def _conversation(self, conversation_id: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
279	        row = self._one("SELECT * FROM conversations WHERE id=?", (conversation_id,))
280	        return dict(row) if row else None
281	
282	    def _last_run(self, conversation_id: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
283	        row = self._one(
284	            "SELECT * FROM runs WHERE conversation_id=? ORDER BY created_at DESC, rowid DESC LIMIT 1",
285	            (conversation_id,),
286	        )
287	        return self._run_summary(row) if row else None
288	
289	    @staticmethod
290	    def _run_summary(row: sqlite3.Row, n_events: int | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
291	        """RunSummary (schemas.py keys on `id`; ARCHITECTURE §5.1 and the CLI key on `run_id`)."""
292	        out = {
293	            "id": row["id"],
294	            "run_id": row["id"],
295	            "status": row["status"],
296	            "scenario_id": row["scenario_id"],
297	            "model": row["model"],
298	            "score": row["score"],
299	            "created_at": row["created_at"],
300	            "finished_at": row["finished_at"],
301	        }
302	        if n_events is not None:
303	            out["events"] = n_events
304	        return out
305	
306	    def list_conversations(self, user_id: str, limit: int = 50) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
307	        rows = self._query(
308	            "SELECT * FROM conversations WHERE user_id=? AND archived_at IS NULL "
309	            "ORDER BY updated_at DESC, rowid DESC LIMIT ?",
310	            (user_id, int(limit)),
311	        )
312	        return [
313	            {
314	                "id": r["id"],
315	                "title": r["title"],
316	                "scenario_id": r["scenario_id"],
317	                "created_at": r["created_at"],
318	                "updated_at": r["updated_at"],
319	                "last_run": self._last_run(r["id"]),
320	            }
321	            for r in rows
322	        ]
323	
324	    def get_conversation(self, conversation_id: str, user_id: str | None = None) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
325	        conv = self._conversation(conversation_id)
326	        if conv is None or (user_id is not None and conv["user_id"] != user_id):
327	            return None
328	        runs = [
329	            self._run_summary(r, n_events=int(r["n_events"]))
330	            for r in self._query(
331	                "SELECT r.*, (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM events e WHERE e.run_id=r.id) AS n_events "
332	                "FROM runs r WHERE r.conversation_id=? ORDER BY r.created_at, r.rowid",
333	                (conversation_id,),
334	            )
335	        ]
336	        return {"conversation": conv, "runs": runs, "messages": self._messages(conversation_id)}
337	
338	    def _messages(self, conversation_id: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
339	        messages = [dict(r) for r in self._query(
340	            "SELECT * FROM messages WHERE conversation_id=? ORDER BY seq", (conversation_id,)
341	        )]
342	        by_id = {m["id"]: m for m in messages}
343	        for m in messages:
344	            m["blocks"] = []
345	        if not messages:
346	            return messages
347	        placeholders = ",".join("?" for _ in messages)
348	        rows = self._query(
349	            f"SELECT * FROM blocks WHERE message_id IN ({placeholders}) ORDER BY message_id, seq",
350	            tuple(m["id"] for m in messages),
351	        )
352	        for b in rows:
353	            by_id[b["message_id"]]["blocks"].append(
354	                {
355	                    "id": b["id"],
356	                    "message_id": b["message_id"],
357	                    "seq": b["seq"],
358	                    "type": b["type"],
359	                    "text": b["text"],
360	                    "tool_name": b["tool_name"],
361	                    "tool_use_id": b["tool_use_id"],
362	                    "input": _unjson(b["input_json"]),
363	                    "is_error": None if b["is_error"] is None else bool(b["is_error"]),
364	                    "exit_code": b["exit_code"],
365	                    "duration_ms": b["duration_ms"],
366	                    "fault": _unjson(b["fault_json"]),
367	                    "truncated": bool(b["truncated"]),
368	                }
369	            )
370	        return messages
371	
372	    def update_conversation(self, conversation_id: str, user_id: str | None = None, *,
373	                            title: str | None = None, archived: bool | None = None) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
374	        conv = self._conversation(conversation_id)
375	        if conv is None or (user_id is not None and conv["user_id"] != user_id):
376	            return None
377	        sets: list[str] = []
378	        params: list[Any] = []
379	        if title is not None:
380	            sets.append("title=?")
381	            params.append(title.strip()[:200] or conv["title"])
382	        if archived is not None:
383	            sets.append("archived_at=?")
384	            params.append(now_iso() if archived else None)
385	        if sets:
386	            sets.append("updated_at=?")
387	            params.append(now_iso())
388	            params.append(conversation_id)
389	            with self._tx() as cur:
390	                cur.execute(f"UPDATE conversations SET {', '.join(sets)} WHERE id=?", tuple(params))
391	        return self._conversation(conversation_id)
392	
393	    def delete_conversation(self, conversation_id: str, user_id: str | None = None) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
394	        """Soft archive — nothing is ever hard-deleted (ARCHITECTURE §4.3 'Ops')."""
395	        conv = self.update_conversation(conversation_id, user_id, archived=True)
396	        return None if conv is None else {"archived": True, "id": conv["id"]}
397	
398	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ runs
399	    def create_run(self, record: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
400	        rec = dict(record or {})
401	        run_id = rec.get("run_id") or rec.get("id") or new_id("r")
402	        existing = self.get_run(run_id)
403	        if existing is not None:
404	            # Idempotent: a re-create (a replayed spawn, a re-import) must not mint a second
405	            # conversation for a run that already has one.
406	            return existing
407	        user_id = rec.get("user_id") or ANON_USER_ID
408	        scenario_id = rec.get("scenario_id") or ""
409	        self.upsert_user(user_id)
410	        conversation_id = rec.get("conversation_id")
411	        if not conversation_id or self._conversation(conversation_id) is None:
412	            conversation_id = self.create_conversation(
413	                user_id, scenario_id, rec.get("title") or scenario_id
414	            )["id"]
415	        extra = {k: v for k, v in rec.items() if k not in _RESERVED}
416	        usage = rec.get("usage") or {}
417	        with self._tx() as cur:
418	            cur.execute(
419	                "INSERT OR IGNORE INTO runs (id, conversation_id, user_id, scenario_id, model, seed, "
420	                "max_steps, task_prompt, episode_id, status, score, evaluation_json, input_tokens, "
421	                "output_tokens, error, created_at, started_at, finished_at, steps, summary, extra_json) "
422	                "VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)",
423	                (
424	                    run_id, conversation_id, user_id, scenario_id,
425	                    rec.get("model") or config.DEFAULT_MODEL, rec.get("seed"),
426	                    int(rec.get("max_steps") or config.DEFAULT_MAX_STEPS),
427	                    rec.get("task_prompt") or "", rec.get("episode_id"),
428	                    rec.get("status") or "queued",
429	                    rec.get("score") if rec.get("score") is not None
430	                    else (rec.get("evaluation") or {}).get("score"),
431	                    _json(rec.get("evaluation")),
432	                    int(usage.get("input_tokens") or 0), int(usage.get("output_tokens") or 0),
433	                    rec.get("error"), rec.get("created_at") or now_iso(), rec.get("started_at"),
434	                    rec.get("finished_at"), rec.get("steps"), rec.get("summary"),
435	                    _json(extra) if extra else None,
436	                ),
437	            )
438	        log.info("run.created", "run row stored", run_id=run_id, conversation_id=conversation_id,
439	                 user_id=user_id, status=rec.get("status"))
440	        return self.get_run(run_id) or {}
441	
442	    def finish_run(self, run_id: str, fields: dict[str, Any] | None = None, **kw: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
443	        """Merge scalar fields into a run row (status, finished_at, evaluation, usage, …).
444	
445	        Named `finish_run` because that is what PLAN.md §3.2 calls it, but it is the general
446	        update: `RunStore.update()` routes here so `api.py` and `loop.py` keep one verb.
447	        """
448	        merged: dict[str, Any] = {**(fields or {}), **kw}
449	        row = self._one("SELECT * FROM runs WHERE id=?", (run_id,))
450	        if row is None:
451	            raise KeyError(run_id)
452	        sets: list[str] = []
453	        params: list[Any] = []
454	        # ONE accumulated extra_json for the whole update. Writing `extra_json=?` once per unknown
455	        # key made `UPDATE … SET extra_json=?, extra_json=?` — which SQLite accepts and resolves as
456	        # last-write-wins, silently dropping every earlier key. That was invisible until a single
457	        # update carried several of them (status + error_class + interruptions + worker_generation).
458	        extra: dict[str, Any] = dict(_unjson(row["extra_json"]) or {})
459	        extra_dirty = False
460	        for key, value in merged.items():
461	            if key in ("run_id", "id", "events"):
462	                continue
463	            if key == "evaluation":
464	                sets.append("evaluation_json=?")
465	                params.append(_json(value))
466	                if isinstance(value, dict) and value.get("score") is not None:
467	                    sets.append("score=?")
468	                    params.append(value.get("score"))
469	                continue
470	            if key == "usage" and isinstance(value, dict):
471	                sets += ["input_tokens=?", "output_tokens=?"]
472	                params += [int(value.get("input_tokens") or 0), int(value.get("output_tokens") or 0)]
473	                extra["usage"] = value
474	                extra_dirty = True
475	                continue
476	            if key in RUN_COLUMNS:
477	                sets.append(f"{key}=?")
478	                params.append(value)
479	                continue
480	            extra[key] = value
481	            extra_dirty = True
482	        if extra_dirty:
483	            sets.append("extra_json=?")
484	            params.append(_json(extra))
485	        if sets:
486	            params.append(run_id)
487	            with self._tx() as cur:
488	                cur.execute(f"UPDATE runs SET {', '.join(sets)} WHERE id=?", tuple(params))
489	            self._touch_conversation(run_id)
490	        if merged.get("status") in TERMINAL_STATUSES:
491	            self.checkpoint()
492	        return self.get_run(run_id) or {}
493	
494	    def get_run(self, run_id: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
495	        row = self._one("SELECT * FROM runs WHERE id=?", (run_id,))
496	        if row is None:
497	            return None
498	        extra = _unjson(row["extra_json"]) or {}
499	        usage = extra.pop("usage", None) or {}
500	        record: dict[str, Any] = {
501	            "run_id": row["id"],
502	            "status": row["status"],
503	            "scenario_id": row["scenario_id"],
504	            "model": row["model"],
505	            "seed": row["seed"],
506	            "max_steps": row["max_steps"],
507	            "episode_id": row["episode_id"],
508	            "created_at": row["created_at"],
509	            "started_at": row["started_at"],
510	            "finished_at": row["finished_at"],
511	            "events": [self._event(r) for r in self._query(
512	                "SELECT * FROM events WHERE run_id=? ORDER BY seq", (run_id,))],
513	            "evaluation": _unjson(row["evaluation_json"]),
514	            "usage": {
515	                "input_tokens": int(usage.get("input_tokens") or row["input_tokens"] or 0),
516	                "output_tokens": int(usage.get("output_tokens") or row["output_tokens"] or 0),
517	                **({"cache_read_input_tokens": usage["cache_read_input_tokens"]}
518	                   if usage.get("cache_read_input_tokens") else {}),
519	                **({"cache_creation_input_tokens": usage["cache_creation_input_tokens"]}
520	                   if usage.get("cache_creation_input_tokens") else {}),
521	            },
522	            "error": row["error"],
523	            "conversation_id": row["conversation_id"],
524	            "user_id": row["user_id"],
525	            "task_prompt": row["task_prompt"],
526	            "steps": row["steps"],
527	            "summary": row["summary"],
528	            "score": row["score"],
529	            # PLAN.md §2.11 additions. Defaulted here (not only when a run happens to have set
530	            # them) so every GET /runs/{id} answers the taxonomy's questions — including the 21
531	            # runs imported from before the taxonomy existed.
532	            "error_class": None,
533	            "interruptions": [],
534	            "worker_generation": 1,
535	        }
536	        record.update(extra)
537	        return record
538	
539	    @staticmethod
540	    def _event(row: sqlite3.Row) -> dict[str, Any]:
541	        return {
542	            "id": row["seq"],
543	            "ts": row["ts"],
544	            "run_id": row["run_id"],
545	            "type": row["type"],
546	            "step": row["step"],
547	            "data": _unjson(row["data"]) or {},
548	        }
549	
550	    def list_runs(self, limit: int = 50, user_id: str | None = None) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
551	        sql = ("SELECT r.*, (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM events e WHERE e.run_id=r.id) AS n_events FROM runs r ")
552	        params: list[Any] = []
553	        if user_id:
554	            sql += "WHERE r.user_id=? "
555	            params.append(user_id)
556	        sql += "ORDER BY r.created_at DESC, r.rowid DESC LIMIT ?"
557	        params.append(int(limit))
558	        return [
559	            {**self._run_summary(r, n_events=int(r["n_events"])), "conversation_id": r["conversation_id"]}
560	            for r in self._query(sql, tuple(params))
561	        ]
562	
563	    def events_after(self, run_id: str, after: int = -1, limit: int = 2000) -> dict[str, Any]:
564	        """The SSE tail: everything with `seq > after`, plus enough state to close the stream."""
565	        row = self._one("SELECT status FROM runs WHERE id=?", (run_id,))
566	        if row is None:
567	            return {"run_id": run_id, "exists": False, "status": None, "events": []}
568	        rows = self._query(
569	            "SELECT * FROM events WHERE run_id=? AND seq>? ORDER BY seq LIMIT ?",
570	            (run_id, int(after), int(limit)),
571	        )
572	        return {
573	            "run_id": run_id,
574	            "exists": True,
575	            "status": row["status"],
576	            "events": [self._event(r) for r in rows],
577	        }
578	
579	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ events + projection
580	    def append_events(self, run_id: str, events: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> dict[str, Any]:
581	        """Insert events idempotently on (run_id, seq) and project them into the transcript.
582	
583	        A row that was already there is skipped entirely — insert AND projection — which is what
584	        makes `run_episode`'s full re-send at `run.finished` a no-op for everything already stored.
585	        """
586	        row = self._one("SELECT conversation_id FROM runs WHERE id=?", (run_id,))
587	        if row is None:
588	            raise KeyError(run_id)
589	        conversation_id = row["conversation_id"]
590	        appended = 0
591	        finished = False
592	        with self._tx() as cur:
593	            for event in events or []:
594	                seq = event.get("id")
595	                if seq is None:
596	                    continue
597	                cur.execute(
598	                    "INSERT OR IGNORE INTO events (run_id, seq, ts, type, step, data) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?)",
599	                    (run_id, int(seq), event.get("ts") or now_iso(), event.get("type") or "log",
600	                     event.get("step"), _json(event.get("data") or {}) or "{}"),
601	                )
602	                if cur.rowcount == 0:  # already stored: never project twice
603	                    continue
604	                appended += 1
605	                if event.get("type") == "run.finished":
606	                    finished = True
607	                self._project(cur, conversation_id, run_id, event)
608	            if appended:
609	                cur.execute("UPDATE conversations SET updated_at=? WHERE id=?", (now_iso(), conversation_id))
610	        total = self._one("SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM events WHERE run_id=?", (run_id,))
611	        if finished:
612	            self.checkpoint(force=True)
613	        return {"run_id": run_id, "appended": appended, "count": int(total["n"]) if total else 0}
614	
615	    # --- projection helpers (ARCHITECTURE §4.5) -------------------------------------------------
616	    def _message(self, cur: sqlite3.Cursor, conversation_id: str, run_id: str, role: str,
617	                 step: int | None, ts: str) -> str:
618	        if step is None:
619	            found = cur.execute(
620	                "SELECT id FROM messages WHERE run_id=? AND role=? AND step IS NULL LIMIT 1",
621	                (run_id, role),
622	            ).fetchone()
623	        else:
624	            found = cur.execute(
625	                "SELECT id FROM messages WHERE run_id=? AND role=? AND step=? LIMIT 1",
626	                (run_id, role, int(step)),
627	            ).fetchone()
628	        if found:
629	            return found["id"]
630	        seq_row = cur.execute(
631	            "SELECT COALESCE(MAX(seq), -1) + 1 AS n FROM messages WHERE conversation_id=?",
632	            (conversation_id,),
633	        ).fetchone()
634	        mid = new_id("m")
635	        cur.execute(
636	            "INSERT INTO messages (id, conversation_id, run_id, seq, role, step, created_at) "
637	            "VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?)",
638	            (mid, conversation_id, run_id, int(seq_row["n"]), role, step, ts),
639	        )
640	        return mid
641	
642	    @staticmethod
643	    def _block(cur: sqlite3.Cursor, message_id: str, btype: str, **cols: Any) -> None:
644	        seq_row = cur.execute(
645	            "SELECT COALESCE(MAX(seq), -1) + 1 AS n FROM blocks WHERE message_id=?", (message_id,)
646	        ).fetchone()
647	        cur.execute(
648	            "INSERT INTO blocks (id, message_id, seq, type, text, tool_name, tool_use_id, input_json, "
649	            "is_error, exit_code, duration_ms, fault_json, truncated) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)",
650	            (
651	                new_id("b"), message_id, int(seq_row["n"]), btype, cols.get("text"),
652	                cols.get("tool_name"), cols.get("tool_use_id"), _json(cols.get("input")),
653	                None if cols.get("is_error") is None else int(bool(cols.get("is_error"))),
654	                cols.get("exit_code"), cols.get("duration_ms"), _json(cols.get("fault")),
655	                int(bool(cols.get("truncated"))),
656	            ),
657	        )
658	
659	    def _project(self, cur: sqlite3.Cursor, conversation_id: str, run_id: str,
660	                 event: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
661	        etype = event.get("type") or ""
662	        data = event.get("data") or {}
663	        step = event.get("step")
664	        ts = event.get("ts") or now_iso()
665	
666	        if etype == "run.started":
667	            cur.execute("UPDATE runs SET status='running', started_at=COALESCE(started_at, ?) WHERE id=?",
668	                        (ts, run_id))
669	        elif etype == "episode.reset":
670	            task_prompt = str(data.get("task_prompt") or "")
671	            cur.execute("UPDATE runs SET episode_id=?, task_prompt=? WHERE id=?",
672	                        (data.get("episode_id"), task_prompt, run_id))
673	            mid = self._message(cur, conversation_id, run_id, "user", None, ts)
674	            self._block(cur, mid, "text", text=task_prompt)
675	        elif etype in ("turn.text", "turn.thinking"):
676	            mid = self._message(cur, conversation_id, run_id, "assistant", step, ts)
677	            self._block(cur, mid, "thinking" if etype == "turn.thinking" else "text",
678	                        text=str(data.get("text") or ""))
679	        elif etype == "tool.call":
680	            mid = self._message(cur, conversation_id, run_id, "assistant", step, ts)
681	            self._block(cur, mid, "tool_use", tool_name=data.get("tool"),
682	                        tool_use_id=data.get("tool_use_id"), input=data.get("input") or {})
683	        elif etype == "tool.result":
684	            # Anthropic requires tool results in a USER turn; one per step, exactly like the
685	            # messages=[…] array the model saw.
686	            mid = self._message(cur, conversation_id, run_id, "user", step, ts)
687	            output = str(data.get("output") or "")
688	            self._block(
689	                cur, mid, "tool_result",
690	                text=output,
691	                tool_name=data.get("tool"),
692	                tool_use_id=data.get("tool_use_id"),
693	                is_error=bool(data.get("is_error")),
694	                exit_code=_exit_code_of(output),
695	                duration_ms=data.get("duration_ms"),
696	                fault=data.get("fault"),
697	                truncated="…[truncated" in output,
698	            )
699	        elif etype == "llm.call":
700	            usage = data.get("usage") or {}
701	            cur.execute(
702	                "INSERT OR IGNORE INTO llm_calls (id, run_id, step, attempt, model, request_id, "
703	                "stop_reason, input_tokens, output_tokens, cache_read_tokens, cache_write_tokens, "
704	                "started_at, duration_ms, error) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)",
705	                (
706	                    new_id("l"), run_id, int(step or 0), int(data.get("attempt") or 1),
707	                    str(data.get("model") or ""), data.get("request_id"), data.get("stop_reason"),
708	                    usage.get("input_tokens"), usage.get("output_tokens"),
709	                    usage.get("cache_read_input_tokens"), usage.get("cache_creation_input_tokens"),
710	                    ts, data.get("duration_ms"), data.get("error"),
711	                ),
712	            )
713	            cur.execute(
714	                "UPDATE runs SET input_tokens=input_tokens+?, output_tokens=output_tokens+? WHERE id=?",
715	                (int(usage.get("input_tokens") or 0), int(usage.get("output_tokens") or 0), run_id),
716	            )
717	        elif etype == "episode.evaluated":
718	            cur.execute("UPDATE runs SET score=?, evaluation_json=? WHERE id=?",
719	                        (data.get("score"), _json(data), run_id))
720	        elif etype == "run.finished":
721	            usage = data.get("usage") or {}
722	            # The full usage (both cache counters) goes in with the status flip, not in the separate
723	            # finish_run() that follows: the SSE stream closes the instant the status is terminal, so
724	            # a client that fetches GET /runs/{id} on the `done` frame would otherwise race the
725	            # update and see token counts without the cache columns.
726	            row = cur.execute("SELECT extra_json FROM runs WHERE id=?", (run_id,)).fetchone()
727	            extra = dict((_unjson(row["extra_json"]) if row is not None else None) or {})
728	            if usage:
729	                extra["usage"] = usage
730	            cur.execute(
731	                "UPDATE runs SET status=?, finished_at=?, error=?, steps=COALESCE(?, steps), "
732	                "input_tokens=?, output_tokens=?, extra_json=? WHERE id=?",
733	                (
734	                    data.get("status") or "ok", ts, data.get("error"), data.get("steps"),
735	                    int(usage.get("input_tokens") or 0), int(usage.get("output_tokens") or 0),
736	                    _json(extra) if extra else None, run_id,
737	                ),
738	            )
739	        # fault.fired / workspace.diff / log / interruption / run.resumed / episode.sandbox:
740	        # event row only — the UI reads those straight from the event log (§4.5).
741	
742	    def _touch_conversation(self, run_id: str) -> None:
743	        with self._tx() as cur:
744	            cur.execute(
745	                "UPDATE conversations SET updated_at=? WHERE id=(SELECT conversation_id FROM runs WHERE id=?)",
746	                (now_iso(), run_id),
747	            )
748	
749	    def llm_calls(self, run_id: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
750	        return [dict(r) for r in self._query(
751	            "SELECT * FROM llm_calls WHERE run_id=? ORDER BY step, attempt", (run_id,))]
752	
753	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ checkpoint
754	    def checkpoint(self, force: bool = False) -> dict[str, Any]:
755	        """VACUUM INTO a temp file on the Volume, rename it into place, commit (ARCHITECTURE §4.3)."""
756	        if not self.snapshot_path:
757	            self._dirty = False
758	            return self.health()
759	        # Only the VACUUM touches the database, so the connection lock is held for that alone —
760	        # writing ~1 MB across the FUSE mount and committing the Volume takes seconds, and an SSE
761	        # poll must not queue behind it. `_ckpt_lock` keeps two checkpoints off the same temp file.
762	        with self._ckpt_lock:
763	            tmp = f"{self.snapshot_path}.tmp"
764	            t0 = time.perf_counter()
765	            try:
766	                with self._lock:
767	                    if not self._dirty and not force:
768	                        return self.health()
769	                    pathlib.Path(self.snapshot_path).parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
770	                    if os.path.exists(tmp):
771	                        os.remove(tmp)
772	                    self._vacuum_into(tmp)
773	                    # Cleared inside the lock: no write can slip between the VACUUM and this line,
774	                    # so "not dirty" always means "everything committed is in the snapshot".
775	                    self._dirty = False
776	                vacuum_ms = int((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000)
777	                os.replace(tmp, self.snapshot_path)
778	                if self._commit_volume is not None:
779	                    self._commit_volume()
780	                self._last_checkpoint_at = now_iso()
781	                self._last_checkpoint_error = None
782	                log.info("store.checkpoint", "snapshot committed", snapshot=self.snapshot_path,
783	                         bytes=os.path.getsize(self.snapshot_path), vacuum_ms=vacuum_ms,
784	                         dur_ms=int((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000))
785	            except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - a failed checkpoint must not fail the run
786	                self._dirty = True  # unsaved writes remain unsaved; try again on the next tick
787	                self._last_checkpoint_error = f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"[:300]
788	                log.error("store.checkpoint_failed", self._last_checkpoint_error)
789	                with contextlib.suppress(OSError):
790	                    if os.path.exists(tmp):
791	                        os.remove(tmp)
792	        return self.health()
793	
794	    def _vacuum_into(self, target: str) -> None:
795	        try:
796	            self.conn.execute("VACUUM INTO ?", (target,))
797	        except sqlite3.Error:
798	            # Older SQLite builds reject a bound parameter as the VACUUM target.
799	            self.conn.execute("VACUUM INTO '{}'".format(target.replace("'", "''")))
800	
801	    def start_autocheckpoint(self) -> None:
802	        """Checkpoint every `checkpoint_interval_s` while there are unsaved writes."""
803	        if self._thread is not None or not self.snapshot_path:
804	            return
805	        self._stop.clear()
806	
807	        def _loop() -> None:
808	            while not self._stop.wait(self.checkpoint_interval_s):
809	                if self._dirty:
810	                    self.checkpoint()
811	
812	        self._thread = threading.Thread(target=_loop, name="store-checkpoint", daemon=True)
813	        self._thread.start()
814	
815	    def stop_autocheckpoint(self) -> None:
816	        self._stop.set()
817	        thread, self._thread = self._thread, None
818	        if thread is not None:
819	            thread.join(timeout=2.0)
820	
821	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ health
822	    def health(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
823	        try:
824	            runs = self._one("SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM runs")
825	            events = self._one("SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM events")
826	            convos = self._one("SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM conversations")
827	            ok = True
828	        except sqlite3.Error as exc:  # pragma: no cover - only a broken DB gets here
829	            log.error("store.health_failed", f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}")
830	            return {"ok": False, "error": f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"[:200],
831	                    "last_checkpoint_at": self._last_checkpoint_at}
832	        return {
833	            "ok": ok,
834	            "last_checkpoint_at": self._last_checkpoint_at,
835	            "last_checkpoint_error": self._last_checkpoint_error,
836	            "dirty": self._dirty,
837	            "db_path": self.db_path,
838	            "snapshot_path": self.snapshot_path,
839	            "restored_from_snapshot": self._restored_from_snapshot,
840	            "user_version": self.user_version(),
841	            "runs": int(runs["n"]) if runs else 0,
842	            "events": int(events["n"]) if events else 0,
843	            "conversations": int(convos["n"]) if convos else 0,
844	        }
845	
846	
847	def _exit_code_of(output: str) -> int | None:
848	    """run_command results carry `exit_code` inside the JSON output; blocks store it as a column."""
849	    if not output or not output.lstrip().startswith("{"):
850	        return None
851	    try:
852	        payload = json.loads(output)
853	    except (TypeError, ValueError):
854	        return None
855	    code = payload.get("exit_code") if isinstance(payload, dict) else None
856	    return int(code) if isinstance(code, int) else None

1	"""Browser-facing API for the harness (Modal function `api`, no provider secret).
2	
3	    GET    /health            includes has_provider_key (MUST be false here) + detail.store
4	    GET    /scenarios         proxy to sandbox-env (the browser never talks to the gym directly)
5	    GET    /me                upsert this browser's anonymous user -> {user_id, conversations}
6	    GET    /conversations     this user's conversations, newest first, archived excluded
7	    POST   /conversations     {scenario_id, title?} -> Conversation
8	    GET    /conversations/{id}   conversation + runs + the persisted transcript (messages/blocks)
9	    PATCH  /conversations/{id}   {title?, archived?} -> Conversation
10	    DELETE /conversations/{id}   soft archive -> {archived: true}
11	    POST   /conversations/{id}/runs  {model?, seed?, max_steps?, task_prompt?} -> {run_id, conversation_id}
12	    POST   /runs              validate + create a conversation + a queued run, spawn `run_episode`
13	    GET    /runs              last 50 runs (scoped to the caller when X-Faultline-User is sent)
14	    GET    /runs/{id}         the whole RunRecord (what we also save as evidence)
15	    GET    /runs/{id}/events  SSE tail of the event stream, resumable via Last-Event-ID
16	
17	Identity (PLAN.md §2.9.1) is scoping, not authentication: `X-Faultline-User: u_<uuid4>` is validated,
18	upserted and used to scope conversations and runs; a mismatch is 404, never 403 (no existence
19	oracle). Requests with no header still work — they are attributed to one well-known anonymous user
20	so the CLI and the smoke scripts keep running unchanged.
21	
22	Why SSE and not "run it in the request": Modal caps a web request at 150 s and then 303-redirects,
23	which breaks CORS. So the run happens in a spawned function and the browser tails events out of the
24	Store; each stream closes itself at 110 s and the client reconnects with Last-Event-ID.
25	"""
26

33	import uuid

72	def sse_frame(event: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
73	    return f"id: {event['id']}\nevent: {event['type']}\ndata: {json.dumps(event, default=str)}\n\n"

75	
76	def parse_last_event_id(header: str | None, after: int | None, query: str | int | None = None) -> int:
77	    """-1 means "send everything".
78	
79	    Precedence: `Last-Event-ID` header, then `?last_event_id=`, then `?after=`. The browser cannot
80	    set headers on an EventSource, and apps/web re-opens the stream itself, so it sends the resume
81	    point as a query param under BOTH spellings; honour all three (PLAN.md §2.3, docs/web-handoff.md).
82	    """
83	    for candidate in (header, query, after):
84	        if candidate is None or candidate == "":
85	            continue
86	        try:
87	            return int(candidate)
88	        except (TypeError, ValueError):
89	            continue
90	    return -1

93	def user_of(request: Request) -> str | None:
94	    """The validated caller id, or None when the request carried no usable X-Faultline-User."""
95	    return getattr(request.state, "user_id", None)

97	
98	def owns(record: dict[str, Any] | None, user_id: str | None) -> bool:
99	    """Ownership check for runs. Anonymous runs (CLI, smoke scripts) stay readable by anyone."""
100	    if record is None:
101	        return False
102	    owner = record.get("user_id")
103	    if user_id is None or owner in (None, "", ANON_USER_ID):
104	        return True
105	    return owner == user_id
106	
107	
108	def create_app(
109	    *,
110	    store: RunStore | None = None,
111	    gym_factory: Callable[[], Any] | None = None,
112	    spawn: Callable[[str, dict[str, Any]], Any] | None = None,
113	) -> FastAPI:
114	    """Build the ASGI app. Everything external is injectable so tests need no Modal and no network."""
115	    # Secret boundary (PLAN.md §2.1/§2.4): the browser-facing function must not hold the provider
116	    # key. Fail loudly at startup rather than serving traffic from a process that leaked it.
117	    if "ANTHROPIC_API_KEY" in os.environ:
118	        raise RuntimeError(
119	            "secret boundary violated: ANTHROPIC_API_KEY is visible to the harness `api` function; "
120	            "attach the Anthropic secret to `run_episode` only"
121	        )
122	    runs = store or RunStore()
123	    make_gym = gym_factory or (lambda: GymClient())
124	    spawn_fn = spawn or default_spawn
125	
126	    app = FastAPI(title="faultline-harness", version=VERSION)
127	    app.add_middleware(
128	        CORSMiddleware,
129	        allow_origins=["*"],
130	        allow_methods=["*"],
131	        allow_headers=["*"],
132	        expose_headers=["*"],
133	    )

135	    @app.middleware("http")
136	    async def identity_middleware(request: Request, call_next):  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
137	        """X-Faultline-User -> validate -> upsert -> request.state.user_id (PLAN.md §2.9.1)."""
138	        raw = (request.headers.get(USER_HEADER) or "").strip()
139	        valid = valid_user_id(raw)
140	        request.state.user_id = raw if valid else None
141	        request.state.user_id_invalid = bool(raw and not valid)
142	        path = request.url.path
143	        if valid and not path.endswith(NO_UPSERT_SUFFIXES):
144	            try:
145	                await asyncio.to_thread(runs.upsert_user, raw, request.headers.get("user-agent"))
146	            except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - identity is a convenience, never a gate
147	                log.warn("identity.upsert_failed", f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}", user_id=raw)
148	        return await call_next(request)
149	
150	    @app.middleware("http")
151	    async def request_id_middleware(request: Request, call_next):  # type: ignore[no-untyped-def]
152	        rid = request.headers.get("x-request-id") or f"req_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}"
153	        ctx_request_id.set(rid)
154	        ctx_run_id.set(None)
155	        t0 = time.perf_counter()
156	        log.info("http.request", f"{request.method} {request.url.path}", method=request.method,
157	                 path=request.url.path, query=str(request.url.query) or None)
158	        try:
159	            response = await call_next(request)
160	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - log then re-raise as a 500
161	            log.error("http.error", f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}", method=request.method,
162	                      path=request.url.path, dur_ms=int((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000))
163	            raise
164	        response.headers["X-Request-Id"] = rid
165	        log.info("http.response", f"{request.method} {request.url.path}", status=response.status_code,
166	                 path=request.url.path, dur_ms=int((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000))
167	        return response
168	
169	    def need_user(request: Request) -> tuple[str | None, JSONResponse | None]:
170	        uid = user_of(request)
171	        if uid:
172	            return uid, None
173	        detail = ("X-Faultline-User is malformed (want u_<uuid4>)"
174	                  if getattr(request.state, "user_id_invalid", False)
175	                  else "X-Faultline-User header is required for this route")
176	        return None, JSONResponse(status_code=400, content={"error": detail})

178	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ health
179	    @app.get("/health")
180	    async def health() -> dict[str, Any]:
181	        reachable: bool | None = None
182	        detail: dict[str, Any] = {}
183	        gym = make_gym()
184	        try:
185	            info = await asyncio.to_thread(gym.health)
186	            reachable = bool(info)
187	            detail["sandbox_env"] = {k: info.get(k) for k in ("svc", "ok", "version") if k in info}
188	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - unreachable is a normal, reportable state
189	            reachable = False
190	            detail["sandbox_env_error"] = f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"[:300]
191	        finally:
192	            try:
193	                gym.close()
194	            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 # pragma: no cover
195	                pass
196	        detail["sandbox_env_reachable"] = reachable
197	        try:
198	            detail["store"] = await asyncio.to_thread(runs.health)
199	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - the Store being down is reportable, not fatal
200	            detail["store"] = {"ok": False, "last_checkpoint_at": None,
201	                               "error": f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"[:300]}
202	        # The secret is attached to `run_episode` only; if this is ever true, the boundary leaked.
203	        has_key = "ANTHROPIC_API_KEY" in os.environ
204	        if has_key:
205	            log.error("health.secret_boundary", "ANTHROPIC_API_KEY visible to the API function")
206	        return {
207	            "svc": "harness",
208	            "ok": True,
209	            "version": VERSION,
210	            "has_provider_key": has_key,
211	            "model_default": config.model_default(),
212	            "sandbox_env_url": config.sandbox_env_url(),
213	            "detail": detail,
214	        }

241	        """Live scenario list, or [] when the gym is unreachable (a blip must not 502 the Run button)."""

274	    async def create_conversation(request: Request, body: dict[str, Any]) -> Any:
275	        uid, err = need_user(request)
276	        if err is not None:

279	        scenario_id = str(body.get("scenario_id") or "").strip()
280	        if not scenario_id:

283	        known = {s.get("id") for s in catalogue if isinstance(s, dict)}
284	        if known and scenario_id not in known:

286	                "error": f"unknown scenario {scenario_id!r}", "known": sorted(k for k in known if k)})

288	        return await asyncio.to_thread(

332	    async def start_conversation_run(request: Request, conversation_id: str,
333	                                     body: dict[str, Any] | None = None) -> Any:
334	        uid, err = need_user(request)
335	        if err is not None:
336	            return err
337	        detail = await asyncio.to_thread(runs.get_conversation, conversation_id, uid)
338	        if detail is None:
339	            return JSONResponse(status_code=404,
340	                                content={"error": f"unknown conversation {conversation_id!r}"})
341	        conversation = detail.get("conversation") or {}
342	        req = dict(body or {})
343	        out = await _start_run(
344	            scenario_id=str(conversation.get("scenario_id") or ""),
345	            body=req,
346	            user_id=uid,
347	            conversation_id=conversation_id,
348	        )
349	        if isinstance(out, JSONResponse):
350	            return out
351	        return JSONResponse(status_code=202, content=out)

353	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ runs
354	    async def _start_run(*, scenario_id: str, body: dict[str, Any], user_id: str | None,
355	                         conversation_id: str | None) -> Any:
356	        if not scenario_id:
357	            return JSONResponse(status_code=400, content={"error": "scenario_id is required"})
358	        model = body.get("model") or config.model_default()
359	        if model not in config.MODEL_ALLOWLIST:
360	            return JSONResponse(
361	                status_code=400,
362	                content={"error": f"model {model!r} is not allowed", "allowed": config.MODEL_ALLOWLIST},
363	            )
364	        seed = body.get("seed")
365	        max_steps = body.get("max_steps")
366	        task_prompt: str | None = body.get("task_prompt") or None
367	
368	        # Optional, additive (PLAN.md §2.11): let a caller inflict a REAL interruption on this run
369	        # even when the scenario declares none — `worker_crash` kills the worker mid-call,
370	        # `transport_abort` cancels the in-flight request. It is how `transport_abort` is proved
371	        # live (no bundled scenario uses it) and how an operator reproduces a resume on demand.
372	        # Validated against schemas.HarnessFault so a typo is a 400 here, not a dead worker later.
373	        harness_faults: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
374	        if body.get("harness_faults"):
375	            try:
376	                harness_faults = [HarnessFault.model_validate(f).model_dump()
377	                                  for f in body["harness_faults"]]
378	            except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
379	                return JSONResponse(status_code=400,
380	                                    content={"error": "invalid harness_faults",
381	                                             "detail": str(exc)[:400]})
382	
383	        # Validate the scenario against the live catalogue; if the gym is unreachable we let the run
384	        # through and let reset fail loudly inside the episode.
385	        catalogue = await scenario_catalogue()
386	        if catalogue:
387	            known = {s.get("id") for s in catalogue if isinstance(s, dict)}
388	            if scenario_id not in known:
389	                return JSONResponse(
390	                    status_code=400,
391	                    content={"error": f"unknown scenario {scenario_id!r}",
392	                             "known": sorted(k for k in known if k)},
393	                )
394	            match = next((s for s in catalogue if s.get("id") == scenario_id), {})
395	            if not max_steps and isinstance(match.get("max_steps"), int):
396	                max_steps = match["max_steps"]
397	            if not task_prompt and isinstance(match.get("task_prompt"), str):
398	                task_prompt = match["task_prompt"]
399	
400	        max_steps = int(max_steps or config.DEFAULT_MAX_STEPS)
401	        max_steps = max(1, min(max_steps, config.HARD_MAX_STEPS))
402	        owner = user_id or ANON_USER_ID
403	
404	        if not conversation_id:
405	            conversation = await asyncio.to_thread(
406	                runs.create_conversation, owner, scenario_id, _scenario_title(catalogue, scenario_id)
407	            )
408	            conversation_id = str((conversation or {}).get("id") or "")
409	
410	        run_id = new_run_id()
411	        ctx_run_id.set(run_id)
412	        record = {
413	            "run_id": run_id,
414	            "status": "queued",
415	            "scenario_id": scenario_id,
416	            "model": model,
417	            "seed": seed,
418	            "max_steps": max_steps,
419	            "episode_id": None,
420	            "task_prompt": task_prompt,
421	            "created_at": now_iso(),
422	            "finished_at": None,
423	            "events": [],
424	            "evaluation": None,
425	            "usage": {"input_tokens": 0, "output_tokens": 0},
426	            "error": None,
427	            "conversation_id": conversation_id,
428	            "user_id": owner,
429	        }
430	        await asyncio.to_thread(runs.create, record)
431	
432	        req = {"scenario_id": scenario_id, "model": model, "seed": seed, "max_steps": max_steps,
433	               "conversation_id": conversation_id, "user_id": owner}
434	        if task_prompt:
435	            req["task_prompt"] = task_prompt
436	        if harness_faults:
437	            req["harness_faults"] = harness_faults
438	        try:
439	            call_id = await asyncio.to_thread(spawn_fn, run_id, req)
440	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - the record exists, so fail it visibly
441	            log.error("spawn.failed", f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}", run_id=run_id)
442	            error = f"spawn failed: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
443	            await asyncio.to_thread(runs.update, run_id, status="error", error=error,
444	                                    finished_at=now_iso())
445	            return JSONResponse(status_code=500, content={"error": error, "run_id": run_id})
446	
447	        log.info("run.spawned", "run_episode spawned", run_id=run_id, scenario_id=scenario_id,
448	                 model=model, max_steps=max_steps, conversation_id=conversation_id, user_id=owner,
449	                 call_id=call_id if isinstance(call_id, str) else None)
450	        return {"run_id": run_id, "conversation_id": conversation_id, "status": "queued",
451	                "scenario_id": scenario_id, "model": model, "max_steps": max_steps, "seed": seed}
452	
453	    @app.post("/runs")
454	    async def create_run(request: Request, body: dict[str, Any]) -> Any:
455	        out = await _start_run(
456	            scenario_id=str(body.get("scenario_id") or "").strip(),
457	            body=body,
458	            user_id=user_of(request),
459	            conversation_id=None,
460	        )
461	        return out

465	        # Scoped to the caller when the browser sends its id; unscoped for the CLI/evidence scripts.

473	        if not owns(record, user_of(request)):

477	    @app.get("/runs/{run_id}/events")
478	    async def stream_events(
479	        request: Request,
480	        run_id: str,
481	        after: int | None = Query(None),
482	        last_event_id_q: str | None = Query(None, alias="last_event_id"),
483	        last_event_id: str | None = Header(None, alias="Last-Event-ID"),
484	    ) -> Any:
485	        ctx_run_id.set(run_id)
486	        head = await asyncio.to_thread(runs.events_after, run_id, 2**62, 1)
487	        if not (head or {}).get("exists"):
488	            return JSONResponse(status_code=404, content={"error": f"unknown run {run_id!r}"})
489	        uid = user_of(request)
490	        if uid:
491	            record = await asyncio.to_thread(runs.get, run_id)
492	            if not owns(record, uid):
493	                return JSONResponse(status_code=404, content={"error": f"unknown run {run_id!r}"})
494	        start_after = parse_last_event_id(last_event_id, after, last_event_id_q)
495	        log.info("sse.open", "client attached", run_id=run_id, after=start_after)
496	        return StreamingResponse(
497	            _event_stream(runs, run_id, start_after),
498	            media_type="text/event-stream",
499	            headers={
500	                "Cache-Control": "no-cache, no-transform",
501	                "Connection": "keep-alive",
502	                "X-Accel-Buffering": "no",
503	            },
504	        )
505	
506	    app.state.store = runs
507	    return app
508	
509	
510	def _scenario_title(catalogue: list[dict[str, Any]], scenario_id: str) -> str:
511	    for item in catalogue or []:
512	        if isinstance(item, dict) and item.get("id") == scenario_id:
513	            title = item.get("title")
514	            if isinstance(title, str) and title.strip():
515	                return title.strip()
516	    return scenario_id
517	
518	
519	async def _event_stream(runs: RunStore, run_id: str, after: int) -> Iterable[str]:
520	    """Replay everything after `after`, then poll Store.events_after until the run ends or the
521	    110 s window closes (Modal kills a web request at 150 s; the client reconnects with
522	    Last-Event-ID and `reason: window` tells it this is NOT the end of the run)."""
523	    t0 = time.monotonic()
524	    last_id = after
525	    last_keepalive = t0
526	    sent = 0
527	    status: str | None = None
528	    yield "retry: 1000\n\n"
529	    try:
530	        while True:
531	            page = await asyncio.to_thread(runs.events_after, run_id, last_id)
532	            for event in (page or {}).get("events") or []:
533	                if not isinstance(event, dict) or event.get("id") is None:
534	                    continue
535	                if event["id"] > last_id:
536	                    last_id = event["id"]
537	                    sent += 1
538	                    yield sse_frame(event)
539	            status = (page or {}).get("status")
540	            now = time.monotonic()
541	            if status in TERMINAL:
542	                yield (
543	                    "event: done\ndata: "
544	                    + json.dumps({"reason": "finished", "status": status, "last_event_id": last_id,
545	                                  "run_id": run_id})
546	                    + "\n\n"
547	                )
548	                log.info("sse.close", "run finished", run_id=run_id, status=status, sent=sent,
549	                         dur_ms=int((now - t0) * 1000))
550	                return
551	            if now - t0 >= config.SSE_WINDOW_S:
552	                yield (
553	                    "event: done\ndata: "
554	                    + json.dumps({"reason": "window", "status": status, "last_event_id": last_id,
555	                                  "run_id": run_id})
556	                    + "\n\n"
557	                )
558	                log.info("sse.close", "window elapsed", run_id=run_id, status=status, sent=sent,
559	                         dur_ms=int((now - t0) * 1000))
560	                return
561	            if now - last_keepalive >= config.SSE_KEEPALIVE_S:
562	                last_keepalive = now
563	                yield ": keepalive\n\n"
564	            await asyncio.sleep(config.SSE_POLL_S)
565	    except asyncio.CancelledError:  # pragma: no cover - client went away
566	        log.info("sse.cancelled", "client disconnected", run_id=run_id, sent=sent)
567	        raise
