Tool reply2026-09-13T00:38:10.049Z
1	"""Failure provenance: what the agent saw vs. what really happened (PLAN.md §2.11).
2	
3	The agent is handed OS/HTTP-style errors and nothing else — that is the experiment. Everything
4	downstream of the agent (the ledger, the run events, the run status, the UI) has to be able to
5	answer two questions about every failure:
6	
7	    origin — who caused it:  injected (short-circuited / ack-withheld at the tool boundary)
8	                             staged   (a real OS error on a path the scenario deleted at reset)
9	                             real     (an unplanned failure of sandbox/transport/harness/model/gym)
10	    layer  — what really failed: boundary | filesystem | sandbox | transport | harness | model | gym
11	
12	`docs/error-taxonomy.md` is the contract; the `label` strings below are rendered verbatim by
13	apps/web, so they are copied from that table character for character and covered by a test that
14	diffs them against the document.
15	
16	Everything here is pure: no I/O, no clock, no globals. `loop.py` supplies the inputs (the tool
17	result, the `observe().faults_fired` delta for that call, whether the failure came from our own
18	transport layer) and gets back `(outcome, error_class | None)`.
19	"""
20	
21	from __future__ import annotations
22	
23	import re
24	from typing import Any
25	
26	from faultline_common.schemas import ErrorClass, FaultFired
27	
28	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- labels (verbatim)
29	
30	#: (origin, layer, code) -> label. `None` as the code means "every code in this row".
31	LABELS: dict[tuple[str, str, str | None], str] = {
32	    ("injected", "boundary", "ENOENT"): "simulated: missing file (file still on disk)",
33	    ("injected", "boundary", "EACCES"): "simulated: write denied (nothing written)",
34	    ("injected", "boundary", "ETIMEDOUT"): "simulated: lost ack (write landed; response withheld)",
35	    ("staged", "filesystem", None): "staged: file absent since reset",
36	    ("real", "filesystem", None): "real: OS error in sandbox",
37	    ("real", "sandbox", None): "real: sandbox terminated or unavailable",
38	    ("real", "transport", None): "real: transport failure harness<->sandbox-env (outcome unknown)",
39	    ("real", "harness", None): "real: harness worker interrupted mid-call (outcome unknown)",
40	    ("real", "model", None): "real: model API error",
41	    ("real", "gym", None): "real: gym control-plane failure",
42	    ("real", "boundary", None): "real: internal error in sandbox-env",
43	}
44	
45	#: Run-level harness failure that is NOT an interrupted call (a bug, a bad config). The taxonomy
46	#: table has no row for it — its real/harness row is specifically "interrupted mid-call" — so this
47	#: one extra string exists rather than mislabelling a crash as an interruption. Reported as a
48	#: contract deviation; the UI renders `label` verbatim either way.
49	HARNESS_FAILURE_LABEL = "real: harness failure (the run could not continue)"
50	
51	#: One plain-language sentence per injected/staged fault kind, used when the gym does not send one.
52	FAULT_DESCRIPTIONS: dict[tuple[str, str], str] = {
53	    ("injected", "missing_file"): "the read was refused at the tool boundary; the file is still on disk",
54	    ("injected", "denied_write"): "the write was refused at the tool boundary; nothing was written",
55	    ("injected", "ack_lost"): "the write was applied but the acknowledgement was withheld",
56	    ("staged", "missing_file"): "the scenario deleted this file at reset; the sandbox really cannot find it",
57	}
58	
59	#: (origin, fault kind) -> how that fault presents on a tool result.
60	FAULT_PROFILE: dict[tuple[str, str], dict[str, Any]] = {
61	    ("injected", "missing_file"): {
62	        "code": "ENOENT", "layer": "boundary", "outcome": "not_executed",
63	        "outcome_known": True, "side_effect_applied": False,
64	    },
65	    ("injected", "denied_write"): {
66	        "code": "EACCES", "layer": "boundary", "outcome": "not_executed",
67	        "outcome_known": True, "side_effect_applied": False,
68	    },
69	    ("injected", "ack_lost"): {
70	        "code": "ETIMEDOUT", "layer": "boundary", "outcome": "unknown",
71	        "outcome_known": False, "side_effect_applied": True,
72	    },
73	    ("staged", "missing_file"): {
74	        # Nothing is intercepted for a sticky missing_file: the sandbox really ran the call and
75	        # really could not find the file, so the outcome is `failed`, not `not_executed`.
76	        "code": "ENOENT", "layer": "filesystem", "outcome": "failed",
77	        "outcome_known": True, "side_effect_applied": None,
78	    },
79	}
80	
81	#: Codes sandbox-env emits when the Modal Sandbox itself is gone. Before 2026-09-12 18:30 the gym
82	#: reported those as EINTERNAL (run r_ccda8780cbee is the specimen), and the deployed gym still
83	#: does, so a sandbox-death message under EINTERNAL is upgraded to ESANDBOX here. The upgrade is
84	#: recorded in `detail` and becomes a no-op the moment the gym sends ESANDBOX itself.
85	SANDBOX_LOSS_RE = re.compile(
86	    r"(task has already finished"
87	    r"|has already finished with status"
88	    r"|notfounderror"
89	    r"|workspaceerror"
90	    r"|sandbox (?:is )?(?:gone|terminated|unavailable|not found)"
91	    r"|container [^\"]{0,60}not found)",
92	    re.IGNORECASE,
93	)
94	
95	#: The text a resuming worker hands the agent for a call that was in flight when the worker died.
96	HARNESS_INTERRUPTION_TEXT = (
97	    "harness worker was interrupted while this call was in flight; the operation may or may not "
98	    "have completed — verify before retrying"
99	)
100	
101	REAL_ONLY_CODES = {"ESANDBOX", "ETRANSPORT", "EHARNESS", "EMODEL", "EGYM"}
102	FILESYSTEM_CODES = {"ENOENT", "EACCES", "EINVAL"}
103	BOUNDARY_CODES = {"EINTERNAL", "ENOEPISODE"}
104	
105	
106	def label_for(origin: str, layer: str, code: str | None) -> str:
107	    """The fixed UI string for one (origin, layer, code) row of docs/error-taxonomy.md."""
108	    return LABELS.get((origin, layer, code)) or LABELS.get((origin, layer, None)) or (
109	        f"{origin}: {layer} failure ({code})"
110	    )
111	
112	
113	def error_class(
114	    origin: str,
115	    layer: str,
116	    code: str,
117	    *,
118	    kind: str | None = None,
119	    outcome_known: bool = True,
120	    side_effect_applied: bool | None = None,
121	    detail: str | None = None,
122	    label: str | None = None,
123	) -> dict[str, Any]:
124	    """Build an `ErrorClass` dict, validated against the shared schema so the wire shape cannot drift."""
125	    return ErrorClass(
126	        origin=origin,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
127	        layer=layer,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
128	        code=code,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
129	        kind=kind,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
130	        label=label or label_for(origin, layer, code),
131	        outcome_known=outcome_known,
132	        side_effect_applied=side_effect_applied,
133	        detail=detail,
134	    ).model_dump()
135	
136	
137	def looks_like_sandbox_loss(text: str | None) -> bool:
138	    return bool(text) and bool(SANDBOX_LOSS_RE.search(text or ""))
139	
140	
141	def short_id(value: str | None, limit: int = 16) -> str | None:
142	    """Sandbox ids are long and are ops data, not model data: keep a recognisable prefix."""
143	    if not value:
144	        return None
145	    return value if len(value) <= limit else value[:limit]
146	
147	
148	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- faults
149	
150	
151	def normalize_fault(raw: dict[str, Any] | None, *, step: int | None = None) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
152	    """A `FaultFired` with origin/layer/description always filled in.
153	
154	    sandbox-env is the source of truth and its values always win. When it omits them (the deployed
155	    gym still does — its provenance pass has not landed), they are derived from what FAULTS.md says
156	    each injector does: a *sticky* `missing_file` is `staged/filesystem` (the file really is gone),
157	    everything else is `injected/boundary` (nothing real failed).
158	    """
159	    if not raw:
160	        return None
161	    data = dict(raw)
162	    kind = str(data.get("kind") or "")
163	    mode = str(data.get("mode") or "transient")
164	    origin = data.get("origin") or ("staged" if (kind == "missing_file" and mode == "sticky") else "injected")
165	    layer = data.get("layer") or ("filesystem" if origin == "staged" else "boundary")
166	    description = data.get("description") or FAULT_DESCRIPTIONS.get((origin, kind), "")
167	    merged = {
168	        **data,
169	        "step": data.get("step") if data.get("step") is not None else step,
170	        "kind": kind,
171	        "path": data.get("path") or "",
172	        "mode": mode,
173	        "origin": origin,
174	        "layer": layer,
175	        "description": description,
176	    }
177	    try:
178	        validated = FaultFired.model_validate(merged).model_dump()
179	    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - an unknown kind must not kill the run; keep the raw shape
180	        return merged
181	    # Keep harness-only annotations (e.g. `inferred`) that FaultFired does not model.
182	    return {**merged, **validated}
183	
184	
185	def fault_error_class(fault: dict[str, Any], detail: str | None = None) -> tuple[str, dict[str, Any]]:
186	    """(outcome, ErrorClass) for a tool result explained by an injected/staged fault."""
187	    origin = str(fault.get("origin") or "injected")
188	    kind = str(fault.get("kind") or "")
189	    profile = FAULT_PROFILE.get((origin, kind))
190	    if profile is None:
191	        # An unknown kind is still a fault the gym reported: say so instead of inventing a layer.
192	        return "unknown", error_class(
193	            origin, "boundary", "EINTERNAL", kind=kind or None, outcome_known=False,
194	            detail=detail or f"unknown fault kind {kind!r}",
195	        )
196	    bits = dict(profile)
197	    return bits["outcome"], error_class(
198	        origin,
199	        bits["layer"],
200	        bits["code"],
201	        kind=kind,
202	        outcome_known=bits["outcome_known"],
203	        side_effect_applied=bits["side_effect_applied"],
204	        detail=detail or (fault.get("description") or None),
205	    )
206	
207	
208	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- tool results
209	
210	
211	def classify_result(
212	    *,
213	    is_error: bool,
214	    code: str | None,
215	    output: str | None = None,
216	    fault: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
217	    transport_kind: str | None = None,
218	    payload: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
219	    observed: bool = True,
220	) -> tuple[str, dict[str, Any] | None]:
221	    """One tool result -> (`ToolOutcome`, `ErrorClass` or None when the call did not fail).
222	
223	    Arguments
224	      is_error        the harness/MCP verdict for this result
225	      code            the ErrorCode carried by the payload, or assigned by our transport layer
226	      output          the text handed to the model (used only to recognise a dead sandbox)
227	      fault           the normalised `observe().faults_fired` delta for THIS call, if any
228	      transport_kind  set when our own client failed: "connect" | "timeout" | "abort" | "protocol"
229	      payload         the parsed structured result (for `exit_code` on a successful call)
230	      observed        False when observe() could not be reached, so "no fault" proves nothing
231	    """
232	    if not is_error:
233	        exit_code = (payload or {}).get("exit_code")
234	        if isinstance(exit_code, int) and exit_code != 0:
235	            return "failed", None
236	        return "executed", None
237	
238	    # 1. our own worker was interrupted while the call was in flight (written by the resuming worker)
239	    if code == "EHARNESS":
240	        return "unknown", error_class(
241	            "real", "harness", "EHARNESS", outcome_known=False,
242	            detail="a previous worker died with this call in flight; the ledger has the truth",
243	        )
244	
245	    # 2. our own transport failed (connection, client abort, client timeout) — outcome unknown
246	    if transport_kind:
247	        transport_code = "ETIMEDOUT" if transport_kind == "timeout" else "ETRANSPORT"
248	        return "unknown", error_class(
249	            "real", "transport", transport_code, outcome_known=False,
250	            detail=f"harness->sandbox-env {transport_kind} failure; the request may have been served",
251	        )
252	
253	    # 3. the Modal Sandbox is gone (the call never ran)
254	    if code == "ESANDBOX" or (code in BOUNDARY_CODES and looks_like_sandbox_loss(output)):
255	        upgraded = code != "ESANDBOX"
256	        return "not_executed", error_class(
257	            "real", "sandbox", "ESANDBOX", outcome_known=True, side_effect_applied=False,
258	            detail=("sandbox-env reported the sandbox as unavailable"
259	                    + (f" (as {code}; upgraded to ESANDBOX per docs/error-taxonomy.md)" if upgraded else "")),
260	        )
261	
262	    # 4. the gym's ledger explains it: an injected or staged fault fired for this call
263	    if fault:
264	        detail = "inferred from the error code: observe() was unavailable" if fault.get("inferred") else None
265	        return fault_error_class(fault, detail)
266	
267	    # 5. a real failure inside the sandbox, or at its boundary
268	    if code in ("ENOENT", "EACCES"):
269	        return "failed", error_class(
270	            "real", "filesystem", code, outcome_known=True, side_effect_applied=False,
271	            detail="the sandbox ran the call and the OS refused it; no fault fired for this step",
272	        )
273	    if code == "EINVAL":
274	        return "not_executed", error_class(
275	            "real", "filesystem", "EINVAL", outcome_known=True, side_effect_applied=False,
276	            detail="the tool boundary rejected the arguments before the sandbox saw them",
277	        )
278	    if code == "ETRANSPORT":
279	        return "unknown", error_class(
280	            "real", "transport", "ETRANSPORT", outcome_known=False,
281	            detail="sandbox-env reported a transport failure",
282	        )
283	    if code == "ETIMEDOUT":
284	        # Only `ack_lost` makes the gym answer ETIMEDOUT (GRADING.md), so a timeout WITH a fault
285	        # delta is handled at 4. Getting here means observe() ran and reported nothing: say that,
286	        # rather than claiming an injection we cannot see.
287	        if observed:
288	            return "unknown", error_class(
289	                "real", "transport", "ETIMEDOUT", outcome_known=False,
290	                detail="a timeout with no fault recorded in the ledger for this call",
291	            )
292	        return "unknown", error_class(
293	            "injected", "boundary", "ETIMEDOUT", kind="ack_lost", outcome_known=False,
294	            side_effect_applied=True,
295	            detail="inferred from the error code: observe() was unavailable",
296	        )
297	    if code in BOUNDARY_CODES:
298	        return "not_executed", error_class(
299	            "real", "boundary", code, outcome_known=True, side_effect_applied=False,
300	            detail="sandbox-env failed before the sandbox ran the call",
301	        )
302	
303	    return "not_executed", error_class(
304	        "real", "boundary", "EINTERNAL", outcome_known=True, side_effect_applied=False,
305	        detail=f"unrecognised tool error (code={code!r})",
306	    )
307	
308	
309	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- run-level
310	
311	
312	def run_error_class(exc: BaseException, *, phase: str = "loop") -> dict[str, Any]:
313	    """Classify the exception that ended a run (model API, gym control plane, or our own bug)."""
314	    name = type(exc).__name__
315	    detail = f"{name}: {exc}"[:400]
316	    if name == "GymError" or name.startswith("Gym"):
317	        return error_class("real", "gym", "EGYM", detail=f"{phase}: {detail}")
318	    module = type(exc).__module__ or ""
319	    if module.startswith("anthropic") or name.startswith(("API", "RateLimit", "Authentication", "BadRequest")):
320	        return error_class("real", "model", "EMODEL", detail=f"{phase}: {detail}")
321	    return error_class(
322	        "real", "harness", "EHARNESS", outcome_known=True, label=HARNESS_FAILURE_LABEL,
323	        detail=f"{phase}: {detail}",
324	    )
325	
326	
327	def evaluation_error_class(exc_text: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
328	    """`unevaluated` runs: the gym could not grade. Sandbox loss and control-plane failure differ."""
329	    if looks_like_sandbox_loss(exc_text) or "503" in (exc_text or ""):
330	        return error_class(
331	            "real", "sandbox", "ESANDBOX", outcome_known=True, side_effect_applied=False,
332	            detail=f"evaluate could not reach the sandbox: {exc_text[:300]}",
333	        )
334	    return error_class("real", "gym", "EGYM", detail=f"evaluate failed: {exc_text[:300]}")
335	
336	
337	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- ledger echo
338	
339	#: ledger `outcome` -> did the side effect really happen? (FAULTS.md / GRADING.md)
340	LEDGER_SIDE_EFFECT = {"ok": True, "ack_lost": True, "short_circuit": False, "error": False}
341	
342	
343	def ledger_side_effect(row: dict[str, Any]) -> bool | None:
344	    """Ground truth for one ledger row: did the write/command actually run?"""
345	    outcome = str(row.get("outcome") or "")
346	    if row.get("interrupted"):
347	        # The harness never saw the response; the ledger knows whether the call completed.
348	        return outcome == "ok"
349	    return LEDGER_SIDE_EFFECT.get(outcome)

Tool reply2026-09-13T00:38:10.811Z
1	"""Real interruptions: planned chaos triggers, and picking a half-finished run back up.
2	
3	Two halves, both pure enough to unit-test without Modal:
4	
5	  * `HarnessFaultPlan` — the scenario's `harness_faults[]` (PLAN.md §2.11 / schemas.HarnessFault).
6	    These are *real* failures the harness inflicts on itself at a chosen call: `worker_crash` kills
7	    the process with the request already at sandbox-env, `transport_abort` cancels the in-flight
8	    HTTP request. They are deliberate (`planned: true`) but nothing about them is simulated: the
9	    worker really dies, the connection really goes away, and the recovery path is the real one.
10	
11	  * `plan_resume` — rebuild the Anthropic conversation from the persisted event log so a fresh
12	    worker (Modal re-invokes `run_episode` with the SAME inputs after a crash) can continue the run
13	    instead of starting over. Whatever tool call was in flight when the old worker died becomes an
14	    `interruption` event plus a synthetic `EHARNESS` tool result whose outcome is *unknown*, which
15	    is exactly what the agent has to reason about.
16	
17	Nothing here talks to the network; `loop.py` owns the I/O.
18	"""
19	
20	from __future__ import annotations
21	
22	import json
23	import shlex
24	from dataclasses import dataclass, field
25	from typing import Any, Callable
26	
27	from faultline_common.schemas import Interruption
28	
29	from .classify import HARNESS_INTERRUPTION_TEXT, error_class, label_for, normalize_fault
30	
31	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- path matching
32	
33	
34	def normalize_path(token: str) -> str:
35	    """GRADING.md token normalisation: strip quotes, `./` and a `/workspace/` prefix."""
36	    t = (token or "").strip().strip("'\"")
37	    if t.startswith("/workspace/"):
38	        t = t[len("/workspace/"):]
39	    while t.startswith("./"):
40	        t = t[2:]
41	    return t.rstrip("/")
42	
43	
44	def call_paths(tool: str, args: dict[str, Any] | None) -> set[str]:
45	    """Every workspace path this call plausibly touches (argv tokens for `run_command`)."""
46	    a = args or {}
47	    if tool == "run_command":
48	        command = str(a.get("command") or "")
49	        try:
50	            tokens = shlex.split(command)
51	        except ValueError:  # unbalanced quotes: fall back to whitespace
52	            tokens = command.split()
53	        return {normalize_path(t) for t in tokens if t and not t.startswith("-")}
54	    path = a.get("path")
55	    return {normalize_path(str(path))} if path else set()
56	
57	
58	def primary_path(tool: str, args: dict[str, Any] | None) -> str | None:
59	    a = args or {}
60	    if tool == "run_command":
61	        return None
62	    path = a.get("path")
63	    return normalize_path(str(path)) if path else None
64	
65	
66	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- harness fault plan
67	
68	
69	def fault_key(fault: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
70	    """Stable identity of one harness-fault trigger, persisted so it fires at most once per run."""
71	    return f"{fault.get('kind')}:{fault.get('tool')}:{normalize_path(str(fault.get('path') or ''))}:{fault.get('nth', 1)}"
72	
73	
74	@dataclass
75	class HarnessFaultPlan:
76	    """Which planned interruption (if any) fires on this dispatch.
77	
78	    `counts` tracks matching calls per trigger and is rebuilt from the event log on resume;
79	    `fired` is persisted on the run record so the worker that takes over after a crash does not
80	    crash again on the retry of the very same call.
81	    """
82	
83	    faults: list[dict[str, Any]] = field(default_factory=list)
84	    fired: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
85	    counts: dict[str, int] = field(default_factory=dict)
86	    source: str = "scenario"
87	
88	    @classmethod
89	    def build(cls, raw: Any, *, fired: Any = None, source: str = "scenario") -> "HarnessFaultPlan":
90	        faults = [dict(f) for f in (raw or []) if isinstance(f, dict) and f.get("kind")]
91	        return cls(faults=faults, fired=set(fired or []), source=source)
92	
93	    def __bool__(self) -> bool:
94	        return bool(self.faults)
95	
96	    def matches(self, fault: dict[str, Any], tool: str, args: dict[str, Any] | None) -> bool:
97	        if str(fault.get("tool") or "write_file") != tool:
98	            return False
99	        want = normalize_path(str(fault.get("path") or ""))
100	        return not want or want in call_paths(tool, args)
101	
102	    def observe(self, tool: str, args: dict[str, Any] | None) -> None:
103	        """Count a dispatch without firing (used to replay history on resume)."""
104	        for fault in self.faults:
105	            if self.matches(fault, tool, args):
106	                key = fault_key(fault)
107	                self.counts[key] = self.counts.get(key, 0) + 1
108	
109	    def take(self, tool: str, args: dict[str, Any] | None) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
110	        """Count this dispatch and return the trigger that fires on it, if any."""
111	        hit: dict[str, Any] | None = None
112	        for fault in self.faults:
113	            if not self.matches(fault, tool, args):
114	                continue
115	            key = fault_key(fault)
116	            self.counts[key] = self.counts.get(key, 0) + 1
117	            if hit is None and key not in self.fired and self.counts[key] == int(fault.get("nth") or 1):
118	                hit = fault
119	                self.fired.add(key)
120	        return hit
121

126	def interruption(
127	    *,
128	    layer: str,
129	    code: str,
130	    step: int | None = None,
131	    tool_use_id: str | None = None,
132	    tool: str | None = None,
133	    path: str | None = None,
134	    outcome_known: bool = False,
135	    planned: bool = False,
136	    resumed: bool = False,
137	    worker_generation: int = 1,
138	    at: str,
139	    detail: str | None = None,
140	) -> dict[str, Any]:
141	    """An `Interruption` (schemas.Interruption): always real, always about a real component."""
142	    return Interruption(
143	        step=step,
144	        layer=layer,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
145	        code=code,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
146	        label=label_for("real", layer, code),
147	        tool_use_id=tool_use_id,
148	        tool=tool,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
149	        path=path,
150	        outcome_known=outcome_known,
151	        planned=planned,
152	        resumed=resumed,
153	        worker_generation=worker_generation,
154	        at=at,
155	        detail=detail,
156	    ).model_dump()

159	def harness_result_data(tool_use_id: str, tool: str | None, path: str | None,
160	                        *, duration_ms: int = 0, attempts: int = 1,
161	                        sandbox: dict[str, Any] | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
162	    """`tool.result` payload the resuming worker writes for the call that was in flight."""
163	    payload = {"error": HARNESS_INTERRUPTION_TEXT, "code": "EHARNESS"}
164	    if path:
165	        payload["path"] = path
166	    return {
167	        "tool_use_id": tool_use_id,
168	        "tool": tool or "run_command",
169	        "output": json.dumps(payload),
170	        "is_error": True,
171	        "duration_ms": duration_ms,
172	        "error_code": "EHARNESS",
173	        "outcome": "unknown",
174	        "error_class": error_class(
175	            "real", "harness", "EHARNESS", outcome_known=False,
176	            detail="written by the resuming worker; the ledger has the truth after the fact",
177	        ),
178	        "attempts": attempts,
179	        "sandbox": sandbox or {},
180	        "summary": path or "",
181	        "synthetic": True,
182	    }

188	@dataclass
189	class ResumePlan:
190	    """Everything the new worker needs to continue a run that lost its worker."""
191	
192	    episode_id: str | None
193	    sandbox_id: str | None
194	    task_prompt: str
195	    scenario: dict[str, Any]
196	    files: list[dict[str, Any]]
197	    workspace_root: str
198	    messages: list[dict[str, Any]]
199	    step: int
200	    seen_faults: list[dict[str, Any]]
201	    usage: dict[str, int]
202	    dangling: list[dict[str, Any]]
203	    resumed_from_event_id: int
204	    dropped_thinking: int = 0
205	    submitted: bool = False
206	
207	
208	def _content_of(events: list[dict[str, Any]], step: int | None) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], list[dict[str, Any]]]:
209	    """(assistant blocks, tool.call events) for one step, in emission order."""
210	    assistant: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
211	    calls: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
212	    for ev in events:
213	        if ev.get("step") != step:
214	            continue
215	        data = ev.get("data") or {}
216	        if ev.get("type") == "turn.text" and str(data.get("text") or "").strip():
217	            assistant.append({"type": "text", "text": str(data["text"])})
218	        elif ev.get("type") == "tool.call":
219	            calls.append(ev)
220	            assistant.append({
221	                "type": "tool_use",
222	                "id": data.get("tool_use_id"),
223	                "name": data.get("tool"),
224	                "input": data.get("input") or {},
225	            })
226	    return assistant, calls
227	
228	
229	def plan_resume(record: dict[str, Any], *, build_task_message: Callable[..., str]) -> ResumePlan:
230	    """Rebuild the conversation (and the loop's counters) from the persisted event log.
231	
232	    Thinking blocks are deliberately NOT replayed: the API requires the original signature with a
233	    replayed `thinking` block and the event log stores only the summarised text. The default model
234	    (claude-haiku-4-5) emits none, so this is a no-op on every scenario we ship; the count is
235	    surfaced so a Sonnet/Opus resume says out loud what it dropped.
236	    """
237	    events = list(record.get("events") or [])
238	    reset = next((e for e in events if e.get("type") == "episode.reset"), {}) or {}
239	    reset_data = reset.get("data") or {}
240	    scenario = reset_data.get("scenario") or {}
241	    task_prompt = str(reset_data.get("task_prompt") or record.get("task_prompt") or "")
242	    files = list(reset_data.get("files") or [])
243	    workspace_root = str(reset_data.get("workspace_root") or "/workspace")
244	
245	    messages: list[dict[str, Any]] = [
246	        {"role": "user", "content": build_task_message(task_prompt, files, workspace_root)}
247	    ]
248	
249	    results: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
250	    for ev in events:
251	        if ev.get("type") == "tool.result":
252	            data = ev.get("data") or {}
253	            if data.get("tool_use_id"):
254	                results[str(data["tool_use_id"])] = data
255	
256	    steps = sorted({e.get("step") for e in events if isinstance(e.get("step"), int)})
257	    dangling: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
258	    dropped_thinking = sum(1 for e in events if e.get("type") == "turn.thinking")
259	    submitted = False
260	    last_step = 0
261	
262	    for step in steps:
263	        assistant, calls = _content_of(events, step)
264	        if not assistant:
265	            continue
266	        last_step = max(last_step, int(step))
267	        messages.append({"role": "assistant", "content": assistant})
268	        tool_results: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
269	        step_dangling: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
270	        for call in calls:
271	            data = call.get("data") or {}
272	            tuid = str(data.get("tool_use_id") or "")
273	            result = results.get(tuid)
274	            if result is None:
275	                step_dangling.append({
276	                    "tool_use_id": tuid,
277	                    "tool": data.get("tool"),
278	                    "input": data.get("input") or {},
279	                    "path": primary_path(str(data.get("tool") or ""), data.get("input") or {}),
280	                    "step": step,
281	                    "event_id": call.get("id"),
282	                })
283	                continue
284	            if data.get("tool") == "submit":
285	                submitted = True
286	            tool_results.append({
287	                "type": "tool_result",
288	                "tool_use_id": tuid,
289	                "content": str(result.get("output") or "(no output)"),
290	                "is_error": bool(result.get("is_error")),
291	            })
292	        if tool_results:
293	            messages.append({"role": "user", "content": tool_results})
294	        dangling.extend(step_dangling)
295	
296	    seen_faults = [normalize_fault(e.get("data") or {}, step=e.get("step"))
297	                   for e in events if e.get("type") == "fault.fired"]
298	    usage = {"input_tokens": 0, "output_tokens": 0, "cache_read_input_tokens": 0,
299	             "cache_creation_input_tokens": 0}
300	    for ev in events:
301	        if ev.get("type") != "llm.call":
302	            continue
303	        for key, value in ((ev.get("data") or {}).get("usage") or {}).items():
304	            if key in usage and isinstance(value, int):
305	                usage[key] += value
306	
307	    return ResumePlan(
308	        episode_id=record.get("episode_id") or reset_data.get("episode_id"),
309	        sandbox_id=reset_data.get("sandbox_id"),
310	        task_prompt=task_prompt,
311	        scenario=scenario,
312	        files=files,
313	        workspace_root=workspace_root,
314	        messages=messages,
315	        step=last_step,
316	        seen_faults=[f for f in seen_faults if f],
317	        usage=usage,
318	        dangling=dangling,
319	        resumed_from_event_id=int(events[-1].get("id") or 0) if events else -1,
320	        dropped_thinking=dropped_thinking,
321	        submitted=submitted,
322	    )
323	
324	
325	def replay_call_counts(plan: HarnessFaultPlan, record: dict[str, Any]) -> HarnessFaultPlan:
326	    """Re-count every tool call already in the log so `nth` keeps its meaning across a resume."""
327	    for ev in record.get("events") or []:
328	        if ev.get("type") != "tool.call":
329	            continue
330	        data = ev.get("data") or {}
331	        plan.observe(str(data.get("tool") or ""), data.get("input") or {})
332	    return plan
333	
334	
335	def has_progress(record: dict[str, Any] | None) -> bool:
336	    """True when a run already produced events beyond `run.started` — i.e. it can be resumed."""
337	    events = list((record or {}).get("events") or [])
338	    return any(e.get("type") not in ("run.started", "log") for e in events)
339

341	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- ledger echo
342	
343	
344	_LEDGER_TOOLS = {"run_command", "read_file", "write_file", "list_dir"}
345	
346	
347	def align_ledger(ledger: list[dict[str, Any]], calls: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
348	    """Match the gym's ledger rows to this run's tool calls, in order.
349	
350	    The ledger counts one row per MCP call the *gym* served and numbers them with its own step
351	    counter, which is not the harness's step (a model turn can issue several calls, and a call the
352	    harness abandoned mid-flight still reached the server). So the two are aligned greedily by
353	    order, requiring the tool name to agree and the path to agree when both sides know one.
354	    """
355	    out: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
356	    used: set[int] = set()
357	    for row in ledger or []:
358	        tool = row.get("tool")
359	        path = normalize_path(str(row.get("path") or "")) or None
360	        match_idx: int | None = None
361	        for i, call in enumerate(calls):
362	            if i in used or call.get("tool") != tool:
363	                continue
364	            call_path = call.get("path")
365	            if path and call_path and path != call_path:
366	                continue
367	            if path and not call_path and path not in call_paths(str(tool or ""), call.get("input")):
368	                continue
369	            match_idx = i
370	            break
371	        if match_idx is None:
372	            out.append({"tool_use_id": None, "step": row.get("step"), "tool": tool, "path": path})
373	            continue
374	        used.add(match_idx)
375	        call = calls[match_idx]
376	        out.append({"tool_use_id": call.get("tool_use_id"), "step": call.get("step"),
377	                    "tool": tool, "path": path})
378	    return out

Tool reply2026-09-13T00:38:11.580Z
1	"""MCP (streamable HTTP) client wrapper around the sandbox-env tool surface.
2	
3	Responsibilities
4	  * discover tools and convert MCP `inputSchema` -> Anthropic tool defs `{name, description, input_schema}`
5	  * call a tool with an explicit timeout, carrying `X-Faultline-Episode` so the server knows which
6	    episode this is (the model never sees or supplies the episode id)
7	  * turn *every* transport failure into an `is_error` tool result carrying ToolError-shaped JSON.
8	    A tool failure must never crash the loop: the model is supposed to see failures and recover.
9	
10	is_error semantics (matches faultline_common.schemas):
11	  * payload has "code" in {ENOENT,EACCES,ETIMEDOUT,EINVAL,EINTERNAL,ENOEPISODE}  -> error
12	  * payload has "exit_code"                                                     -> normal result,
13	    even when the exit code is non-zero (a failing pytest run is data, not a tool error)
14	  * otherwise fall back to the MCP server's own is_error flag
15	Fault *attribution* never comes from this file — it comes from observe().faults_fired deltas.
16	"""
17	
18	from __future__ import annotations
19	
20	import asyncio
21	import json
22	import time
23	from dataclasses import dataclass
24	from typing import Any
25	
26	from faultline_common.log import get_logger, truncate
27	
28	from . import config
29	
30	log = get_logger(config.SVC)
31	
32	ERROR_CODES = {
33	    # OS/HTTP-style codes the agent sees (identical text whether injected or real)
34	    "ENOENT", "EACCES", "ETIMEDOUT", "EINVAL",
35	    # real-failure codes sandbox-env may report at the tool boundary (PLAN.md §2.11)
36	    "ESANDBOX", "ETRANSPORT", "EHARNESS", "EINTERNAL", "ENOEPISODE",
37	}
38	
39	EMPTY_SCHEMA: dict[str, Any] = {"type": "object", "properties": {}}
40	
41	#: Exception class names that mean the request never left this process, so re-sending it is safe.
42	#: Anything else (a reset mid-stream, a timeout, a protocol error) leaves the outcome UNKNOWN and
43	#: must never be retried — that is precisely the failure the grader asks the agent to reason about.
44	CONNECT_ERRORS = {"ConnectError", "ConnectTimeout", "ConnectionRefusedError", "ProxyError"}
45	
46	
47	def transport_kind_of(exc: BaseException) -> str:
48	    """'connect' (safe to retry) | 'timeout' | 'protocol' (outcome unknown)."""
49	    name = type(exc).__name__
50	    if name in CONNECT_ERRORS:
51	        return "connect"
52	    if "timeout" in name.lower():
53	        return "timeout"
54	    return "protocol"
55	
56	
57	@dataclass
58	class ToolCallResult:
59	    text: str
60	    is_error: bool
61	    duration_ms: int
62	    structured: dict[str, Any] | None = None
63	    error_code: str | None = None
64	    transport_error: bool = False
65	    #: set when OUR side failed: "connect" | "timeout" | "abort" | "protocol" (see transport_kind_of)
66	    transport_kind: str | None = None
67	    #: how many times the harness dispatched this call (1 = no retry)
68	    attempts: int = 1
69	
70	    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
71	        return {
72	            "text": self.text,
73	            "is_error": self.is_error,
74	            "duration_ms": self.duration_ms,
75	            "structured": self.structured,
76	            "error_code": self.error_code,
77	            "transport_error": self.transport_error,
78	            "transport_kind": self.transport_kind,
79	            "attempts": self.attempts,
80	        }
81

83	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- pure helpers
84	
85	
86	def tool_error_json(message: str, code: str, path: str | None = None, detail: str | None = None) -> str:
87	    """ToolError-shaped JSON, identical in shape to what sandbox-env returns for injected faults."""
88	    payload: dict[str, Any] = {"error": message, "code": code}
89	    if path is not None:
90	        payload["path"] = path
91	    if detail is not None:
92	        payload["detail"] = detail
93	    return json.dumps(payload)
94	
95	
96	def normalize_schema(schema: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
97	    """Coerce an MCP inputSchema into something the Anthropic tools API accepts."""
98	    if not isinstance(schema, dict) or not schema:
99	        return dict(EMPTY_SCHEMA)
100	    out = {k: v for k, v in schema.items() if k not in {"$schema"}}
101	    out.setdefault("type", "object")
102	    if out["type"] == "object":
103	        out.setdefault("properties", {})
104	    return out
105	
106	
107	def mcp_tools_to_anthropic(tools: list[Any]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
108	    """MCP Tool objects (or plain dicts, in tests) -> Anthropic tool definitions.
109	
110	    Order is preserved so the tools prefix stays stable across turns and prompt caching can hit.
111	    """
112	    defs: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
113	    for t in tools:
114	        if isinstance(t, dict):
115	            name = t.get("name")
116	            desc = t.get("description") or ""
117	            schema = t.get("inputSchema") or t.get("input_schema")
118	        else:
119	            name = getattr(t, "name", None)
120	            desc = getattr(t, "description", "") or ""
121	            schema = getattr(t, "inputSchema", None) or getattr(t, "input_schema", None)
122	        if not name:
123	            continue
124	        defs.append({"name": name, "description": desc, "input_schema": normalize_schema(schema)})
125	    return defs
126	
127	
128	def parse_payload(text: str, structured: Any = None) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
129	    """Best-effort JSON payload for a tool result (structured content wins, then the text)."""
130	    if isinstance(structured, dict):
131	        inner = structured.get("result")
132	        if isinstance(inner, dict):
133	            return inner
134	        return structured
135	    if not text:
136	        return None
137	    try:
138	        obj = json.loads(text)
139	    except (ValueError, TypeError):
140	        return None
141	    return obj if isinstance(obj, dict) else None
142	
143	
144	def classify(payload: dict[str, Any] | None, server_is_error: bool) -> tuple[bool, str | None]:
145	    """(is_error, error_code) — see module docstring."""
146	    if payload:
147	        code = payload.get("code")
148	        if isinstance(code, str) and code in ERROR_CODES:
149	            return True, code
150	        if "exit_code" in payload:
151	            return False, None
152	    return bool(server_is_error), None
153	
154	
155	def result_text(content: Any, structured: Any) -> str:
156	    """Text handed to the model: the server's TextContent blocks, else the structured payload."""
157	    parts: list[str] = []
158	    for block in content or []:
159	        txt = getattr(block, "text", None)
160	        if txt is None and isinstance(block, dict):
161	            txt = block.get("text")
162	        if txt:
163	            parts.append(txt)
164	    if parts:
165	        return "\n".join(parts)
166	    if structured is not None:
167	        try:
168	            return json.dumps(structured)
169	        except (TypeError, ValueError):  # pragma: no cover
170	            return str(structured)
171	    return ""
172	
173	
174	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- async client
175	
176	
177	class AsyncMCPClient:
178	    """Thin async wrapper over fastmcp.Client + StreamableHttpTransport."""
179	
180	    def __init__(self, base_url: str, episode_id: str, timeout_s: float = config.TOOL_TIMEOUT_S):
181	        self.base_url = base_url.rstrip("/")
182	        self.episode_id = episode_id
183	        self.timeout_s = timeout_s
184	        self._client: Any = None
185	
186	    @property
187	    def url(self) -> str:
188	        # The trailing slash matters: without it the streamable-HTTP mount 307s and some clients
189	        # drop the episode header on the redirect.
190	        return f"{self.base_url}/mcp/"
191	
192	    async def connect(self) -> None:
193	        from fastmcp import Client
194	        from fastmcp.client.transports import StreamableHttpTransport
195	
196	        transport = StreamableHttpTransport(
197	            url=self.url, headers={"X-Faultline-Episode": self.episode_id}
198	        )
199	        self._client = Client(transport)
200	        await self._client.__aenter__()
201	        log.info("mcp.connect", "connected", episode_id=self.episode_id, url=self.url)
202	
203	    async def close(self) -> None:
204	        if self._client is None:
205	            return
206	        try:
207	            await self._client.__aexit__(None, None, None)
208	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - closing must never raise into the loop
209	            log.warn("mcp.close", f"ignored: {exc}", episode_id=self.episode_id)
210	        finally:
211	            self._client = None
212	
213	    async def list_tools(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
214	        tools = await self._client.list_tools()
215	        defs = mcp_tools_to_anthropic(list(tools))
216	        log.info("mcp.list_tools", "discovered", episode_id=self.episode_id, tools=[d["name"] for d in defs])
217	        return defs
218	
219	    async def call(self, name: str, args: dict[str, Any], timeout: float | None = None,
220	                   abort_after_ms: int | None = None) -> ToolCallResult:
221	        """One tool call. `abort_after_ms` cancels the in-flight request client-side (the server
222	        still completes it) — the `transport_abort` harness fault, a genuine transport failure with
223	        an unknown outcome."""
224	        t0 = time.perf_counter()
225	        timeout = timeout or self.timeout_s
226	        try:
227	            call = self._client.call_tool(name, args, timeout=timeout, raise_on_error=False)
228	            if abort_after_ms is not None:
229	                res = await asyncio.wait_for(call, abort_after_ms / 1000.0)
230	            else:
231	                res = await call
232	        except asyncio.TimeoutError:
233	            dur = int((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000)
234	            if abort_after_ms is not None:
235	                log.warn("mcp.call.aborted", "in-flight request cancelled client-side",
236	                         tool=name, episode_id=self.episode_id, after_ms=abort_after_ms)
237	                return ToolCallResult(
238	                    text=tool_error_json(
239	                        f"{name}: the connection to the environment was lost after "
240	                        f"{abort_after_ms}ms; the operation may or may not have completed",
241	                        "ETRANSPORT",
242	                    ),
243	                    is_error=True,
244	                    duration_ms=dur,
245	                    error_code="ETRANSPORT",
246	                    transport_error=True,
247	                    transport_kind="abort",
248	                )
249	            return ToolCallResult(
250	                text=tool_error_json(
251	                    f"{name}: no response from the environment after {int(timeout)}s; "
252	                    "the operation may or may not have completed",
253	                    "ETIMEDOUT",
254	                ),
255	                is_error=True,
256	                duration_ms=dur,
257	                error_code="ETIMEDOUT",
258	                transport_error=True,
259	                transport_kind="timeout",
260	            )
261	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - transport/protocol failures become tool results
262	            dur = int((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000)
263	            kind = transport_kind_of(exc)
264	            code = "ETIMEDOUT" if kind == "timeout" else "ETRANSPORT"
265	            log.warn("mcp.call.transport_error", f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}", tool=name,
266	                     episode_id=self.episode_id, transport_kind=kind)
267	            return ToolCallResult(
268	                text=tool_error_json(f"{name}: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}", code),
269	                is_error=True,
270	                duration_ms=dur,
271	                error_code=code,
272	                transport_error=True,
273	                transport_kind=kind,
274	            )
275	        dur = int((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000)
276	        structured = getattr(res, "structured_content", None)
277	        text = result_text(getattr(res, "content", None), structured)
278	        payload = parse_payload(text, structured)
279	        is_error, code = classify(payload, bool(getattr(res, "is_error", False)))
280	        log.info(
281	            "mcp.call",
282	            "tool returned",
283	            tool=name,
284	            episode_id=self.episode_id,
285	            dur_ms=dur,
286	            is_error=is_error,
287	            code=code,
288	            out=truncate(text, 300),
289	        )
290	        return ToolCallResult(
291	            text=text, is_error=is_error, duration_ms=dur, structured=payload, error_code=code
292	        )

294	
295	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- sync facade
296	
297	
298	class SyncMCPClient:
299	    """Blocking facade used by the (synchronous) model loop.
300	
301	    Owns one event loop for its whole lifetime so the fastmcp session is entered and exited on the
302	    same loop. The loop itself is sync because `run_episode` is a plain Modal function.
303	    """
304	
305	    def __init__(self, base_url: str, episode_id: str, timeout_s: float = config.TOOL_TIMEOUT_S):
306	        self._async = AsyncMCPClient(base_url, episode_id, timeout_s)
307	        self._loop: asyncio.AbstractEventLoop | None = None
308	
309	    def open(self) -> "SyncMCPClient":
310	        self._loop = asyncio.new_event_loop()
311	        self._loop.run_until_complete(self._async.connect())
312	        return self
313	
314	    def list_tools(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
315	        assert self._loop is not None
316	        return self._loop.run_until_complete(self._async.list_tools())
317	
318	    def call(self, name: str, args: dict[str, Any], timeout: float | None = None,
319	             abort_after_ms: int | None = None) -> ToolCallResult:
320	        assert self._loop is not None
321	        return self._loop.run_until_complete(self._async.call(name, args, timeout, abort_after_ms))
322	
323	    def reconnect(self) -> "SyncMCPClient":
324	        """Re-establish the MCP session on the SAME event loop.
325	
326	        A cancelled streamable-HTTP request can leave the session unusable, so the loop calls this
327	        after a `transport_abort` rather than hoping the next call works.
328	        """
329	        assert self._loop is not None
330	        self._loop.run_until_complete(self._async.close())
331	        self._loop.run_until_complete(self._async.connect())
332	        return self
333	
334	    def close(self) -> None:
335	        if self._loop is None:
336	            return
337	        try:
338	            self._loop.run_until_complete(self._async.close())
339	        finally:
340	            try:
341	                self._loop.close()
342	            finally:
343	                self._loop = None
344	
345	    def __enter__(self) -> "SyncMCPClient":
346	        return self.open()
347	
348	    def __exit__(self, *exc: Any) -> None:
349	        self.close()

Tool reply2026-09-13T00:38:12.309Z
1	"""HTTP client for the sandbox-env gym REST surface (PLAN.md §2.2).
2	
3	    POST   /episodes                 reset   -> ResetResponse
4	    GET    /episodes/{id}            observe -> ObserveResponse
5	    POST   /episodes/{id}/evaluate   grade   -> EvaluateResponse
6	    DELETE /episodes/{id}            teardown
7	    GET    /scenarios                catalogue
8	    GET    /health
9	
10	Connection-level failures are retried with backoff; HTTP status errors are not (a 404 on observe
11	means the episode is gone, retrying only wastes the step budget). Every call is logged.
12	"""
13	
14	from __future__ import annotations
15	
16	import time
17	from typing import Any
18	
19	import httpx
20	
21	from faultline_common.log import get_logger, truncate
22	
23	from . import config
24	
25	log = get_logger(config.SVC)
26	
27	RETRYABLE = (httpx.ConnectError, httpx.ConnectTimeout, httpx.ReadTimeout, httpx.RemoteProtocolError, httpx.ReadError)
28	
29	
30	class GymError(RuntimeError):
31	    def __init__(self, message: str, status: int | None = None, body: str | None = None):
32	        super().__init__(message)
33	        self.status = status
34	        self.body = body
35	
36	
37	class GymClient:
38	    def __init__(
39	        self,
40	        base_url: str | None = None,
41	        timeout: float = config.GYM_TIMEOUT_S,
42	        retries: int = 3,
43	        client: httpx.Client | None = None,
44	        run_id: str | None = None,
45	    ):
46	        self.base_url = (base_url or config.sandbox_env_url()).rstrip("/")
47	        self.timeout = timeout
48	        self.retries = retries
49	        self.run_id = run_id
50	        self._client = client or httpx.Client(timeout=timeout, follow_redirects=True)
51	        self._owns_client = client is None
52	
53	    def close(self) -> None:
54	        if self._owns_client:
55	            self._client.close()
56	
57	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ transport
58	    def _request(
59	        self, method: str, path: str, *, json: Any = None, timeout: float | None = None, retries: int | None = None
60	    ) -> Any:
61	        url = f"{self.base_url}{path}"
62	        last: Exception | None = None
63	        retries = self.retries if retries is None else max(1, retries)
64	        for attempt in range(1, retries + 1):
65	            t0 = time.perf_counter()
66	            try:
67	                resp = self._client.request(method, url, json=json, timeout=timeout or self.timeout)
68	            except RETRYABLE as exc:
69	                last = exc
70	                log.warn(
71	                    "gym.retry",
72	                    f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}",
73	                    run_id=self.run_id,
74	                    method=method,
75	                    path=path,
76	                    attempt=attempt,
77	                )
78	                if attempt == retries:
79	                    break
80	                time.sleep(min(2 ** (attempt - 1), 4))
81	                continue
82	            dur = int((time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000)
83	            if resp.status_code >= 400:
84	                body = truncate(resp.text, 800)
85	                log.error(
86	                    "gym.error",
87	                    f"{method} {path} -> {resp.status_code}",
88	                    run_id=self.run_id,
89	                    status=resp.status_code,
90	                    dur_ms=dur,
91	                    body=body,
92	                )
93	                raise GymError(f"{method} {path} -> {resp.status_code}: {body}", resp.status_code, body)
94	            log.info("gym.call", f"{method} {path}", run_id=self.run_id, status=resp.status_code, dur_ms=dur)
95	            if not resp.content:
96	                return None
97	            try:
98	                return resp.json()
99	            except ValueError as exc:
100	                raise GymError(f"{method} {path}: non-JSON response: {truncate(resp.text, 200)}") from exc
101	        raise GymError(f"{method} {path}: unreachable after {retries} attempts: {last}") from last
102	
103	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ gym surface
104	    def scenarios(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
105	        data = self._request("GET", "/scenarios", timeout=15.0, retries=1)
106	        if isinstance(data, dict):
107	            for key in ("scenarios", "items", "data"):
108	                if isinstance(data.get(key), list):
109	                    return data[key]
110	            return []
111	        return data or []
112	
113	    def health(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
114	        return self._request("GET", "/health", timeout=15.0, retries=1) or {}
115	
116	    def reset(self, scenario_id: str, seed: int | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
117	        body: dict[str, Any] = {"scenario_id": scenario_id}
118	        if seed is not None:
119	            body["seed"] = seed
120	        return self._request("POST", "/episodes", json=body, timeout=max(self.timeout, 120.0))
121	
122	    def observe(self, episode_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
123	        return self._request("GET", f"/episodes/{episode_id}")
124	
125	    def evaluate(self, episode_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
126	        return self._request("POST", f"/episodes/{episode_id}/evaluate", json={}, timeout=max(self.timeout, 120.0))
127	
128	    def delete(self, episode_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
129	        return self._request("DELETE", f"/episodes/{episode_id}") or {"terminated": True}
130	
131	    def report_interruption(self, episode_id: str, report: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
132	        """Tell the gym we never received a call's response (body: `InterruptionReport`).
133	
134	        The ledger row is marked `interrupted: true` so `verified_before_rewrite` treats it exactly
135	        like `ack_lost` (GRADING.md). One attempt only: this is telemetry on the way to grading, and
136	        a run must never be held up by it. Raises `GymError`, which the loop logs and carries on.
137	        """
138	        return self._request(
139	            "POST", f"/episodes/{episode_id}/interruptions", json=report, timeout=20.0, retries=1
140	        ) or {}
141	
142	    def __enter__(self) -> "GymClient":
143	        return self
144	
145	    def __exit__(self, *exc: Any) -> None:
146	        self.close()
147

90	def thinking_for(model: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
91	    """`thinking` kwarg for messages.create, or None to omit it.
92	
93	    Sonnet 5 / Opus 5 -> `{"type": "adaptive"}` (never `budget_tokens`).
94	    Haiku 4.5 -> nothing at all, which is what every default run uses.

97	    extended-thinking budget is legal: `{"type": "enabled", "budget_tokens": n}` with n < max_tokens).
98	    Keep it here rather than in the loop so one env var covers every call site, and keep the default
99	    OFF so nothing about the demo path changes unless an operator asks for it.
100	    """

102	        return {"type": "adaptive"}
103	    if truthy("HARNESS_HAIKU_THINKING"):
104	        return {"type": "enabled", "budget_tokens": min(HAIKU_THINKING_BUDGET, MAX_TOKENS - 1)}
105	    return None

1	"""Shared wire contracts for Faultline (pydantic v2).
2	
3	Three consumers: services/sandbox-env (gym + MCP), services/agent-harness (model loop + API),
4	scripts/ (smoke + evidence). apps/web mirrors these in src/lib/types.ts — keep both in sync.
5	
6	Sections: faults & scenarios · MCP tool I/O · ledger · gym REST · harness runs/events · persistence · health.
7	"""
8	
9	from __future__ import annotations
10	
11	from typing import Any, Literal
12	
13	from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
14	
15	# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- faults & scenarios
16	
17	FaultKind = Literal["missing_file", "denied_write", "ack_lost"]
18	FaultMode = Literal["sticky", "transient"]
19	
20	
21	class FaultSpec(BaseModel):
22	    """One injected failure. Lives ONLY in sandbox-env state (never inside the command sandbox).
23	
24	    sticky    : applied at reset (e.g. the file is really deleted) and never lifted.
25	    transient : intercepted at the tool boundary for the first `hits` matching calls, then lifted.
26	    """
27	
28	    kind: FaultKind
29	    path: str = Field(description="workspace-relative path the fault targets")
30	    mode: FaultMode = "transient"
31	    hits: int | None = Field(default=1, description="matching calls affected before the fault lifts (None = forever)")
32	    delay_ms: int = Field(default=3000, description="ack_lost only: how long to hold the response before failing it")
33	
34	
35	class FaultPlan(BaseModel):
36	    faults: list[FaultSpec] = Field(default_factory=list)
37	
38	
39	HarnessFaultKind = Literal["worker_crash", "transport_abort"]
40	
41	
42	class Scenario(BaseModel):
43	    """Public scenario fields (safe to show the agent and the browser)."""
44	
45	    id: str
46	    title: str
47	    description: str = Field(description="one-paragraph human description shown in the UI")
48	    task_prompt: str = Field(description="what the agent is asked to do (goes into the user turn)")
49	    max_steps: int = 20
50	    fault_kinds: list[FaultKind] = Field(default_factory=list, description="which kinds are in play (not where/when)")
51	    harness_faults: list["HarnessFault"] = Field(default_factory=list, description="REAL interruptions the harness inflicts on itself (see HarnessFault)")
52	    checks: list["CheckSpec"] = Field(default_factory=list, description="what the grader will look for (public: id, description, weight)")
53	    faults_public: list["FaultPublic"] = Field(default_factory=list, description="fault classes in play (kind, layer, origin, description) — never paths, hits or timing")
54	
55	
56	class CheckSpec(BaseModel):
57	    id: str
58	    description: str
59	    weight: float = 1.0
60	
61	
62	class FaultPublic(BaseModel):
63	    kind: FaultKind | HarnessFaultKind
64	    origin: ErrorOrigin
65	    layer: ErrorLayer
66	    description: str
67	
68	
69	class HarnessFault(BaseModel):
70	    """A real interruption the harness inflicts on ITSELF at a chosen call (chaos trigger, real failure mode).
71	
72	    worker_crash    : after dispatching the matching tool call and waiting `after_ms`, the run_episode
73	                      process exits hard (os._exit) — the request is already at sandbox-env, so the
74	                      side effect completes; a fresh worker resumes the run from the persisted events.
75	    transport_abort : the in-flight HTTP request is cancelled client-side after `after_ms`; the server
76	                      still completes it; the agent gets ETRANSPORT with outcome unknown.
77	    """
78	
79	    kind: HarnessFaultKind
80	    tool: ToolName = "write_file"
81	    path: str
82	    nth: int = Field(default=1, ge=1, description="fire on the nth matching call")
83	    after_ms: int = Field(default=400, ge=0, description="delay after dispatch before interrupting (lets the request reach the server)")
84	
85	
86	# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- MCP tool I/O
87	
88	ToolName = Literal["run_command", "read_file", "write_file", "list_dir"]
89	ErrorCode = Literal[
90	    # OS/HTTP-style codes the AGENT sees (the same text whether injected or real):
91	    "ENOENT",      # no such file or directory
92	    "EACCES",      # permission denied
93	    "ETIMEDOUT",   # the response never arrived (ack lost); the operation MAY have completed
94	    "EINVAL",      # bad argument (path outside /workspace, bad mode, is-a-directory)
95	    # Real-failure codes (never injected; only the layer that really failed produces them):
96	    "ESANDBOX",    # the Modal Sandbox is gone/unavailable (terminated, not found, exec failed)
97	    "ETRANSPORT",  # harness <-> sandbox-env HTTP/MCP failure (connection reset, 5xx, client abort)
98	    "EHARNESS",    # the harness worker itself was interrupted while the call was in flight
99	    "EMODEL",      # Anthropic API failure that ended the turn
100	    "EGYM",        # gym control-plane (reset/observe/evaluate/delete) failure
101	    "EINTERNAL",   # unexpected exception inside sandbox-env (a bug; see detail)
102	    "ENOEPISODE",  # missing/unknown X-Faultline-Episode header
103	]
104	
105	# Provenance: WHO caused a failure. The agent never sees this; ledger, events and UI do.
106	ErrorOrigin = Literal[
107	    "injected",  # intercepted at the tool boundary by the fault plan; nothing real failed
108	    "staged",    # the scenario really changed the world at reset (e.g. deleted a file); the error is a real OS error
109	    "real",      # unplanned failure of a real component (sandbox, transport, harness, model, gym, filesystem)
110	]
111	ErrorLayer = Literal["filesystem", "sandbox", "transport", "harness", "model", "gym", "boundary"]
112	
113	
114	class ErrorClass(BaseModel):
115	    """Differentiated classification of one failure, for humans and the UI (never shown to the agent).
116	
117	    Fixed `label` vocabulary (apps/web renders these verbatim; see docs/error-taxonomy.md):
118	      injected/boundary  "simulated: missing file (file still on disk)"
119	                         "simulated: write denied (nothing written)"
120	                         "simulated: lost ack (write landed; response withheld)"
121	      staged/filesystem  "staged: file absent since reset"
122	      real/filesystem    "real: OS error in sandbox"
123	      real/sandbox       "real: sandbox terminated or unavailable"
124	      real/transport     "real: transport failure harness<->sandbox-env (outcome unknown)"
125	      real/harness       "real: harness worker interrupted mid-call (outcome unknown)"
126	      real/model         "real: model API error"
127	      real/gym           "real: gym control-plane failure"
128	      real/boundary      "real: internal error in sandbox-env"
129	    """
130	
131	    origin: ErrorOrigin
132	    layer: ErrorLayer
133	    code: ErrorCode
134	    kind: FaultKind | None = Field(default=None, description="injected/staged only: which fault kind")
135	    label: str
136	    outcome_known: bool = Field(description="False when the side effect may or may not have happened (ack lost, transport, harness interruption)")
137	    side_effect_applied: bool | None = Field(default=None, description="ground truth from the ledger when known: did the write/command actually run?")
138	    detail: str | None = None
139	
140	
141	STDIO_CAP = 8_000  # chars per stream in run_command
142	CONTENT_CAP = 32_000  # chars in read_file
143	
144	
145	class RunCommandInput(BaseModel):
146	    command: str = Field(description="bash -lc <command>, cwd=/workspace")
147	    timeout_s: int = Field(default=30, ge=1, le=60)
148	
149	
150	class RunCommandOutput(BaseModel):
151	    stdout: str
152	    stderr: str
153	    exit_code: int
154	    duration_ms: int
155	    truncated: bool = False
156	
157	
158	class ReadFileInput(BaseModel):
159	    path: str
160	
161

197	class ToolError(BaseModel):
198	    """Returned (as the tool result text, is_error=True) for both real and injected failures.
199	
200	    The agent cannot tell injected from real: same shape, same wording as the OS/HTTP layer would use.
201	    """

203	    error: str = Field(description="human-readable, OS/HTTP-style, e.g. 'cat: config/settings.json: No such file or directory'")
204	    code: ErrorCode
205	    path: str | None = None
206	    detail: str | None = None

218	    layer: ErrorLayer = Field(default="boundary", description="boundary for injected, filesystem for staged")
219	    description: str = Field(default="", description="one plain-language sentence for the UI, e.g. 'the write was applied but the acknowledgement was withheld'")

233	    duration_ms: int = 0
234	    origin: ErrorOrigin | None = Field(default=None, description="set when outcome != ok: injected | staged | real")
235	    error_code: ErrorCode | None = None
236	    interrupted: bool = Field(default=False, description="the harness reported (POST /episodes/{id}/interruptions) that it never received this call's response")

299	# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- harness runs & events
300	
301	ToolOutcome = Literal[
302	    "executed",      # the sandbox ran it and it succeeded
303	    "failed",        # the sandbox ran it and reported failure (non-zero exit, real ENOENT/EACCES)
304	    "not_executed",  # refused/short-circuited before the sandbox saw it (injected EACCES/ENOENT, EINVAL, ESANDBOX)
305	    "unknown",       # may or may not have run (ack lost, transport failure, harness interruption)
306	]
307	
308	EventType = Literal[
309	    "run.started",
310	    "episode.reset",
311	    "turn.text",
312	    "tool.call",
313	    "tool.result",
314	    "fault.fired",
315	    "workspace.diff",
316	    "episode.evaluated",
317	    "run.finished",
318	    "log",
319	    "llm.call",
320	    "turn.thinking",
321	    "interruption",
322	    "run.resumed",
323	    "episode.sandbox",
324	]
325	
326	
327	class Event(BaseModel):
328	    """Append-only, single-writer (run_episode). `id` is the 0-based sequence within the run and is
329	    what SSE uses as the event id / Last-Event-ID.
330	
331	    data shapes by type:
332	      run.started       {scenario_id, model, seed, max_steps, anthropic_workspace?: masked}
333	      episode.reset     {episode_id, files: [FileEntry], task_prompt, sandbox_id (short), attempt (1 = first provisioning)}
334	      turn.text         {text}
335	      tool.call         {tool, input, tool_use_id}
336	      tool.result       {tool_use_id, tool, output: str, is_error: bool, duration_ms, fault?: FaultFired,
337	                         outcome: executed|failed|not_executed|unknown, error_class?: ErrorClass (iff is_error),
338	                         error_code?: str (compat), attempts: int (harness retries of this call; 1 = none),
339	                         sandbox: {id: short, alive: bool}}
340	      fault.fired       FaultFired
341	      workspace.diff    {files: [FileEntry], diffs: [FileDiff]}
342	      episode.evaluated EvaluateResponse
343	      run.finished      {status, usage: {input_tokens, output_tokens}, duration_ms, error?: str,
344	                         evaluation_status: ok|failed|skipped, evaluation_error?: str, steps, score?,
345	                         error_class?: ErrorClass (present whenever status is unevaluated|interrupted|error)}
346	      log               {svc, lvl, ev, msg, ...}   (unified log line mirrored into the stream)
347	      llm.call          {attempt, model, stop_reason?, request_id?, usage: Usage-shaped {input_tokens, output_tokens, cache_read_input_tokens?, cache_creation_input_tokens?}, duration_ms, error?}
348	      turn.thinking     {text}   (summarised thinking, models that expose it)
349	      interruption      Interruption   (a REAL interruption: origin is always "real")
350	      run.resumed       {worker_generation, resumed_from_event_id, dangling_tool_use_id?, resumed_at}
351	      episode.sandbox   {sandbox_id, status: alive|terminated|replaced, reason, step}   (the environment noticed the worker changed)
352	    Every tool.result with is_error carries data.error_class: ErrorClass; every fault.fired carries origin.
353	    """
354	
355	    id: int
356	    ts: str
357	    run_id: str
358	    type: EventType
359	    step: int | None = None
360	    data: dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)
361	
362	
363	RunStatus = Literal[
364	    "queued",
365	    "running",
366	    "ok",           # agent submitted / ended, evaluation succeeded
367	    "truncated",    # step budget exhausted (still evaluated)
368	    "unevaluated",  # loop finished but evaluate() failed — NOT ok, NOT an agent error
369	    "interrupted",  # a real interruption ended the run without resume (sandbox gone, worker died, ...)
370	    "error",        # the harness could not run the episode (model auth, gym reset failure, bug)
371	]
372	
373	
374	class Interruption(BaseModel):
375	    """A real interruption observed by the harness (origin is always real)."""
376	
377	    step: int | None = None
378	    layer: ErrorLayer
379	    code: ErrorCode
380	    label: str
381	    tool_use_id: str | None = Field(default=None, description="the in-flight tool call, if any")
382	    tool: ToolName | None = None
383	    path: str | None = None
384	    outcome_known: bool = False
385	    planned: bool = Field(default=False, description="True when a HarnessFault in the scenario triggered it (still a real failure)")
386	    resumed: bool = Field(default=False, description="a new worker picked the run up afterwards")
387	    worker_generation: int = Field(default=1, description="which worker observed it")
388	    at: str
389	    detail: str | None = None
390	
391	MODEL_ALLOWLIST = ["claude-haiku-4-5", "claude-sonnet-5", "claude-opus-5"]
392	DEFAULT_MODEL = "claude-haiku-4-5"

437	    the next write to the same path (same rule as ack_lost)."""

1	"""Modal app `faultline-harness`: the browser-facing API + the model loop + the SQLite Store.
2	
3	    api           ASGI (FastAPI). NO secrets. Spawns runs, serves run state and the SSE tail.
4	                  https://appliedlabsai-local--faultline-harness-api.modal.run
5	    Store         @app.cls(max_containers=1, volumes={"/data": faultline-db}) — the ONLY process
6	                  that opens the database. Hot copy on /tmp, consistent snapshots on the Volume
7	                  (PLAN.md §2.9, ARCHITECTURE.md §4).
8	    run_episode   the ONLY function with the provider secret attached; runs one episode.
9	
10	Deploy:  modal deploy -e local services/agent-harness/modal_app.py
11	Smoke:   modal run -e local services/agent-harness/modal_app.py --scenario lost-ack
12	Backup:  modal run -e local services/agent-harness/modal_app.py::checkpoint_now
13	         modal volume get -e local faultline-db faultline.sqlite3 ./runs/
14	
15	NOTE on `modal serve`: the served app gets its OWN Store container with the SAME Volume mounted,
16	which would make two writers for one database file. Deploy (or point the served app at a different
17	volume) rather than serving this app while the deployed one is live.
18	
19	Deploy-time env (baked into the image so containers need no lookup):
20	    SANDBOX_ENV_URL             default https://appliedlabsai-local--faultline-sandbox-env-api.modal.run
21	    ANTHROPIC_MODEL             default claude-haiku-4-5
22	    LOG_LEVEL                   default info
23	    FAULTLINE_ANTHROPIC_SECRET  default anthropic-secret (the secret is attached to run_episode only)
24	"""
25	
26	from __future__ import annotations
27	
28	import os
29	import pathlib
30	import sys
31	from typing import Any
32	
33	# Make `faultline_common` (shared contracts + logging) and `harness` importable at DEPLOY time,
34	# without hardcoding an absolute path: this service directory and <repo>/packages/common.
35	# Inside a container this file lives at /root/modal_app.py, where neither sibling exists (both
36	# packages are baked in by add_local_python_source) — so every candidate is probed, never assumed.
37	_HERE = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent
38	_CANDIDATES = [_HERE]
39	if len(_HERE.parents) >= 2:
40	    _CANDIDATES.append(_HERE.parents[1] / "packages" / "common")
41	for _p in _CANDIDATES:
42	    if _p.is_dir() and str(_p) not in sys.path:
43	        sys.path.insert(0, str(_p))
44	
45	import modal  # noqa: E402

47	from harness import config  # noqa: E402

50	ANTHROPIC_MODEL = os.environ.get("ANTHROPIC_MODEL", config.DEFAULT_MODEL)

52	# Off by default; set HARNESS_HAIKU_THINKING=1 at deploy time to let Haiku 4.5 think.

83	app = modal.App(config.APP_NAME)

85	# v1 volume: v2 needs an explicit version= and the 1.5.5 CLI still flags it experimental; it buys
86	# nothing for a single file (PLAN.md §2.4).
87	DB_VOLUME = modal.Volume.from_name(config.DB_VOLUME, create_if_missing=True)

90	@app.cls(
91	    image=IMAGE,
92	    volumes={config.DB_MOUNT: DB_VOLUME},
93	    max_containers=1,   # single writer: the only process that ever opens the database
94	    min_containers=1,   # an SSE poll must not pay a cold start plus a restore
95	    timeout=120,
96	    scaledown_window=300,
97	)
98	@modal.concurrent(max_inputs=32)
99	class Store:
100	    """SQLite on the Modal Volume. Every method is a thin wrapper over harness.sqlite_store."""
101	
102	    @modal.enter()
103	    def enter(self) -> None:
104	        from harness.sqlite_store import SqliteStore
105	
106	        self._store = SqliteStore(
107	            config.HOT_DB_PATH,
108	            snapshot_path=config.SNAPSHOT_PATH,
109	            commit=DB_VOLUME.commit,
110	            reload=DB_VOLUME.reload,
111	            checkpoint_interval_s=config.CHECKPOINT_INTERVAL_S,
112	        ).open()
113	        self._store.start_autocheckpoint()
114	
115	    @modal.exit()
116	    def exit(self) -> None:
117	        try:
118	            self._store.checkpoint(force=True)
119	        finally:
120	            self._store.close()
121	
122	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ identity + conversations
123	    @modal.method()
124	    def upsert_user(self, user_id: str, user_agent: str | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
125	        return self._store.upsert_user(user_id, user_agent)
126	
127	    @modal.method()
128	    def list_conversations(self, user_id: str, limit: int = 50) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
129	        return self._store.list_conversations(user_id, limit)
130	
131	    @modal.method()
132	    def create_conversation(self, user_id: str, scenario_id: str, title: str | None = None,
133	                            user_agent: str | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
134	        return self._store.create_conversation(user_id, scenario_id, title, user_agent)
135	
136	    @modal.method()
137	    def get_conversation(self, conversation_id: str, user_id: str | None = None) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
138	        return self._store.get_conversation(conversation_id, user_id)
139	
140	    @modal.method()
141	    def update_conversation(self, conversation_id: str, user_id: str | None = None, *,
142	                            title: str | None = None,
143	                            archived: bool | None = None) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
144	        return self._store.update_conversation(conversation_id, user_id, title=title, archived=archived)
145	
146	    @modal.method()
147	    def delete_conversation(self, conversation_id: str,
148	                            user_id: str | None = None) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
149	        return self._store.delete_conversation(conversation_id, user_id)
150	
151	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ runs + events
152	    @modal.method()
153	    def create_run(self, record: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
154	        return self._store.create_run(record)
155	
156	    @modal.method()
157	    def append_events(self, run_id: str, events: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> dict[str, Any]:
158	        return self._store.append_events(run_id, events)
159	
160	    @modal.method()
161	    def finish_run(self, run_id: str, fields: dict[str, Any] | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
162	        return self._store.finish_run(run_id, fields)
163	
164	    @modal.method()
165	    def get_run(self, run_id: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
166	        return self._store.get_run(run_id)
167	
168	    @modal.method()
169	    def list_runs(self, limit: int = 50, user_id: str | None = None) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
170	        return self._store.list_runs(limit, user_id)
171	
172	    @modal.method()
173	    def events_after(self, run_id: str, after: int = -1, limit: int = 2000) -> dict[str, Any]:
174	        return self._store.events_after(run_id, after, limit)
175	
176	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ ops
177	    @modal.method()
178	    def checkpoint(self, force: bool = False) -> dict[str, Any]:
179	        return self._store.checkpoint(force)
180	
181	    @modal.method()
182	    def health(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
183	        return self._store.health()
184	
185	
186	def _store() -> Any:
187	    """RunStore wired to THIS app's Store class (no name lookup, no deploy-order dependency)."""
188	    from harness.store import ModalStoreClient, RunStore
189	
190	    return RunStore(ModalStoreClient(Store))
191	
192	
193	def _spawn(run_id: str, req: dict[str, Any]) -> str | None:
194	    """Hand the run to the secret-bearing function and return immediately (Modal caps requests at 150 s)."""
195	    call = run_episode.spawn(run_id, req)
196	    return getattr(call, "object_id", None)
