199	@app.function(image=IMAGE, min_containers=1, scaledown_window=300, timeout=300)
200	@modal.concurrent(max_inputs=50)
201	@modal.asgi_app()
202	def api():
203	    """FastAPI app. Deliberately has no secrets: /health must report has_provider_key=false."""
204	    from faultline_common.log import get_logger
205	
206	    from harness.api import create_app
207	
208	    log = get_logger(config.SVC)
209	    log.info(
210	        "api.boot",
211	        "asgi app starting",
212	        has_key=bool(os.environ.get("ANTHROPIC_API_KEY")),
213	        sandbox_env_url=config.sandbox_env_url(),
214	        model_default=config.model_default(),
215	    )
216	    return create_app(store=_store(), spawn=_spawn)
217	
218	
219	@app.function(
220	    image=IMAGE,
221	    # required_keys makes a misconfigured secret fail at deploy time, not mid-episode.
222	    secrets=[modal.Secret.from_name(SECRET_NAME, required_keys=["ANTHROPIC_API_KEY"])],
223	    timeout=900,
224	    # PLAN.md §2.11: this is the resume mechanism. Modal re-invokes the function with the SAME
225	    # (run_id, req) after the worker dies — which is what the `worker_crash` harness fault causes
226	    # on purpose, and what an OOM/preemption causes by accident. `run_episode_sync` notices the
227	    # run already has events, rebuilds the conversation and continues it; a run that already
228	    # reached a terminal status returns immediately, so a retry can never double-run an episode.
229	    # Constant backoff: the sandbox is still alive (idle_timeout 600 s) and waiting is pure loss.
230	    retries=modal.Retries(max_retries=config.RUN_RETRIES, initial_delay=config.RUN_RETRY_DELAY_S,
231	                          backoff_coefficient=1.0),
232	)
233	def run_episode(run_id: str, req: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
234	    """Run one episode. The only process in Faultline that sees ANTHROPIC_API_KEY."""
235	    from faultline_common.log import get_logger
236	
237	    from harness.loop import run_episode_sync
238	
239	    log = get_logger(config.SVC)
240	    log.info(
241	        "run_episode.start",
242	        "spawned",
243	        run_id=run_id,
244	        has_key=bool(os.environ.get("ANTHROPIC_API_KEY")),  # never the value
245	        anthropic_workspace=config.masked_workspace(),
246	        scenario_id=req.get("scenario_id"),
247	        model=req.get("model") or config.model_default(),
248	        sandbox_env_url=config.sandbox_env_url(),
249	    )
250	    record = run_episode_sync(run_id, req, store=_store())
251	    return {
252	        "run_id": record.get("run_id"),
253	        "status": record.get("status"),
254	        "episode_id": record.get("episode_id"),
255	        "steps": record.get("steps"),
256	        "score": (record.get("evaluation") or {}).get("score"),
257	        "events": len(record.get("events") or []),
258	        "error": record.get("error"),
259	        "error_class": (record.get("error_class") or {}).get("code"),
260	        "worker_generation": record.get("worker_generation"),
261	        "interruptions": len(record.get("interruptions") or []),
262	    }
263	
264	
265	def _deployed_store() -> Any:
266	    """The DEPLOYED Store, resolved by name.
267	
268	    Local entrypoints must never touch the in-module `Store`: `modal run` builds an ephemeral app,
269	    so that would start a SECOND container with the same Volume mounted — two writers for one
270	    SQLite file. By name, every caller lands on the one deployed container.
271	    """
272	    return modal.Cls.from_name(
273	        config.APP_NAME, "Store", environment_name=config.modal_environment()
274	    )()
275	
276	
277	@app.local_entrypoint()
278	def run(scenario: str = "lost-ack", model: str = "", max_steps: int = 0, seed: int = -1,
279	        user: str = "") -> None:
280	    """Spawn one episode on the DEPLOYED harness and tail its events from the Store.
281	
282	    Dev smoke test, no web app needed. `--user u_<uuid4>` scopes the run to a browser identity, so
283	    `GET /conversations` with the same header lists it afterwards.
284	    """
285	    import json
286	    import time
287	    import uuid
288	
289	    run_id = f"r_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}"
290	    req: dict[str, Any] = {"scenario_id": scenario}
291	    if model:
292	        req["model"] = model
293	    if max_steps:
294	        req["max_steps"] = max_steps
295	    if seed >= 0:
296	        req["seed"] = seed
297	    if user:
298	        req["user_id"] = user
299	
300	    store = _deployed_store()
301	    fn = modal.Function.from_name(config.APP_NAME, "run_episode",
302	                                  environment_name=config.modal_environment())
303	    print(json.dumps({"ev": "cli.spawn", "run_id": run_id, "req": req}))
304	    call = fn.spawn(run_id, req)
305	
306	    seen = -1
307	    deadline = time.time() + 900
308	    status = None
309	    while time.time() < deadline:
310	        page = store.events_after.remote(run_id, seen) or {}
311	        for event in page.get("events") or []:
312	            data = event.get("data") or {}
313	            label = data.get("summary") or data.get("msg") or data.get("status") or ""
314	            print(f"[{event.get('id'):>3}] step={event.get('step')} {event.get('type'):<18} {str(label)[:110]}")
315	            seen = max(seen, int(event.get("id", seen)))
316	        status = page.get("status")
317	        if status in {"ok", "error", "truncated", "unevaluated", "interrupted"}:
318	            record = store.get_run.remote(run_id) or {}
319	            print(json.dumps({"ev": "cli.finished", "status": status,
320	                              "score": (record.get("evaluation") or {}).get("score"),
321	                              "conversation_id": record.get("conversation_id"),
322	                              "error": record.get("error")}))
323	            break
324	        time.sleep(1.0)
325	    else:
326	        print(json.dumps({"ev": "cli.timeout", "run_id": run_id}))
327	
328	    print(json.dumps({"ev": "cli.result", "result": call.get(timeout=60)}, default=str))
329	
330	
331	@app.local_entrypoint()
332	def import_legacy_runs(dict_name: str = "faultline-runs", apply: bool = False,
333	                       chunk: int = 50) -> None:
334	    """One-off: copy runs out of the old Modal Dict into the Store, so pre-Store `?run=<id>` links
335	    (the demo runs the evidence and the web app reference) keep resolving.
336	
337	    Idempotent: `create_run` is INSERT OR IGNORE and `append_events` is keyed on (run_id, seq), so
338	    re-running changes nothing. Default is a dry run; pass --apply to write.
339	    """
340	    import json
341	
342	    import modal as _modal
343	
344	    src = _modal.Dict.from_name(dict_name, environment_name=config.modal_environment(),
345	                                create_if_missing=False)
346	    store = _deployed_store()
347	    index = list(src.get("__index__", []) or [])
348	    moved, skipped = 0, 0
349	    for run_id in index:
350	        record = src.get(run_id) or {}
351	        if not isinstance(record, dict) or not record.get("run_id"):
352	            skipped += 1
353	            continue
354	        events = list(record.get("events") or [])
355	        line = {"run_id": run_id, "scenario": record.get("scenario_id"),
356	                "status": record.get("status"), "events": len(events)}
357	        if not apply:
358	            print(json.dumps({"ev": "import.dry_run", **line}))
359	            moved += 1
360	            continue
361	        store.create_run.remote({k: v for k, v in record.items() if k != "events"})
362	        appended = 0
363	        for i in range(0, len(events), chunk):
364	            appended += int((store.append_events.remote(run_id, events[i:i + chunk]) or {}).get("appended") or 0)
365	        store.finish_run.remote(run_id, {k: record[k] for k in
366	                                         ("status", "finished_at", "error", "steps", "summary",
367	                                          "episode_id", "evaluation", "usage", "task_prompt")
368	                                         if record.get(k) is not None})
369	        print(json.dumps({"ev": "import.moved", **line, "appended": appended}))
370	        moved += 1
371	    print(json.dumps({"ev": "import.done", "runs": moved, "skipped": skipped, "applied": apply,
372	                      "store": store.health.remote()}, default=str))
373	
374	
375	@app.local_entrypoint()
376	def prune_empty_conversations(user: str = "", apply: bool = False) -> None:
377	    """Archive conversations that have no runs (maintenance; soft, nothing is hard-deleted)."""
378	    import json
379	
380	    from harness.sqlite_store import ANON_USER_ID
381	
382	    store = _deployed_store()
383	    owner = user or ANON_USER_ID
384	    empty = [c for c in (store.list_conversations.remote(owner, 500) or []) if not c.get("last_run")]
385	    for conv in empty:
386	        if apply:
387	            store.delete_conversation.remote(conv["id"], owner)
388	        print(json.dumps({"ev": "prune.archived" if apply else "prune.dry_run",
389	                          "conversation_id": conv["id"], "title": conv.get("title")}))
390	    print(json.dumps({"ev": "prune.done", "user": owner, "archived": len(empty), "applied": apply,
391	                      "store": store.health.remote()}, default=str))
392

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""One real round trip against a deployed (or local) sandbox-env.

    services/sandbox-env/.venv/bin/python scripts/smoke_roundtrip.py
    ... --scenario missing-config --fault-proof
    ... --base http://127.0.0.1:8000 --out runs/local_smoke

What it does, in order:

    GET  /health                      the secret boundary: has_provider_key must be false
    GET  /scenarios                   public catalogue
    POST /episodes                    reset -> a real Modal Sandbox with the fixture in it
    MCP  list_tools                   over streamable HTTP, episode via X-Faultline-Episode
    MCP  run_command                  `echo hello from $(hostname) && ls -la && python -m pytest -q`
    MCP  read_file README.md
    MCP  write_file scratch/note.txt
    GET  /episodes/{id}               observe: the new file shows up as `added`, with a diff
    POST /episodes/{id}/evaluate      hidden tests + recovery checks + score
    DELETE /episodes/{id}             terminate the sandbox (always, even on failure)

Every response is written to the output directory as JSON, plus a `summary.json` with one
pass/fail row per assertion, and the last line on stdout is `SMOKE PASS` / `SMOKE FAIL`.

`--fault-proof` additionally proves the fault boundary on `missing-config`: `read_file README.md`
must come back ENOENT the first time and with real content the second, from the same sandbox.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import asyncio
import json
import sys
import time
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

REPO_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]

    if _p not in sys.path:
        sys.path.insert(0, _p)

from fastmcp import Client  # noqa: E402
from fastmcp.client.transports import StreamableHttpTransport  # noqa: E402

from faultline_common.log import get_logger  # noqa: E402

NOTE_PATH = "scratch/note.txt"
NOTE_BODY = "faultline smoke test\n"

#: scenarios whose fault plan hides README.md on the first read (see sandbox_env/scenarios/)
TRANSIENT_README_SCENARIOS = ("missing-config",)
#: scenarios that delete config/settings.json at reset (sticky missing_file)
STICKY_CONFIG_SCENARIOS = ("missing-config", "gauntlet")


def utc_stamp() -> str:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ")


class Smoke:
    def __init__(self, base: str, scenario: str, out: Path, fault_proof: bool):

        self.scenario = scenario
        self.out = out
        self.fault_proof = fault_proof

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""V3 fault proofs (PLAN.md §4) — scripted, **no-model** episodes against a live sandbox-env.

    services/sandbox-env/.venv/bin/python scripts/fault_proofs.py
    ... --only lost-ack_careless --base http://127.0.0.1:8000 --out runs/local_faults

One episode per case, driven entirely from this script through the real MCP streamable-HTTP tool
surface. No Claude, no harness: every tool call here is chosen by the script, so whatever the
evidence shows is a property of the *environment*, not of a model that happened to behave well.

What each case proves
---------------------
`missing-config_careful`  missing_file, both modes.
    transient: `read_file README.md` -> ENOENT, the *next* identical read -> the real file, and a
    shell `sha256sum README.md` in the same sandbox exits 0 the whole time — the file never left
    the disk, so the fault provably lives at the tool boundary and not in the filesystem. Listings
    hide the name while the fault is pending (and do not consume its single hit).
    sticky: `config/settings.json` is simply gone from the reset tree; the ENOENT is real.
    Then a careful recovery (recreate the file from the README's documented schema) + evaluate.

`locked-file_careful`     denied_write.
    Two `write_file` calls come back EACCES, and after each one `GET /episodes/{id}` shows
    `src/ratelimiter/limits.py` with status `unchanged` and the *baseline* sha — nothing executed.
    The third write lands; the fix is then verified by re-reading and re-running pytest.

`lost-ack_careful`        ack_lost, recovered well.
    The write is held for ~`delay_ms` and then fails with ETIMEDOUT, but observe shows CHANGELOG.md
    `modified` against the baseline — the write *did* land. The careful path reads the file back,
    sees the section already present, and does not write it again.

`lost-ack_careless`       ack_lost, recovered badly (the control).
    Identical up to the lost ack, then a blind second append with no verification. Same fault, same
    environment, and the grader must separate them: `verified_before_rewrite` and
    `no_duplicate_entry` both fail and the hidden release test fails.

`gauntlet_careful`        all three faults in one episode.

`secret-boundary`         V6, sandbox half: `env | grep -i anthropic` finds nothing, and both curl
    and a raw python socket fail to reach the public internet (`block_network=True`).

Exit code 0 iff every case's assertions hold. Evidence (one JSON per case, a combined trace and a
summary) is written to `runs/<UTC timestamp>_faults/`.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import asyncio
import json
import re
import sys
import time
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Callable

REPO_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]

1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
2	"""Live proof of failure provenance and real interruptions (PLAN.md §2.11), against the DEPLOYED services.
3	
4	The agent only ever sees OS/HTTP-style codes. Everything downstream — the ledger, the run events,
5	the run status, the UI — has to be able to say who caused a failure (`origin`) and which layer
6	really failed (`layer`). These cases prove that live, with real Modal Sandboxes and a real model:
7	
8	  worker-crash   (a) the harness worker REALLY dies (os._exit(137)) with the first CHANGELOG write
9	                 in flight; Modal re-invokes run_episode; a fresh worker resumes from the persisted
10	                 events, tells the gym, and hands the agent an EHARNESS result with an UNKNOWN
11	                 outcome. Asserted from run.json: the `interruption` (harness/EHARNESS, planned,
12	                 resumed), `run.resumed` with worker_generation 2, the synthetic result
13	                 (outcome unknown, error_class.origin real), THE MODEL'S NEXT CALL TOUCHING
14	                 CHANGELOG.md IS A READ (decided here, not by the grader), an evaluation, the
15	                 ledger row for that write marked `interrupted: true`, and a status of ok/truncated.
16	                 The read-back content is saved and its `## [0.2.0]` headings counted.
17	  lost-ack       (a, comparison) the same ambiguity, INJECTED: ETIMEDOUT must read
18	                 origin `injected`, layer `boundary`, outcome unknown, side_effect_applied true
19	                 once the ledger resolves it. Same agent experience, different provenance.
20	  sandbox-loss   (b) a real sandbox loss WITHOUT `reap`: after `episode.reset` this script calls
21	                 DELETE /episodes/{id} on sandbox-env, which terminates ONLY this run's sandbox.
22	                 The run must end `interrupted` with error_class real/sandbox/ESANDBOX, emit
23	                 `episode.sandbox {terminated}`, skip grading, and stop within 1 step of the first
24	                 ESANDBOX instead of letting the model flail into a corpse.
25	  conformance    (c) every event of the three runs validated against
26	                 faultline_common.schemas.Event + the documented data shapes.
27	  specimen       GET /runs/r_ccda8780cbee — the pre-taxonomy specimen is served untouched but its
28	                 record still answers the taxonomy's questions.
29	  transport-abort (opt-in, --case transport-abort) the client cancels an in-flight request the
30	                 server completes: real/transport/ETRANSPORT, outcome unknown, never retried.
31	
32	    services/agent-harness/.venv/bin/python scripts/prove_interruptions.py
33	    services/agent-harness/.venv/bin/python scripts/prove_interruptions.py --case sandbox-loss
34	
35	Evidence: runs/<UTC ts>_interruptions/{summary.json, <case>/…, <case>.json, conformance.json,
36	worker-crash_readback.txt}. Prints PROVE PASS / PROVE FAIL; exit code 0 iff every check passed.
37	
38	Nothing here calls `reap` (that kills other people's live sandboxes): the only sandbox this script
39	terminates is the one belonging to the episode of the run it just started, by DELETEing that episode.
40	"""

91	def utc_stamp() -> str:
92	    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ")

95	# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- assertions
96	
97	
98	class Checks:
99	    """A named list of assertions; nothing throws, everything is recorded."""
100	
101	    def __init__(self, case: str):
102	        self.case = case
103	        self.rows: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
104	
105	    def check(self, name: str, ok: bool, detail: Any = "") -> bool:
106	        self.rows.append({"name": name, "ok": bool(ok), "detail": str(detail)[:400]})
107	        print(f"  [{'PASS' if ok else 'FAIL'}] {name}" + (f"  {str(detail)[:140]}" if detail else ""),
108	              flush=True)
109	        return bool(ok)
110	
111	    @property
112	    def passed(self) -> int:
113	        return sum(1 for r in self.rows if r["ok"])
114	
115	    @property
116	    def ok(self) -> bool:
117	        return all(r["ok"] for r in self.rows) and bool(self.rows)
118	
119	
120	# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- event helpers
121	
122	
123	def events_of(record: dict[str, Any], type_: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
124	    return [e for e in record.get("events") or [] if e.get("type") == type_]
125	
126	
127	def data_of(event: dict[str, Any] | None) -> dict[str, Any]:
128	    return (event or {}).get("data") or {}
129	
130	
131	def finished_data(record: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
132	    got = events_of(record, "run.finished")
133	    return data_of(got[-1]) if got else {}
134	
135	
136	def ledger_resolutions(record: dict[str, Any]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
137	    """The harness's append-only echo of the gym ledger (`log` event, ev=ledger.resolution)."""
138	    for event in record.get("events") or []:
139	        if event.get("type") == "log" and data_of(event).get("ev") == "ledger.resolution":
140	            return list(data_of(event).get("resolutions") or [])
141	    return []
142	
143	
144	def evaluation_ledger(record: dict[str, Any]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
145	    return list(((record.get("evaluation") or {}).get("ledger")) or [])
146	
147	
148	def result_for(record: dict[str, Any], tool_use_id: str | None) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
149	    if not tool_use_id:
150	        return None
151	    for event in events_of(record, "tool.result"):
152	        if data_of(event).get("tool_use_id") == tool_use_id:
153	            return event
154	    return None
155	
156	
157	def tool_output_text(result_event: dict[str, Any] | None) -> str:
158	    """The text the model was handed: read_file `content`, run_command `stdout`, else the raw body."""
159	    raw = data_of(result_event).get("output") or ""
160	    try:
161	        payload = json.loads(raw)
162	    except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError):
163	        return str(raw)
164	    if isinstance(payload, dict):
165	        for key in ("content", "stdout"):
166	            if isinstance(payload.get(key), str):
167	                return payload[key]
168	    return str(raw)
169	
170	
171	# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- GRADING.md mirrors
172	
173	
174	def normalize_token(token: str) -> str:
175	    t = token.strip().strip("'\"")
176	    t = t.removeprefix("/workspace/").removeprefix("./")
177	    return t.strip("/")
178	
179	
180	def touches(event_data: dict[str, Any], path: str) -> bool:
181	    tool = str(event_data.get("tool") or "")
182	    args = event_data.get("input") or {}
183	    target = normalize_token(path)
184	    if tool in ("read_file", "write_file", "list_dir"):
185	        return normalize_token(str(args.get("path") or "")) == target
186	    if tool == "run_command":
187	        command = str(args.get("command") or "")
188	        try:
189	            tokens = shlex.split(command)
190	        except ValueError:
191	            tokens = command.split()
192	        return any(normalize_token(tok) == target for tok in tokens)
193	    return False
194	
195	
196	def is_mutating(event_data: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
197	    tool = str(event_data.get("tool") or "")
198	    if tool == "write_file":
199	        return True
200	    if tool != "run_command":
201	        return False
202	    command = str((event_data.get("input") or {}).get("command") or "")
203	    return bool(MUTATING_RE.search(command)) or bool(PY_WRITE_RE.search(command))
204	
205	
206	# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- run driving
207	
208	
209	def run_via_cli(scenario: str, out_dir: pathlib.Path, harness: str, log_path: pathlib.Path,
210	                extra: list[str] | None = None) -> tuple[int, dict[str, Any]]:
211	    """Drive a live episode with scripts/run_episode_cli.py (the real CLI, default model).
212	
213	    Returns (exit code, run.json). The CLI writes {events.jsonl,run.json,evaluate.json,summary.txt}.
214	    """
215	    cmd = [sys.executable, str(REPO_ROOT / "scripts" / "run_episode_cli.py"),
216	           "--scenario", scenario, "--harness", harness, "--out", str(out_dir),
217	           "--timeout", "600", *(extra or [])]
218	    print(f"  $ {' '.join(cmd[1:])}", flush=True)
219	    proc = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=900)
220	    log_path.write_text(proc.stdout + ("\n--- stderr ---\n" + proc.stderr if proc.stderr else ""))
221	    for line in proc.stdout.splitlines():
222	        print("    | " + line, flush=True)
223	    record_path = out_dir / "run.json"
224	    record = json.loads(record_path.read_text()) if record_path.exists() else {}
225	    return proc.returncode, record
226	
227	
228	def start_run(client: httpx.Client, harness: str, scenario: str, **body: Any) -> str:
229	    resp = client.post(f"{harness}/runs", json={"scenario_id": scenario, **body}, timeout=60.0)
230	    resp.raise_for_status()
231	    return resp.json()["run_id"]
232	
233	
234	def poll(client: httpx.Client, harness: str, run_id: str, *, timeout: float = 600.0,
235	         on_event: Callable[[dict[str, Any], dict[str, Any]], None] | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
236	    """Poll GET /runs/{id} until the status is terminal, calling `on_event` for every new event."""
237	    deadline = time.time() + timeout
238	    seen = -1
239	    record: dict[str, Any] = {}
240	    while time.time() < deadline:
241	        record = client.get(f"{harness}/runs/{run_id}", timeout=60.0).json()
242	        for event in record.get("events") or []:
243	            if int(event.get("id", -1)) <= seen:
244	                continue
245	            seen = int(event["id"])
246	            if on_event is not None:
247	                on_event(event, record)
248	        if record.get("status") in TERMINAL:
249	            return record
250	        time.sleep(1.0)
251	    return record
252	
253	
254	def save_case(out: pathlib.Path, case: str, record: dict[str, Any], checks: Checks) -> None:
255	    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
256	    (out / f"{case}.json").write_text(json.dumps(
257	        {"case": case, "run_id": record.get("run_id"), "status": record.get("status"),
258	         "scenario_id": record.get("scenario_id"), "model": record.get("model"),
259	         "error_class": record.get("error_class"), "interruptions": record.get("interruptions"),
260	         "worker_generation": record.get("worker_generation"),
261	         "score": (record.get("evaluation") or {}).get("score"),
262	         "checks_graded": [{"id": c.get("id"), "ok": c.get("ok"), "detail": c.get("detail")}
263	                           for c in (record.get("evaluation") or {}).get("checks") or []],
264	         "run_finished": finished_data(record),
265	         "ledger_resolution": ledger_resolutions(record),
266	         "ledger": evaluation_ledger(record),
267	         "assertions": checks.rows}, indent=2, default=str))
268	    if not (out / case / "events.jsonl").exists():
269	        with (out / f"{case}_events.jsonl").open("w") as fh:
270	            for event in record.get("events") or []:
271	                fh.write(json.dumps(event, default=str) + "\n")
272	
273	
274	# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- (a) worker-crash
275	
276	
277	def case_worker_crash(client: httpx.Client, harness: str, out: pathlib.Path,
278	                      records: dict[str, dict[str, Any]]) -> Checks:
279	    c = Checks("worker-crash")
280	    code, record = run_via_cli("worker-crash", out / "worker-crash", harness,
281	                               out / "worker-crash_cli.log")
282	    records["worker-crash"] = record
283	    if not record:
284	        c.check("the CLI produced a run record", False, f"exit={code}")
285	        return c
286	    print(f"  run {record.get('run_id')}", flush=True)
287	
288	    # --- the interruption itself -------------------------------------------------
289	    intrs = [data_of(e) for e in events_of(record, "interruption")]
290	    harness_intrs = [i for i in intrs
291	                     if i.get("layer") == "harness" and i.get("code") == "EHARNESS"
292	                     and i.get("planned") is True and i.get("resumed") is True]
293	    c.check("an `interruption` event: layer harness, code EHARNESS, planned, resumed",
294	            len(harness_intrs) == 1, harness_intrs[0] if harness_intrs else intrs)
295	    c.check("...labelled with the taxonomy's exact string",
296	            bool(harness_intrs) and harness_intrs[0].get("label") == LABEL_HARNESS,
297	            harness_intrs[0].get("label") if harness_intrs else "")
298	    c.check("...and outcome_known is false (the write may or may not have landed)",
299	            bool(harness_intrs) and harness_intrs[0].get("outcome_known") is False,
300	            harness_intrs[0].get("outcome_known") if harness_intrs else "")
301	
302	    resumed = [data_of(e) for e in events_of(record, "run.resumed")]
303	    c.check("a `run.resumed` event with worker_generation 2",
304	            len(resumed) == 1 and resumed[0].get("worker_generation") == 2, resumed)
305	    c.check("the run record agrees it is on its second worker",
306	            record.get("worker_generation") == 2, record.get("worker_generation"))
307	
308	    # --- the synthetic result the resuming worker handed the agent ---------------
309	    dangling = resumed[0].get("dangling_tool_use_id") if resumed else None
310	    synthetic = data_of(result_for(record, dangling))
311	    ec = synthetic.get("error_class") or {}
312	    c.check("the synthetic tool.result has outcome `unknown` and error_class.origin `real`",
313	            synthetic.get("outcome") == "unknown" and ec.get("origin") == "real",
314	            {"outcome": synthetic.get("outcome"), "error_class": ec})
315	    c.check("...real/harness/EHARNESS, outcome_known false",
316	            (ec.get("layer"), ec.get("code")) == ("harness", "EHARNESS")
317	            and ec.get("outcome_known") is False, ec)
318	    c.check("...and the agent was told to verify before retrying",
319	            "verify before retrying" in str(synthetic.get("output")), "")
320	
321	    # --- what the MODEL did next (decided here, not by the grader) ---------------
322	    synthetic_event = result_for(record, dangling)
323	    after_id = int(synthetic_event["id"]) if synthetic_event else -1
324	    later_calls = [e for e in events_of(record, "tool.call") if int(e.get("id", -1)) > after_id]
325	    touching = [e for e in later_calls if touches(data_of(e), CHANGELOG)]
326	    first = data_of(touching[0]) if touching else {}
327	    c.check("the model touched CHANGELOG.md again after the interruption", bool(touching),
328	            f"{len(later_calls)} later calls, {len(touching)} touching {CHANGELOG}")
329	    c.check("...and its FIRST such call is a READ, not another write "
330	            "(checked from the event stream, independently of the grader)",
331	            bool(touching) and not is_mutating(first),
332	            {"tool": first.get("tool"), "input": first.get("input")})
333	
334	    # --- the read-back content, saved and counted --------------------------------
335	    readback_path = out / "worker-crash_readback.txt"
336	    headings = -1
337	    if touching:
338	        result = result_for(record, data_of(touching[0]).get("tool_use_id"))
339	        content = tool_output_text(result)
340	        headings = len(HEADING_RE.findall(content))
341	        readback_path.write_text(
342	            f"# what the model read back from {CHANGELOG} after the harness worker died\n"
343	            f"# run {record.get('run_id')}  call {data_of(touching[0]).get('tool')} "
344	            f"{json.dumps(data_of(touching[0]).get('input'))}\n"
345	            f"# '## [0.2.0]' headings in this read-back: {headings}\n\n{content}\n")
346	        c.check(f"the read-back was saved ({readback_path.name})", bool(content.strip()),
347	                f"{len(content)} chars")
348	        c.check("the interrupted write landed EXACTLY once: one '## [0.2.0]' heading in the read-back",
349	                headings == 1, f"headings={headings}")
350	
351	    # --- the gym's side: ledger + grading ----------------------------------------
352	    ledger = evaluation_ledger(record)
353	    interrupted_rows = [r for r in ledger if r.get("interrupted") is True]
354	    changelog_rows = [r for r in interrupted_rows
355	                      if normalize_token(str(r.get("path") or "")) == CHANGELOG
356	                      or (r.get("command") and CHANGELOG in str(r.get("command")))]
357	    c.check("the ledger row for that write is marked `interrupted: true`", bool(changelog_rows),
358	            interrupted_rows or ledger[:3])
359	    c.check("...and the ledger knows whether the side effect really happened",
360	            bool(changelog_rows) and changelog_rows[0].get("outcome") in ("ok", "error", "ack_lost"),
361	            {k: changelog_rows[0].get(k) for k in ("outcome", "origin", "error_code")}
362	            if changelog_rows else "")
363	    resolutions = [r for r in ledger_resolutions(record) if r.get("tool_use_id") == dangling]
364	    c.check("the harness echoed that resolution into the event stream",
365	            bool(resolutions) and resolutions[0].get("side_effect_applied") is not None,
366	            resolutions)
367	
368	    evaluation = record.get("evaluation") or {}
369	    c.check("an evaluation is present with a score",
370	            bool(evaluation) and isinstance(evaluation.get("score"), (int, float)),
371	            {"score": evaluation.get("score"), "passed": evaluation.get("passed")})
372	    c.check("the run ends `ok` or `truncated` — never `error` — despite a REAL worker death",
373	            record.get("status") in GRADED, record.get("status"))
374	    graded = {ck.get("id"): ck.get("ok") for ck in evaluation.get("checks") or []}
375	    c.check("`verified_before_rewrite` was graded from the interrupted row (no injected fault exists here)",
376	            "verified_before_rewrite" in graded, graded)
377	    save_case(out, "worker-crash", record, c)
378	    return c
379	
380	
381	# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- (a) lost-ack
382	
383	
384	def case_lost_ack(client: httpx.Client, harness: str, out: pathlib.Path,
385	                  records: dict[str, dict[str, Any]]) -> Checks:
386	    """The same ambiguity, injected: the comparison that makes `origin` mean something."""
387	    c = Checks("lost-ack")
388	    code, record = run_via_cli("lost-ack", out / "lost-ack", harness, out / "lost-ack_cli.log")
389	    records["lost-ack"] = record
390	    if not record:
391	        c.check("the CLI produced a run record", False, f"exit={code}")
392	        return c
393	    print(f"  run {record.get('run_id')}", flush=True)
394	
395	    timeouts = [data_of(e) for e in events_of(record, "tool.result")
396	                if (data_of(e).get("error_class") or {}).get("code") == "ETIMEDOUT"]
397	    c.check("the agent got an ETIMEDOUT (the same thing the crash looked like)", bool(timeouts),
398	            f"{len(timeouts)} ETIMEDOUT results")
399	    ec = (timeouts[0].get("error_class") or {}) if timeouts else {}
400	    c.check("...but its origin is `injected` and the layer is `boundary` (nothing real failed)",
401	            (ec.get("origin"), ec.get("layer")) == ("injected", "boundary"), ec)
402	    c.check("...outcome `unknown`, outcome_known false",
403	            bool(timeouts) and timeouts[0].get("outcome") == "unknown"
404	            and ec.get("outcome_known") is False,
405	            {"outcome": timeouts[0].get("outcome") if timeouts else None})
406	    c.check("...side_effect_applied true — the write HAD landed", ec.get("side_effect_applied") is True,
407	            ec.get("side_effect_applied"))
408	    c.check("...with the taxonomy's exact label", ec.get("label") == LABEL_ACK_LOST, ec.get("label"))
409	    c.check("...and it was never labelled a REAL interruption",
410	            not events_of(record, "interruption") and record.get("worker_generation") == 1,
411	            f"interruptions={len(events_of(record, 'interruption'))}")
412	
413	    tuid = timeouts[0].get("tool_use_id") if timeouts else None
414	    resolutions = [r for r in ledger_resolutions(record) if r.get("tool_use_id") == tuid]
415	    c.check("the ledger resolution confirms side_effect_applied: true for that call",
416	            bool(resolutions) and resolutions[0].get("side_effect_applied") is True, resolutions)
417	    ack_rows = [r for r in evaluation_ledger(record) if r.get("outcome") == "ack_lost"]
418	    c.check("...and the gym's own row says outcome `ack_lost`, origin `injected`",
419	            bool(ack_rows) and ack_rows[0].get("origin") == "injected",
420	            {k: ack_rows[0].get(k) for k in ("tool", "path", "outcome", "origin", "error_code")}
421	            if ack_rows else "")
422	
423	    fired = [data_of(e) for e in events_of(record, "fault.fired")]
424	    c.check("every fault.fired carries origin/layer/description",
425	            bool(fired) and all(f.get("origin") and f.get("layer") and f.get("description")
426	                                for f in fired), fired[:2])
427	    c.check("an injected fault still ends `ok` WITH a score",
428	            record.get("status") == "ok"
429	            and finished_data(record).get("evaluation_status") == "ok"
430	            and (record.get("evaluation") or {}).get("score") is not None,
431	            f"status={record.get('status')} score={(record.get('evaluation') or {}).get('score')}")
432	    save_case(out, "lost-ack", record, c)
433	    return c
434	
435	
436	# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- (b) sandbox loss
437	
438	
439	def case_sandbox_loss(client: httpx.Client, harness: str, sandbox_env: str, out: pathlib.Path,
440	                      records: dict[str, dict[str, Any]]) -> Checks:
441	    """A REAL sandbox loss without `reap`: DELETE our own episode mid-run.
442	
443	    `reap` terminates every faultline sandbox, including other people's live runs. DELETE
444	    /episodes/{id} terminates exactly one sandbox — the one belonging to the episode this run just
445	    created — which is the same failure that killed run r_ccda8780cbee, scoped to us.
446	    """
447	    c = Checks("sandbox-loss")
448	    run_id = start_run(client, harness, "lost-ack")
449	    print(f"  run {run_id}", flush=True)
450	    state: dict[str, Any] = {"episode_id": None, "deleted": None, "tool_results": 0,
451	                             "reset_at": None, "delete_status": None}
452	
453	    def on_event(event: dict[str, Any], record: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
454	        if event["type"] == "episode.reset":
455	            state["episode_id"] = data_of(event).get("episode_id")
456	            state["reset_at"] = time.time()
457	        if event["type"] == "tool.result":
458	            state["tool_results"] += 1
459	        if not state["episode_id"] or state["deleted"]:
460	            return
461	        # After episode.reset, as soon as the agent is really working (or 25 s later, whatever
462	        # comes first) kill OUR sandbox: one DELETE, one episode, nobody else's run.
463	        ready = state["tool_results"] >= 1 or (time.time() - (state["reset_at"] or 0)) > 25
464	        if not ready:
465	            return
466	        resp = httpx.delete(f"{sandbox_env}/episodes/{state['episode_id']}", timeout=120.0)
467	        state["deleted"] = state["episode_id"]
468	        state["delete_status"] = resp.status_code
469	        state["deleted_after_results"] = state["tool_results"]
470	        print(f"  DELETE /episodes/{state['episode_id']} -> {resp.status_code} "
471	              f"(after {state['tool_results']} tool results)", flush=True)
472	
473	    record = poll(client, harness, run_id, on_event=on_event)
474	    records["sandbox-loss"] = record
475	    (out / "sandbox-loss").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
476	    (out / "sandbox-loss" / "run.json").write_text(json.dumps(record, indent=2, default=str))
477	    with (out / "sandbox-loss" / "events.jsonl").open("w") as fh:
478	        for event in record.get("events") or []:
479	            fh.write(json.dumps(event, default=str) + "\n")
480	
481	    c.check("we terminated exactly one sandbox — our own episode's — with DELETE /episodes/{id}",
482	            state["delete_status"] == 200,
483	            f"episode={state['deleted']} http={state['delete_status']} "
484	            f"after {state.get('deleted_after_results')} tool results")
485	    c.check("the run ends `interrupted`, not `ok`", record.get("status") == "interrupted",
486	            record.get("status"))
487	    ec = record.get("error_class") or {}
488	    c.check("error_class is real/sandbox/ESANDBOX",
489	            (ec.get("origin"), ec.get("layer"), ec.get("code")) == ("real", "sandbox", "ESANDBOX"), ec)
490	    c.check("...with the taxonomy's exact label", ec.get("label") == LABEL_SANDBOX, ec.get("label"))
491	    sandbox_events = [data_of(e) for e in events_of(record, "episode.sandbox")]
492	    c.check("an `episode.sandbox {status: terminated}` event was emitted",
493	            any(s.get("status") == "terminated" for s in sandbox_events), sandbox_events)
494	    fin = finished_data(record)
495	    c.check("evaluation_status is `skipped` (grading a half-run would be a lie)",
496	            fin.get("evaluation_status") == "skipped", fin.get("evaluation_status"))
497	    c.check("no score is claimed", (record.get("evaluation") or {}).get("score") is None,
498	            (record.get("evaluation") or {}).get("score"))
499	    c.check("run.finished explains the ending without a second fetch",
500	            (fin.get("error_class") or {}).get("code") == "ESANDBOX", fin.get("error_class"))
501	    intrs = [data_of(e) for e in events_of(record, "interruption")]
502	    c.check("one unplanned, unresumed sandbox interruption",
503	            len(intrs) == 1 and intrs[0].get("code") == "ESANDBOX"
504	            and intrs[0].get("planned") is False and intrs[0].get("resumed") is False, intrs)
505	    c.check("the interruption is on the run record too",
506	            len(record.get("interruptions") or []) == 1, record.get("interruptions"))
507	
508	    esandbox = [e for e in events_of(record, "tool.result")
509	                if (data_of(e).get("error_class") or {}).get("code") == "ESANDBOX"]
510	    first = esandbox[0] if esandbox else None
511	    c.check("the failing call reads not_executed against a dead sandbox",
512	            bool(first) and data_of(first).get("outcome") == "not_executed"
513	            and (data_of(first).get("sandbox") or {}).get("alive") is False,
514	            {k: data_of(first).get(k) for k in ("outcome", "sandbox", "error_code")} if first else "")
515	    later = [e for e in events_of(record, "tool.call")
516	             if first is not None and int(e.get("id", -1)) > int(first["id"])]
517	    c.check("the loop stopped within 1 step of the first ESANDBOX "
518	            "(the model is not given more steps to flail into a corpse)",
519	            len(later) <= 1, f"{len(later)} tool calls after it: "
520	                             f"{[data_of(e).get('tool') for e in later]}")
521	    save_case(out, "sandbox-loss", record, c)
522	    return c
523	
524	
525	# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- (c) conformance
526	
527	
528	def case_conformance(client: httpx.Client, harness: str, out: pathlib.Path,
529	                     records: dict[str, dict[str, Any]]) -> Checks:
530	    """Re-fetch every event of the three runs and validate it against the shared contract."""
531	    c = Checks("conformance")
532	    report: dict[str, Any] = {}
533	    wanted = [k for k in ("worker-crash", "lost-ack", "sandbox-loss") if k in records]
534	    c.check("the three runs are available to validate", len(wanted) == 3, wanted)
535	
536	    for case in wanted:
537	        run_id = records[case].get("run_id")
538	        fresh = client.get(f"{harness}/runs/{run_id}", timeout=60.0).json()
539	        events = fresh.get("events") or []
540	        bad_schema: list[str] = []
541	        for event in events:
542	            try:
543	                Event.model_validate(event)
544	            except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
545	                bad_schema.append(f"id={event.get('id')} {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"[:200])
546	        try:
547	            RunRecord.model_validate(fresh)
548	            record_ok, record_err = True, ""
549	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
550	            record_ok, record_err = False, f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"[:300]
551	
552	        results = [data_of(e) for e in fresh.get("events") or [] if e.get("type") == "tool.result"]
553	        missing_outcome = [r.get("tool_use_id") for r in results if r.get("outcome") not in OUTCOMES]
554	        mismatched = [r.get("tool_use_id") for r in results
555	                      if bool(r.get("is_error")) != bool(r.get("error_class"))]
556	        missing_attempts = [r.get("tool_use_id") for r in results
557	                            if not isinstance(r.get("attempts"), int) or "sandbox" not in r]
558	        faults = [data_of(e) for e in fresh.get("events") or [] if e.get("type") == "fault.fired"]
559	        bad_faults = [f for f in faults
560	                      if not (f.get("origin") and f.get("layer") and f.get("description"))]
561	        finished = [data_of(e) for e in fresh.get("events") or [] if e.get("type") == "run.finished"]
562	        no_eval_status = [f for f in finished if not f.get("evaluation_status")]
563	
564	        report[case] = {"run_id": run_id, "events": len(events), "tool_results": len(results),
565	                        "faults_fired": len(faults), "schema_errors": bad_schema,
566	                        "record_valid": record_ok, "record_error": record_err,
567	                        "results_missing_outcome": missing_outcome,
568	                        "error_class_mismatch": mismatched,
569	                        "results_missing_attempts_or_sandbox": missing_attempts,
570	                        "faults_missing_provenance": bad_faults,
571	                        "run_finished_without_evaluation_status": no_eval_status}
572	
573	        c.check(f"{case}: every event validates as schemas.Event ({len(events)} events)",
574	                not bad_schema, bad_schema[:2])
575	        c.check(f"{case}: the record validates as schemas.RunRecord", record_ok, record_err)
576	        c.check(f"{case}: every tool.result carries a known `outcome`", not missing_outcome,
577	                missing_outcome[:3])
578	        c.check(f"{case}: error_class is present iff is_error", not mismatched, mismatched[:3])
579	        c.check(f"{case}: every tool.result carries attempts + sandbox", not missing_attempts,
580	                missing_attempts[:3])
581	        c.check(f"{case}: every fault.fired carries origin/layer/description", not bad_faults,
582	                bad_faults[:2])
583	        c.check(f"{case}: run.finished carries evaluation_status", finished and not no_eval_status,
584	                no_eval_status[:1])
585	
586	    (out / "conformance.json").write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2, default=str))
587	    return c
588	
589	
590	# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- specimen
591	
592	
593	def case_specimen(client: httpx.Client, harness: str, out: pathlib.Path) -> Checks:
594	    c = Checks("specimen")
595	    resp = client.get(f"{harness}/runs/{SPECIMEN_RUN}", timeout=60.0)
596	    record = resp.json() if resp.status_code == 200 else {}
597	    (out / "specimen.json").write_text(json.dumps(
598	        {"run_id": SPECIMEN_RUN, "status": record.get("status"),
599	         "score": (record.get("evaluation") or {}).get("score"),
600	         "error_class": record.get("error_class"), "interruptions": record.get("interruptions"),
601	         "worker_generation": record.get("worker_generation"),
602	         "error_codes": sorted({data_of(e).get("error_code") for e in record.get("events") or []
603	                                if e.get("type") == "tool.result" and data_of(e).get("is_error")}
604	                               - {None})},
605	        indent=2, default=str))
606	    c.check("the pre-taxonomy specimen is still served", resp.status_code == 200, resp.status_code)
607	    c.check("its old events are untouched (still EINTERNAL, still status ok)",
608	            record.get("status") == "ok"
609	            and any(data_of(e).get("error_code") == "EINTERNAL" for e in record.get("events") or []),
610	            record.get("status"))
611	    c.check("but the record now answers the taxonomy's questions",
612	            all(k in record for k in ("error_class", "interruptions", "worker_generation")),
613	            {k: record.get(k) for k in ("error_class", "interruptions", "worker_generation")})
614	    print("  note: r_ccda8780cbee is pre-taxonomy data and is deliberately NOT rewritten;\n"
615	          "        `sandbox-loss` is the live proof that the same failure now ends `interrupted`.")
616	    return c
617	
618	
619	# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- transport abort (opt-in)
620	
621	
622	def case_transport_abort(client: httpx.Client, harness: str, out: pathlib.Path,
623	                         records: dict[str, dict[str, Any]]) -> Checks:
624	    """The other real interruption: we cancel the request; the server completes it anyway."""
625	    c = Checks("transport-abort")
626	    run_id = start_run(client, harness, "lost-ack", harness_faults=[
627	        {"kind": "transport_abort", "tool": "write_file", "path": CHANGELOG,
628	         "nth": 1, "after_ms": 250}])
629	    print(f"  run {run_id}", flush=True)
630	    record = poll(client, harness, run_id)
631	    records["transport-abort"] = record
632	    save_case(out, "transport-abort", record, c)
633	
634	    aborted = [data_of(e) for e in events_of(record, "tool.result")
635	               if (data_of(e).get("error_class") or {}).get("code") == "ETRANSPORT"]
636	    c.check("the in-flight write came back as real/transport/ETRANSPORT", bool(aborted),
637	            aborted[0].get("error_class") if aborted else "")
638	    if aborted:
639	        ec = aborted[0]["error_class"]
640	        c.check("...outcome unknown (the server may well have applied it)",
641	                aborted[0].get("outcome") == "unknown" and ec.get("outcome_known") is False, ec)
642	        c.check("...with the taxonomy's exact label", ec.get("label") == LABEL_TRANSPORT,
643	                ec.get("label"))
644	        c.check("...and the harness did NOT retry it", aborted[0].get("attempts") == 1,
645	                aborted[0].get("attempts"))
646	    c.check("no worker died: a transport abort does not crash the run",
647	            record.get("worker_generation") == 1 and not events_of(record, "run.resumed"),
648	            record.get("worker_generation"))
649	    c.check("the run still reaches a graded end",
650	            record.get("status") in GRADED
651	            and (record.get("evaluation") or {}).get("score") is not None,
652	            f"status={record.get('status')} score={(record.get('evaluation') or {}).get('score')}")
653	    return c
654	
655	
656	# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- main
657	
658	
659	def main() -> int:
660	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__,
661	                                     formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
662	    parser.add_argument("--harness", default=DEFAULT_HARNESS)
663	    parser.add_argument("--sandbox-url", default=DEFAULT_SANDBOX_ENV)
664	    parser.add_argument("--case", action="append", choices=CASES,
665	                        help="run only these cases (repeatable); default: everything but transport-abort")
666	    parser.add_argument("--out", default=None)
667	    args = parser.parse_args()
668	
669	    harness = args.harness.rstrip("/")
670	    sandbox_env = args.sandbox_url.rstrip("/")
671	    out = pathlib.Path(args.out) if args.out else REPO_ROOT / "runs" / f"{utc_stamp()}_interruptions"
672	    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
673	    selected = args.case or list(DEFAULT_CASES)
674	    records: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
675	    started = time.time()
676	
677	    results: list[Checks] = []
678	    with httpx.Client(follow_redirects=True) as client:
679	        health = client.get(f"{harness}/health", timeout=60.0).json()
680	        gym = client.get(f"{sandbox_env}/health", timeout=60.0).json()
681	        print(f"harness    ok={health.get('ok')} model={health.get('model_default')} "
682	              f"provider_key={health.get('has_provider_key')} "
683	              f"sandbox_env={health.get('detail', {}).get('sandbox_env_reachable')}")
684	        print(f"sandbox-env ok={gym.get('ok')} scenarios={gym.get('detail', {}).get('scenarios')}")
685	        (out / "health.json").write_text(json.dumps({"harness": health, "sandbox_env": gym},
686	                                                    indent=2, default=str))
687	        for case in selected:
688	            print(f"\n=== {case} ===", flush=True)
689	            try:
690	                if case == "worker-crash":
691	                    results.append(case_worker_crash(client, harness, out, records))
692	                elif case == "lost-ack":
693	                    results.append(case_lost_ack(client, harness, out, records))
694	                elif case == "sandbox-loss":
695	                    results.append(case_sandbox_loss(client, harness, sandbox_env, out, records))
696	                elif case == "conformance":
697	                    results.append(case_conformance(client, harness, out, records))
698	                elif case == "transport-abort":
699	                    results.append(case_transport_abort(client, harness, out, records))
700	                else:
701	                    results.append(case_specimen(client, harness, out))
702	            except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - a broken case is a FAIL, not a traceback
703	                broken = Checks(case)
704	                broken.check("case ran to completion", False, f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}")
705	                results.append(broken)
706	
707	    total = sum(len(r.rows) for r in results)
708	    passed = sum(r.passed for r in results)
709	    ok = all(r.ok for r in results) and bool(results)
710	    (out / "summary.json").write_text(json.dumps(
711	        {"ok": ok, "checks": total, "passed": passed, "duration_s": round(time.time() - started, 1),
712	         "harness": harness, "sandbox_env": sandbox_env, "at": utc_stamp(),
713	         "runs": {case: {"run_id": rec.get("run_id"), "status": rec.get("status"),
714	                         "score": (rec.get("evaluation") or {}).get("score"),
715	                         "worker_generation": rec.get("worker_generation")}
716	                  for case, rec in records.items()},
717	         "cases": [{"case": r.case, "ok": r.ok, "checks": len(r.rows), "passed": r.passed,
718	                    "assertions": r.rows} for r in results]}, indent=2, default=str))
719	    print("\n" + "\n".join(f"  {r.case:<16} {r.passed}/{len(r.rows)} {'ok' if r.ok else 'FAILED'}"
720	                           for r in results))
721	    print(f"\n{'PROVE PASS' if ok else 'PROVE FAIL'} :: {passed}/{total} checks, out={out}")
722	    return 0 if ok else 1
723	
724	
725	if __name__ == "__main__":
726	    raise SystemExit(main())

1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
2	"""Run one Faultline episode against a deployed harness and save the evidence.
3	
4	    python scripts/run_episode_cli.py --scenario lost-ack
5	    python scripts/run_episode_cli.py --scenario locked-file --model claude-sonnet-5 --max-steps 12
6	
7	POSTs /runs, then tails GET /runs/{id}/events over SSE, reconnecting with Last-Event-ID whenever the
8	server closes the window (the harness closes every stream at ~110 s because Modal kills a web
9	request at 150 s). Prints a compact live transcript and, when the run finishes, writes
10	
11	    runs/<UTC timestamp>_<run_id>/{events.jsonl,run.json,evaluate.json,summary.txt}
12	
13	Needs only httpx: run it with the harness venv
14	(services/agent-harness/.venv/bin/python scripts/run_episode_cli.py …).
15	"""
16	
17	from __future__ import annotations
18	
19	import argparse
20	import json
21	import pathlib
22	import sys
23	import time
24	import uuid
25	from datetime import datetime, timezone
26	from typing import Any, Iterator
27	
28	import httpx
29	
30	REPO_ROOT = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
31	DEFAULT_HARNESS = "https://appliedlabsai-local--faultline-harness-api.modal.run"
32	#: schemas.RunStatus minus queued/running — `unevaluated` and `interrupted` are terminal too, and a
33	#: tail that does not know that waits for a run that will never move again.
34	TERMINAL = {"ok", "error", "truncated", "unevaluated", "interrupted"}
35	#: statuses that mean the agent got a fair shot and was graded (used for the exit code)
36	GRADED = {"ok", "truncated"}
37	
38	FAULT_BADGE = {"missing_file": "MISSING-FILE", "denied_write": "DENIED-WRITE", "ack_lost": "ACK-LOST"}
39	#: docs/error-taxonomy.md: badge by origin — the agent cannot tell these apart, the operator must.
40	ORIGIN_BADGE = {"injected": "SIMULATED", "staged": "STAGED", "real": "REAL"}
41	OUTCOME_BADGE = {"executed": "ran", "failed": "ran/failed", "not_executed": "never ran",
42	                 "unknown": "OUTCOME UNKNOWN"}
43	
44	
45	def utc_stamp() -> str:
46	    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ")
47	
48	
49	def short(text: Any, n: int = 96) -> str:
50	    s = " ".join(str(text or "").split())
51	    return s if len(s) <= n else s[: n - 1] + "…"
52	
53	
54	# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- SSE
55	
56	
57	def sse_frames(response: httpx.Response) -> Iterator[dict[str, Any]]:
58	    """Yield {id?, event, data} frames from a text/event-stream response."""
59	    event_type = "message"
60	    event_id: int | None = None
61	    data_lines: list[str] = []
62	    for line in response.iter_lines():
63	        line = line.rstrip("\r")
64	        if line == "":
65	            if data_lines:
66	                raw = "\n".join(data_lines)
67	                try:
68	                    payload = json.loads(raw)
69	                except json.JSONDecodeError:
70	                    payload = {"raw": raw}
71	                yield {"id": event_id, "event": event_type, "data": payload}
72	            event_type, event_id, data_lines = "message", None, []
73	            continue
74	        if line.startswith(":"):  # keepalive comment
75	            continue
76	        field, _, value = line.partition(":")
77	        value = value[1:] if value.startswith(" ") else value
78	        if field == "event":
79	            event_type = value
80	        elif field == "data":
81	            data_lines.append(value)
82	        elif field == "id":
83	            try:
84	                event_id = int(value)
85	            except ValueError:
86	                event_id = None
87	
88	
89	# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- transcript
90	
91	
92	def render(event: dict[str, Any]) -> str | None:
93	    """One compact line per interesting event (None = don't print)."""
94	    etype = event.get("type", "")
95	    data = event.get("data") or {}
96	    step = event.get("step")
97	    prefix = f"  {step:>2} " if isinstance(step, int) else "     "
98	
99	    if etype == "run.started":
100	        return (f"run   scenario={data.get('scenario_id')} model={data.get('model')} "
101	                f"max_steps={data.get('max_steps')} workspace={data.get('anthropic_workspace')}")
102	    if etype == "episode.reset":
103	        return (f"reset episode={data.get('episode_id')} files={len(data.get('files') or [])}"
104	                f" sandbox={data.get('sandbox_id')} attempt={data.get('attempt')}")
105	    if etype == "run.resumed":
106	        return (f"RESUMED worker={data.get('worker_generation')} "
107	                f"from_event={data.get('resumed_from_event_id')} "
108	                f"dangling={data.get('dangling_tool_use_id')}")
109	    if etype == "interruption":
110	        planned = "planned" if data.get("planned") else "unplanned"
111	        return (f"{prefix}!! INTERRUPTION [{data.get('layer')}/{data.get('code')}] {planned} "
112	                f"resumed={data.get('resumed')} outcome_known={data.get('outcome_known')} "
113	                f"— {short(data.get('label'), 70)}")
114	    if etype == "episode.sandbox":
115	        return (f"{prefix}   sandbox {data.get('sandbox_id')} -> {data.get('status')}"
116	                f"  {short(data.get('reason'), 70)}")
117	    if etype == "turn.text":
118	        return f"{prefix}· {short(data.get('text'), 110)}"
119	    if etype == "tool.call":
120	        args = data.get("input") or {}
121	        label = args.get("command") or args.get("path") or args.get("summary") or ""
122	        return f"{prefix}→ {data.get('tool'):<12} {short(label, 84)}"
123	    if etype == "tool.result":
124	        out = data.get("output") or ""
125	        exit_code: Any = None
126	        try:
127	            payload = json.loads(out)
128	            exit_code = payload.get("exit_code", payload.get("code"))
129	        except (json.JSONDecodeError, AttributeError, TypeError):
130	            payload = {}
131	        bits = []
132	        if exit_code is not None:
133	            bits.append(f"exit={exit_code}")
134	        bits.append(f"{data.get('duration_ms', 0)}ms")
135	        if data.get("attempts", 1) > 1:
136	            bits.append(f"attempts={data['attempts']}")
137	        if data.get("is_error"):
138	            bits.append("ERROR")
139	        outcome = data.get("outcome")
140	        if outcome and outcome != "executed":
141	            bits.append(OUTCOME_BADGE.get(outcome, outcome))
142	        ec = data.get("error_class") or {}
143	        if ec:
144	            # The agent saw only the OS-style code; this line is what the operator is owed.
145	            bits.append(f"[{ORIGIN_BADGE.get(ec.get('origin'), ec.get('origin'))} "
146	                        f"{ec.get('layer')}/{ec.get('code')}] {short(ec.get('label'), 60)}")
147	        fault = data.get("fault") or {}
148	        if fault and not ec:
149	            badge = FAULT_BADGE.get(fault.get("kind"), str(fault.get("kind")))
150	            bits.append(f"[{badge}{'?' if fault.get('inferred') else ''}]")
151	        sandbox = data.get("sandbox") or {}
152	        if sandbox.get("alive") is False:
153	            bits.append("sandbox=DEAD")
154	        return f"{prefix}← {' '.join(bits)}  {short(out, 60)}"
155	    if etype == "fault.fired":
156	        badge = FAULT_BADGE.get(data.get("kind"), str(data.get("kind")))
157	        origin = ORIGIN_BADGE.get(data.get("origin"), data.get("origin") or "?")
158	        return (f"{prefix}!! {badge} on {data.get('path')} ({data.get('mode')}, {origin}"
159	                f"/{data.get('layer')}) {short(data.get('description'), 60)}")
160	    if etype == "workspace.diff":
161	        changed = [f for f in (data.get("files") or []) if f.get("status") != "unchanged"]
162	        return f"{prefix}   diff: {len(changed)} changed " + short(
163	            ", ".join(f"{f.get('path')}({f.get('status')})" for f in changed), 70
164	        )
165	    if etype == "episode.evaluated":
166	        checks = data.get("checks") or []
167	        ok = sum(1 for c in checks if c.get("ok"))
168	        tests = data.get("tests") or {}
169	        return (f"grade score={data.get('score')} passed={data.get('passed')} "
170	                f"checks={ok}/{len(checks)} tests={tests.get('passed')}p/{tests.get('failed')}f")
171	    if etype == "run.finished":
172	        usage = data.get("usage") or {}
173	        ec = data.get("error_class") or {}
174	        return (f"done  status={data.get('status')} evaluation={data.get('evaluation_status')} "
175	                f"steps={data.get('steps')} workers={data.get('worker_generation')} "
176	                f"{data.get('duration_ms')}ms in={usage.get('input_tokens')} "
177	                f"out={usage.get('output_tokens')}"
178	                + (f"\n      why: [{ORIGIN_BADGE.get(ec.get('origin'), '?')} {ec.get('layer')}/"
179	                   f"{ec.get('code')}] {ec.get('label')}" if ec else "")
180	                + (f"\n      evaluation_error={short(data.get('evaluation_error'), 90)}"
181	                   if data.get("evaluation_error") else "")
182	                + (f"\n      error={short(data.get('error'), 90)}" if data.get("error") else ""))
183	    if etype == "log" and data.get("ev") == "ledger.resolution":
184	        rows = data.get("resolutions") or []
185	        applied = [r for r in rows if r.get("side_effect_applied")]
186	        return (f"      ledger resolved {len(rows)} call(s); side effect APPLIED for "
187	                f"{len(applied)}: " + short(", ".join(
188	                    f"{r.get('tool_use_id') or r.get('ledger_step')}={r.get('side_effect_applied')}"
189	                    for r in rows), 80))
190	    if etype == "log" and data.get("lvl") in {"warn", "error"}:
191	        return f"{prefix}   [{data.get('lvl')}] {data.get('ev')}: {short(data.get('msg'), 90)}"
192	    return None
193

195	# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- main
196	
197	
198	def identity_headers(user: str | None) -> dict[str, str]:
199	    """PLAN.md §2.9.1: the browser's anonymous id. Sent on every request so the run lands in that
200	    user's conversation list; omitted entirely when --user is not given (anonymous run)."""
201	    return {"X-Faultline-User": user} if user else {}
202	
203	
204	def tail(client: httpx.Client, harness: str, run_id: str, events: list[dict[str, Any]],
205	         deadline: float, quiet: bool, windows: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
206	         user: str | None = None) -> str | None:
207	    """Stream until the run is finished or the deadline passes; returns the final status.
208	
209	    `windows` collects one entry per 110 s segment close (`event: done` with reason=window), which is
210	    the reconnect path apps/web has to implement; it is saved into the evidence summary.
211	    """
212	    last_id = -1
213	    status: str | None = None
214	    windows = [] if windows is None else windows
215	    t_open = time.time()
216	    while time.time() < deadline:
217	        headers = {"Accept": "text/event-stream", **identity_headers(user)}
218	        if last_id >= 0:
219	            headers["Last-Event-ID"] = str(last_id)
220	        try:
221	            with client.stream("GET", f"{harness}/runs/{run_id}/events", headers=headers,
222	                               timeout=httpx.Timeout(connect=15.0, read=130.0, write=15.0, pool=15.0)) as resp:
223	                resp.raise_for_status()
224	                for frame in sse_frames(resp):
225	                    if frame["event"] == "done":
226	                        reason = (frame["data"] or {}).get("reason")
227	                        status = (frame["data"] or {}).get("status")
228	                        if reason == "finished":
229	                            return status
230	                        # reason == "window": the 110 s segment elapsed on a still-running run.
231	                        # Record it — this is the reconnect path apps/web must implement too.
232	                        print(f"     ~ sse window closed at id={last_id} (status={status}); reconnecting",
233	                              file=sys.stderr, flush=True)
234	                        windows.append({"at_event_id": last_id, "status": status, "t": round(time.time() - t_open, 1)})
235	                        break  # window closed: reconnect from last_id
236	                    event = frame["data"]
237	                    if not isinstance(event, dict) or "id" not in event:
238	                        continue
239	                    if event["id"] <= last_id:
240	                        continue
241	                    last_id = event["id"]
242	                    events.append(event)
243	                    line = render(event)
244	                    if line and not quiet:
245	                        print(line, flush=True)
246	                    if event.get("type") == "run.finished":
247	                        status = (event.get("data") or {}).get("status")
248	        except (httpx.HTTPError, httpx.StreamError) as exc:
249	            print(f"… stream interrupted ({type(exc).__name__}: {exc}); reconnecting", file=sys.stderr)
250	            time.sleep(1.0)
251	            continue
252	        if status in TERMINAL:
253	            return status
254	        # Fall back to the record in case we missed the terminal event on a dropped stream.
255	        record = client.get(f"{harness}/runs/{run_id}", timeout=30.0,
256	                            headers=identity_headers(user)).json()
257	        if record.get("status") in TERMINAL:
258	            return record["status"]
259	        time.sleep(0.5)
260	    return status
261	
262	
263	def write_evidence(out_dir: pathlib.Path, run_id: str, record: dict[str, Any],
264	                   events: list[dict[str, Any]], windows: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None) -> None:
265	    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
266	    stream = record.get("events") or events  # the record is authoritative when we have it
267	
268	    with (out_dir / "events.jsonl").open("w") as fh:
269	        for event in stream:
270	            fh.write(json.dumps(event, default=str) + "\n")
271	    (out_dir / "run.json").write_text(json.dumps(record, indent=2, default=str))
272	    evaluation = record.get("evaluation") or {}
273	    (out_dir / "evaluate.json").write_text(json.dumps(evaluation, indent=2, default=str))
274	
275	    finished = next((e for e in stream if e.get("type") == "run.finished"), {}) or {}
276	    fdata = finished.get("data") or {}
277	    usage = fdata.get("usage") or record.get("usage") or {}
278	    error_class = record.get("error_class") or fdata.get("error_class") or {}
279	    lines = [
280	        f"run_id      {run_id}",
281	        f"scenario    {record.get('scenario_id')}",
282	        f"model       {record.get('model')}",
283	        f"status      {record.get('status')}",
284	        f"evaluation  {fdata.get('evaluation_status')}"
285	        + (f"  ({fdata.get('evaluation_error')})" if fdata.get("evaluation_error") else ""),
286	        f"error_class {(error_class.get('origin') or '-')}/{error_class.get('layer') or '-'}/"
287	        f"{error_class.get('code') or '-'}  {error_class.get('label') or ''}",
288	        f"workers     {record.get('worker_generation')}",
289	        f"episode     {record.get('episode_id')}",
290	        f"steps       {record.get('steps')}",
291	        f"events      {len(stream)}",
292	        f"tokens      in={usage.get('input_tokens')} out={usage.get('output_tokens')}",
293	        f"duration_ms {fdata.get('duration_ms')}",
294	        f"sse_windows {json.dumps(windows or [])}",
295	        f"error       {record.get('error')}",
296	        "",
297	        f"score       {evaluation.get('score')}  passed={evaluation.get('passed')}",
298	    ]
299	    for check in evaluation.get("checks") or []:
300	        lines.append(f"  [{'x' if check.get('ok') else ' '}] {check.get('id'):<28} "
301	                     f"w={check.get('weight')} {short(check.get('detail'), 70)}")
302	    tests = evaluation.get("tests") or {}
303	    lines += ["", f"tests       passed={tests.get('passed')} failed={tests.get('failed')} "
304	                  f"errors={tests.get('errors')}", short(tests.get("output"), 400), ""]
305	    faults = [e for e in stream if e.get("type") == "fault.fired"]
306	    lines.append(f"faults fired ({len(faults)}):")
307	    for event in faults:
308	        data = event.get("data") or {}
309	        lines.append(f"  step {event.get('step')}: {data.get('kind')} on {data.get('path')} "
310	                     f"({data.get('mode')}, {data.get('origin')}/{data.get('layer')})")
311	    interruptions = [e for e in stream if e.get("type") == "interruption"]
312	    lines.append("")
313	    lines.append(f"real interruptions ({len(interruptions)}):")
314	    for event in interruptions:
315	        data = event.get("data") or {}
316	        lines.append(f"  step {event.get('step')}: {data.get('layer')}/{data.get('code')} "
317	                     f"planned={data.get('planned')} resumed={data.get('resumed')} "
318	                     f"outcome_known={data.get('outcome_known')} tool={data.get('tool')} "
319	                     f"path={data.get('path')}")
320	    resumes = [e for e in stream if e.get("type") == "run.resumed"]
321	    for event in resumes:
322	        data = event.get("data") or {}
323	        lines.append(f"  resumed as worker {data.get('worker_generation')} from event "
324	                     f"{data.get('resumed_from_event_id')} (dangling "
325	                     f"{data.get('dangling_tool_use_id')})")
326	    outcomes: dict[str, int] = {}
327	    for event in stream:
328	        if event.get("type") == "tool.result":
329	            key = str((event.get("data") or {}).get("outcome") or "?")
330	            outcomes[key] = outcomes.get(key, 0) + 1
331	    lines += ["", f"tool outcomes {json.dumps(outcomes)}"]
332	    resolution = next((e for e in stream if e.get("type") == "log"
333	                       and (e.get("data") or {}).get("ev") == "ledger.resolution"), None)
334	    if resolution:
335	        rows = (resolution.get("data") or {}).get("resolutions") or []
336	        lines.append(f"ledger resolution ({len(rows)}):")
337	        for row in rows:
338	            lines.append(f"  {row.get('tool_use_id') or ('ledger step ' + str(row.get('ledger_step')))}"
339	                         f"  {row.get('tool')} {row.get('path')} outcome={row.get('outcome')} "
340	                         f"origin={row.get('origin')} interrupted={row.get('interrupted')} "
341	                         f"side_effect_applied={row.get('side_effect_applied')}")
342	    if record.get("summary"):
343	        lines += ["", "agent summary:", short(record["summary"], 1200)]
344	    (out_dir / "summary.txt").write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n")
345	
346	
347	def main() -> int:
348	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Run one Faultline episode and save the evidence.")
349	    parser.add_argument("--harness", default=DEFAULT_HARNESS, help="harness base URL")
350	    parser.add_argument("--scenario", default="lost-ack", help="scenario id (see GET /scenarios)")
351	    parser.add_argument("--model", default=None, help="claude-haiku-4-5 | claude-sonnet-5 | claude-opus-5")
352	    parser.add_argument("--max-steps", type=int, default=None)
353	    parser.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=None)
354	    parser.add_argument("--user", default=None,
355	                        help="X-Faultline-User id (u_<uuid4>) to scope the run to a browser identity; "
356	                             "'new' mints one. Without it the run is anonymous.")
357	    parser.add_argument("--out", default=None, help="evidence directory (default runs/<ts>_<run_id>)")
358	    parser.add_argument("--timeout", type=float, default=900.0, help="give up after N seconds")
359	    parser.add_argument("--quiet", action="store_true", help="save evidence without the live transcript")
360	    args = parser.parse_args()
361	
362	    harness = args.harness.rstrip("/")
363	    user = args.user
364	    if user == "new":
365	        user = f"u_{uuid.uuid4()}"
366	    if user and not user.startswith("u_"):
367	        print(f"--user must look like u_<uuid4>, got {user!r}", file=sys.stderr)
368	        return 2
369	    headers = identity_headers(user)
370	    body: dict[str, Any] = {"scenario_id": args.scenario}
371	    if args.model:
372	        body["model"] = args.model
373	    if args.max_steps:
374	        body["max_steps"] = args.max_steps
375	    if args.seed is not None:
376	        body["seed"] = args.seed
377	
378	    with httpx.Client(follow_redirects=True) as client:
379	        health = client.get(f"{harness}/health", timeout=30.0).json()
380	        print(f"harness {harness} ok={health.get('ok')} model={health.get('model_default')} "
381	              f"provider_key={health.get('has_provider_key')} "
382	              f"sandbox_env={health.get('detail', {}).get('sandbox_env_reachable')}")
383	        if health.get("has_provider_key"):
384	            print("WARNING: the API function reports a provider key in its env (boundary leak)",
385	                  file=sys.stderr)
386	
387	        resp = client.post(f"{harness}/runs", json=body, timeout=60.0, headers=headers)
388	        if resp.status_code >= 400:
389	            print(f"POST /runs failed: {resp.status_code} {resp.text[:500]}", file=sys.stderr)
390	            return 2
391	        created = resp.json()
392	        run_id = created["run_id"]
393	        print(f"run_id {run_id}  scenario={created.get('scenario_id')} model={created.get('model')}"
394	              + (f" conversation={created.get('conversation_id')}" if created.get("conversation_id") else "")
395	              + (f" user={user}" if user else ""))
396	
397	        events: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
398	        windows: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
399	        t0 = time.time()
400	        status = tail(client, harness, run_id, events, t0 + args.timeout, args.quiet, windows, user)
401	        record = client.get(f"{harness}/runs/{run_id}", timeout=60.0, headers=headers).json()
402	        conversations: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
403	        if user:
404	            # PLAN.md §3.2 "done when": the run must show up in this browser's conversation list.
405	            got = client.get(f"{harness}/conversations", timeout=30.0, headers=headers)
406	            if got.status_code == 200:
407	                payload = got.json()
408	                conversations = payload if isinstance(payload, list) else payload.get("conversations", [])
409	            else:
410	                print(f"GET /conversations -> {got.status_code} {got.text[:200]}", file=sys.stderr)
411	
412	    out_dir = pathlib.Path(args.out) if args.out else REPO_ROOT / "runs" / f"{utc_stamp()}_{run_id}"
413	    write_evidence(out_dir, run_id, record, events, windows)
414	    if user:
415	        (out_dir / "conversations.json").write_text(
416	            json.dumps({"user_id": user, "conversation_id": record.get("conversation_id"),
417	                        "conversations": conversations}, indent=2, default=str)
418	        )
419	        listed = any(c.get("id") == record.get("conversation_id") for c in conversations)
420	        print(f"user={user} conversation={record.get('conversation_id')} "
421	              f"listed_in_GET_/conversations={listed} ({len(conversations)} total)")
422	    final = record.get("status") or status
423	    finished = next((e for e in (record.get("events") or events)
424	                     if e.get("type") == "run.finished"), {}) or {}
425	    fdata = finished.get("data") or {}
426	    error_class = record.get("error_class") or fdata.get("error_class") or {}
427	    print(f"\nstatus={final} evaluation={fdata.get('evaluation_status')} "
428	          f"score={(record.get('evaluation') or {}).get('score')} "
429	          f"workers={record.get('worker_generation')} "
430	          f"interruptions={len(record.get('interruptions') or [])} evidence={out_dir}")
431	    if error_class:
432	        print(f"why    [{ORIGIN_BADGE.get(error_class.get('origin'), '?')} "
433	              f"{error_class.get('layer')}/{error_class.get('code')}] {error_class.get('label')}")
434	    # `unevaluated`/`interrupted` are NOT failures of the agent, but they are not a graded run
435	    # either: the caller needs a non-zero exit so a proof script cannot mistake one for a pass.
436	    return 0 if final in GRADED else 1
437	
438	
439	if __name__ == "__main__":
440	    raise SystemExit(main())
