1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
2	"""Export a captured run as a bundled browser replay.
3	
4	    python3 scripts/export_demo.py runs/20260912T173000Z_r_abc
5	    python3 scripts/export_demo.py runs/<dir> --out-name gauntlet --pretty
6	    python3 scripts/export_demo.py runs/<dir> --check
7	
8	Reads ``<run_dir>/run.json`` (a ``faultline_common.schemas.RunRecord``, written by
9	``scripts/run_episode_cli.py``) and writes ``apps/web/public/demo/<scenario_id>.json``.
10	
11	The browser folds that file through the same reducer a live SSE run goes through, so the replay is
12	pixel-identical to the real run — see ``apps/web/public/demo/README.md``. Register the new file in
13	``apps/web/src/lib/replay.ts`` (``DEMOS``) so the home page offers it.
14	
15	Owned by the apps/web workstream; writes only into apps/web/public/demo/.
16	"""
17	
18	from __future__ import annotations
19	
20	import argparse
21	import json
22	import sys
23	from pathlib import Path
24	from typing import Any
25	
26	REPO_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
27	DEMO_DIR = REPO_ROOT / "apps" / "web" / "public" / "demo"
28	
29	# Keep the shared logger optional: this script must run from a bare checkout.
30	sys.path.insert(0, str(REPO_ROOT / "packages" / "common"))
31	try:
32	    from faultline_common.log import get_logger  # type: ignore
33	
34	    log = get_logger("script")
35	except Exception:  # pragma: no cover - fallback when the shared package is absent
36	
37	    class _Fallback:
38	        def _emit(self, lvl: str, ev: str, msg: str, **kw: Any) -> None:
39	            rec = {"svc": "script", "lvl": lvl, "ev": ev, "msg": msg, **kw}
40	            print(json.dumps(rec, default=str), file=sys.stderr)
41	
42	        def info(self, ev: str, msg: str = "", **kw: Any) -> None:
43	            self._emit("info", ev, msg, **kw)
44	
45	        def warn(self, ev: str, msg: str = "", **kw: Any) -> None:
46	            self._emit("warn", ev, msg, **kw)
47	
48	        def error(self, ev: str, msg: str = "", **kw: Any) -> None:
49	            self._emit("error", ev, msg, **kw)
50	
51	    log = _Fallback()
52	
53	REQUIRED_RECORD_FIELDS = ("run_id", "status", "scenario_id", "model", "created_at", "events")
54	EVENT_TYPES = {
55	    "run.started",
56	    "episode.reset",
57	    "turn.text",
58	    "tool.call",
59	    "tool.result",
60	    "fault.fired",
61	    "workspace.diff",
62	    "episode.evaluated",
63	    "run.finished",
64	    "log",
65	    # added once the harness started emitting them (faultline_common.schemas.EventType)
66	    "llm.call",
67	    "turn.thinking",
68	}
69	
70	
71	class ExportError(RuntimeError):
72	    pass
73	
74	
75	def load_record(run_dir: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
76	    path = run_dir / "run.json" if run_dir.is_dir() else run_dir
77	    if not path.exists():
78	        raise ExportError(f"{path} not found — pass a runs/<ts>_<run_id> directory or a run.json")
79	    try:
80	        rec = json.loads(path.read_text())
81	    except json.JSONDecodeError as e:
82	        raise ExportError(f"{path} is not valid JSON: {e}") from e
83	    if not isinstance(rec, dict):
84	        raise ExportError(f"{path} does not contain a RunRecord object")
85	    log.info("demo.read", "loaded run record", path=str(path), bytes=path.stat().st_size)
86	    return rec
87	
88	
89	def validate(rec: dict[str, Any], *, allow_unevaluated: bool) -> list[str]:
90	    """Return a list of problems. Empty means the record is fit to ship."""
91	    problems: list[str] = []
92	
93	    for field in REQUIRED_RECORD_FIELDS:
94	        if field not in rec:
95	            problems.append(f"missing RunRecord.{field}")
96	
97	    events = rec.get("events")
98	    if not isinstance(events, list) or not events:
99	        problems.append("RunRecord.events is empty — nothing to replay")
100	        return problems
101	
102	    run_id = rec.get("run_id")
103	    seen_types: set[str] = set()
104	    for i, ev in enumerate(events):
105	        if not isinstance(ev, dict):
106	            problems.append(f"event[{i}] is not an object")
107	            continue
108	        if ev.get("id") != i:
109	            problems.append(f"event[{i}].id is {ev.get('id')!r}; ids must run 0..n-1 (SSE Last-Event-ID)")
110	        if run_id is not None and ev.get("run_id") != run_id:
111	            problems.append(f"event[{i}].run_id {ev.get('run_id')!r} != record run_id {run_id!r}")
112	        if not isinstance(ev.get("ts"), str):
113	            problems.append(f"event[{i}].ts is missing or not a string")
114	        etype = ev.get("type")
115	        if etype not in EVENT_TYPES:
116	            problems.append(f"event[{i}].type {etype!r} is not a known EventType")
117	        else:
118	            seen_types.add(etype)
119	        if not isinstance(ev.get("data"), dict):
120	            problems.append(f"event[{i}].data must be an object")
121	
122	    if "episode.evaluated" not in seen_types and not allow_unevaluated:
123	        problems.append(
124	            "no episode.evaluated event — an unfinished run makes a poor demo "
125	            "(pass --allow-unevaluated to ship it anyway)"
126	        )
127	    return problems
128	
129	
130	def sanitise(rec: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
131	    """Mask the few fields that could carry account identifiers into a public bundle."""
132	    out = json.loads(json.dumps(rec))  # deep copy via round-trip
133	    masked = 0
134	
135	    def mask(d: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
136	        nonlocal masked
137	        for key in ("anthropic_workspace", "api_key", "authorization"):
138	            if key in d and d[key]:
139	                d[key] = "****"
140	                masked += 1
141	
142	    mask(out)
143	    for ev in out.get("events", []):
144	        data = ev.get("data")
145	        if isinstance(data, dict):
146	            mask(data)
147	    # sandbox ids are ops detail, not part of the story
148	    for ev in out.get("events", []):
149	        data = ev.get("data")
150	        if isinstance(data, dict) and "sandbox_id" in data:
151	            data.pop("sandbox_id", None)
152	    if masked:
153	        log.info("demo.sanitised", "masked account fields", fields=masked)
154	    return out
155	
156	
157	def summarise(rec: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
158	    events = rec.get("events", [])
159	    counts: dict[str, int] = {}
160	    for ev in events:
161	        t = ev.get("type", "?")
162	        counts[t] = counts.get(t, 0) + 1
163	    evaluation = rec.get("evaluation") or {}
164	    return {
165	        "run_id": rec.get("run_id"),
166	        "scenario_id": rec.get("scenario_id"),
167	        "model": rec.get("model"),
168	        "status": rec.get("status"),
169	        "events": len(events),
170	        "event_types": counts,
171	        "score": evaluation.get("score"),
172	        "passed": evaluation.get("passed"),
173	    }
174	
175	
176	def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
177	    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
178	    ap.add_argument("run_dir", type=Path, help="runs/<ts>_<run_id> directory (or a run.json path)")
179	    ap.add_argument("--out-name", help="basename without .json (default: the run's scenario_id)")
180	    ap.add_argument("--out-dir", type=Path, default=DEMO_DIR, help=f"default: {DEMO_DIR}")
181	    ap.add_argument("--pretty", action="store_true", help="indent the JSON (bigger file, readable diff)")
182	    ap.add_argument("--check", action="store_true", help="validate only; write nothing")
183	    ap.add_argument("--allow-unevaluated", action="store_true", help="ship a run with no episode.evaluated")
184	    ap.add_argument("--force", action="store_true", help="overwrite an existing demo file")
185	    args = ap.parse_args(argv)
186	
187	    try:
188	        rec = load_record(args.run_dir)
189	    except ExportError as e:
190	        log.error("demo.error", str(e))
191	        return 2
192	
193	    problems = validate(rec, allow_unevaluated=args.allow_unevaluated)
194	    if problems:
195	        for p in problems:
196	            log.error("demo.invalid", p)
197	        return 1
198	
199	    summary = summarise(rec)
200	    log.info("demo.valid", "record passed validation", **summary)
201	
202	    if args.check:
203	        print(json.dumps(summary, indent=2))
204	        return 0
205	
206	    name = args.out_name or str(rec.get("scenario_id") or "run")
207	    out_path = args.out_dir / f"{name}.json"
208	    if out_path.exists() and not args.force:
209	        log.error("demo.exists", f"{out_path} already exists; pass --force to overwrite")
210	        return 1
211	
212	    out_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
213	    payload = sanitise(rec)
214	    text = json.dumps(payload, indent=2) if args.pretty else json.dumps(payload)
215	    out_path.write_text(text + "\n")
216	
217	    log.info("demo.written", "demo bundled", path=str(out_path), bytes=out_path.stat().st_size, **summary)
218	    print(f"wrote {out_path} ({out_path.stat().st_size} bytes, {summary['events']} events)")
219	    print(
220	        "remember: add it to DEMOS in apps/web/src/lib/replay.ts so the home page offers it, "
221	        "then `pnpm test` in apps/web."
222	    )
223	    return 0

227	    raise SystemExit(main())

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Contract check for the browser edge: is the deployed SPA served, and can it talk to the harness?

Plain httpx — no browser, no playwright. It checks the three things that break the web app in ways
unit tests cannot see, because they only exist once both apps are deployed to Modal:

  1. the site is served at all, and `/config.json` carries a usable `harnessUrl`
  2. the harness answers `/health` and `/scenarios` *with CORS headers that allow the site origin*
     — including the preflight for `X-Faultline-User`, which the SPA sends on every JSON request
     (ARCHITECTURE.md §6). A missing `access-control-allow-headers` here is invisible to curl and
     fatal in a browser.
  3. the SSE route (`GET /runs/{id}/events`) answers with `text/event-stream`, an `access-control-
     allow-origin` the site can use, and honours the resume point as a QUERY param — EventSource
     cannot set `Last-Event-ID`, so `?last_event_id=` is the only resume the browser has.

Everything is reported, nothing is fatal: a missing web deployment is a normal outcome here (the
SPA is owned by another process) and is recorded as `skipped`, not as a failure.

Usage:
    python scripts/web_check.py [--web-url URL] [--harness-url URL] [--run-id r_...] [--label web]

Writes runs/<UTC ts>_<label>/{summary.json,checks.json,config.json,health.json,scenarios.json,
sse_head.txt} and exits 0 when every non-skipped check passed, 1 otherwise.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import pathlib
import sys
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Any

import httpx

REPO = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0, str(REPO / "packages" / "common"))

from faultline_common.log import get_logger  # noqa: E402

log = get_logger("web-check")

DEFAULT_WEB_URL = "https://appliedlabsai-local--faultline-web-site.us-east.modal.direct"
DEFAULT_HARNESS_URL = "https://appliedlabsai-local--faultline-harness-api.modal.run"

# Headers the SPA actually sends on its JSON requests (src/lib/api.ts).
REQUEST_HEADERS = "content-type,x-faultline-user"


def now_stamp() -> str:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ")


class Checks:
    """A flat list of {name, ok, detail} plus `skip`, so one bad URL never aborts the run."""

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.items: list[dict[str, Any]] = []

    def add(self, name: str, ok: bool, detail: str = "", **extra: Any) -> bool:
        self.items.append({"name": name, "ok": bool(ok), "detail": detail[:500], **extra})
        log.info("check", name, ok=bool(ok), detail=detail[:200] or None)
        return bool(ok)

    def skip(self, name: str, why: str) -> None:
        self.items.append({"name": name, "ok": None, "skipped": True, "detail": why[:500]})
        log.warn("check.skipped", name, detail=why[:200])

    @property
    def failed(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        return [c for c in self.items if c["ok"] is False]

    @property
    def passed(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        return [c for c in self.items if c["ok"] is True]

    @property
    def skipped(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        return [c for c in self.items if c["ok"] is None]

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Deploy all three Faultline apps to Modal, in dependency order, and print their URLs.
# Usage: scripts/deploy.sh [modal-environment]   (default: local)
set -euo pipefail

log() { printf '{"ts":"%s","svc":"deploy","lvl":"info","ev":"%s","msg":"%s"}\n' "$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ)" "$1" "$2"; }

  || { log deploy.error "secret ${FAULTLINE_ANTHROPIC_SECRET:-anthropic-secret} missing in env $ENV"; exit 1; }

printf '\nSANDBOX_ENV_URL=%s\nHARNESS_URL=%s\nWEB_URL=%s\n' "$SANDBOX_ENV_URL" "$HARNESS_URL" "$WEB_URL"

def check_sse(client: httpx.Client, harness: str, origin: str, run_id: str | None,
              checks: Checks, out: pathlib.Path) -> None:
    """SSE framing + resume, against a real finished run (the UI's live path in miniature)."""
    if run_id is None:
        try:
            res = client.get(f"{harness}/runs", timeout=45.0)
            runs = (res.json() or {}).get("runs") or []
            run_id = next((r["run_id"] for r in runs if r.get("status") == "ok"), None)
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
            checks.skip("harness.sse", f"could not pick a run: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}")
            return
    if run_id is None:
        checks.skip("harness.sse", "no finished run on this deployment to stream")
        return

    url = f"{harness}/runs/{run_id}/events"
    try:
        full = client.get(url, headers={"Origin": origin, "Accept": "text/event-stream"}, timeout=90.0)
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        checks.add("harness.sse_status", False, f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}")
        return

    (out / "sse_head.txt").write_text(
        "\n".join(f"{k}: {v}" for k, v in full.headers.items()) + "\n\n" + full.text[:20_000],
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    checks.add("harness.sse_status", full.status_code == 200, f"GET {url} -> {full.status_code}",
               run_id=run_id)
    checks.add("harness.sse_content_type",
               full.headers.get("content-type", "").startswith("text/event-stream"),
               f"content-type: {full.headers.get('content-type')}")
    ok, detail = cors_origin_allows(full.headers, origin)
    checks.add("harness.sse_cors", ok, detail)

1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
2	"""Verify, against the DEPLOYED harness, every wire fact apps/web relies on.
3	
4	    services/agent-harness/.venv/bin/python services/agent-harness/tools/check_contract.py \
5	        <finished run_id> <out.json> [harness base url]
6	
7	Reads an already-finished run (no model call, no cost), validates its JSON against
8	`faultline_common.schemas`, and exercises the SSE surface four ways (plain, ?after=,
9	?last_event_id=, Last-Event-ID header). Writes a JSON report; exits non-zero if any check fails.
10	
11	The result of the last run of this file is documented in docs/harness-contract.md.
12	"""
13	from __future__ import annotations
14	
15	import json
16	import pathlib
17	import sys
18	
19	import httpx
20	
21	from faultline_common.schemas import EvaluateResponse, Event, RunRecord
22	
23	RUN_ID = sys.argv[1]
24	OUT = pathlib.Path(sys.argv[2])
25	HARNESS = (sys.argv[3] if len(sys.argv) > 3
26	           else "https://appliedlabsai-local--faultline-harness-api.modal.run").rstrip("/")
27	
28	checks: list[dict] = []
29	
30	
31	def check(name: str, ok: bool, detail) -> None:
32	    checks.append({"check": name, "ok": bool(ok), "detail": detail})
33	    print(f"[{'PASS' if ok else 'FAIL'}] {name}: {str(detail)[:170]}")
34	
35	
36	def frames(text: str) -> list[dict]:
37	    out, ev, eid, data = [], "message", None, []
38	    for line in text.split("\n"):
39	        line = line.rstrip("\r")
40	        if line == "":
41	            if data:
42	                raw = "\n".join(data)
43	                try:
44	                    payload = json.loads(raw)
45	                except json.JSONDecodeError:
46	                    payload = {"raw": raw}
47	                out.append({"id": eid, "event": ev, "data": payload})
48	            ev, eid, data = "message", None, []
49	            continue
50	        if line.startswith(":"):
51	            continue
52	        f, _, v = line.partition(":")
53	        v = v[1:] if v.startswith(" ") else v
54	        if f == "event":
55	            ev = v
56	        elif f == "data":
57	            data.append(v)
58	        elif f == "id":
59	            eid = v
60	    return out
61	
62	
63	with httpx.Client(follow_redirects=True, timeout=60.0) as c:
64	    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- REST shapes
65	    sc = c.get(f"{HARNESS}/scenarios").json()
66	    check("GET /scenarios -> {scenarios:[...]}", isinstance(sc, dict) and isinstance(sc.get("scenarios"), list),
67	          sorted(s["id"] for s in sc.get("scenarios", [])))
68	
69	    rl = c.get(f"{HARNESS}/runs").json()
70	    check("GET /runs -> {runs:[...]}", isinstance(rl, dict) and isinstance(rl.get("runs"), list),
71	          f"{len(rl.get('runs', []))} runs; keys={sorted(rl['runs'][0].keys()) if rl.get('runs') else None}")
72	
73	    rec = c.get(f"{HARNESS}/runs/{RUN_ID}").json()
74	    check("GET /runs/{id} -> whole RunRecord", rec.get("run_id") == RUN_ID and "events" in rec,
75	          {k: rec.get(k) for k in ("status", "scenario_id", "model", "steps", "episode_id")})
76	
77	    events = rec["events"]
78	    ids = [e["id"] for e in events]
79	    check("Event.id 0-based and contiguous", ids == list(range(len(events))), f"0..{len(events)-1}")
80	
81	    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- pydantic contract
82	    try:
83	        RunRecord.model_validate(rec)
84	        check("RunRecord validates against faultline_common.schemas", True, "ok")
85	    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
86	        check("RunRecord validates against faultline_common.schemas", False, str(exc)[:300])
87	
88	    bad = []
89	    for e in events:
90	        try:
91	            Event.model_validate(e)
92	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
93	            bad.append((e.get("id"), str(exc)[:120]))
94	    check("every Event validates against faultline_common.schemas", not bad,
95	          f"{len(events)} events, {len(bad)} invalid" + (f" {bad[:3]}" if bad else ""))
96	
97	    try:
98	        EvaluateResponse.model_validate(rec["evaluation"])
99	        check("EvaluateResponse validates against faultline_common.schemas", True,
100	              {"score": rec["evaluation"]["score"], "passed": rec["evaluation"]["passed"],
101	               "checks": len(rec["evaluation"]["checks"]), "ledger": len(rec["evaluation"]["ledger"])})
102	    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
103	        check("EvaluateResponse validates against faultline_common.schemas", False, str(exc)[:300])
104	
105	    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- event payload shapes
106	    tr = [e for e in events if e["type"] == "tool.result"]
107	    structured = [e for e in tr if e["data"].get("tool") in {"read_file", "write_file", "list_dir", "run_command"}]
108	    json_strings = []
109	    for e in structured:
110	        out = e["data"].get("output")
111	        ok = isinstance(out, str)
112	        if ok:
113	            try:
114	                json.loads(out)
115	                json_strings.append(True)
116	            except json.JSONDecodeError:
117	                json_strings.append(False)
118	        else:
119	            json_strings.append(False)
120	    check("tool.result.data.output is a JSON string for structured tools",
121	          bool(structured) and all(json_strings),
122	          f"{sum(json_strings)}/{len(structured)} parse as JSON")
123	
124	    ff = [e for e in events if e["type"] == "fault.fired"]
125	    check("fault.fired data is the FaultFired object at top level",
126	          bool(ff) and all({"step", "kind", "path", "mode"} <= set(e["data"]) for e in ff),
127	          [e["data"] for e in ff])
128	
129	    llm = [e for e in events if e["type"] == "llm.call"]
130	    check("llm.call carries attempt/model/stop_reason/request_id/usage/duration_ms",
131	          bool(llm) and all({"attempt", "model", "stop_reason", "request_id", "usage", "duration_ms"}
132	                            <= set(e["data"]) for e in llm),
133	          f"{len(llm)} calls; first={llm[0]['data'] if llm else None}")
134	
135	    # A trap for the UI: Event.step is the harness loop turn, data.step (fault.fired) is the
136	    # sandbox-env ledger index. They are different counters and must not be conflated.
137	    check("fault.fired: Event.step (loop turn) != data.step (ledger index) is expected",
138	          all(isinstance(e["data"].get("step"), int) and isinstance(e.get("step"), int) for e in ff),
139	          [{"event.step": e["step"], "data.step": e["data"]["step"], "kind": e["data"]["kind"]} for e in ff])
140	
141	    ev_types = sorted({e["type"] for e in events})
142	    check("event types emitted", True, ev_types)
143	
144	    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- SSE
145	    r = c.get(f"{HARNESS}/runs/{RUN_ID}/events")
146	    fr = frames(r.text)
147	    check("SSE starts with retry:", r.text.startswith("retry: 1000"), r.text[:20].replace("\n", "\\n"))
148	    data_frames = [f for f in fr if f["event"] != "done"]
149	    check("SSE frame id == Event.id",
150	          [f["id"] for f in data_frames] == [str(e["id"]) for e in events],
151	          f"{len(data_frames)} frames, first id={data_frames[0]['id']}, last id={data_frames[-1]['id']}")
152	    check("SSE names the frame with event: <Event.type>",
153	          all(f["event"] == f["data"]["type"] for f in data_frames),
154	          sorted({f["event"] for f in data_frames}))
155	    done = [f for f in fr if f["event"] == "done"]
156	    check("terminal `event: done` with reason=finished",
157	          len(done) == 1 and done[0]["data"].get("reason") == "finished"
158	          and done[0]["data"].get("status") in {"ok", "error", "truncated"},
159	          done[0]["data"] if done else None)
160	
161	    n = len(events)
162	    r2 = frames(c.get(f"{HARNESS}/runs/{RUN_ID}/events?after={n-3}").text)
163	    check("?after=<n> resumes", [f["id"] for f in r2 if f["event"] != "done"] == [str(n - 2), str(n - 1)],
164	          [f["id"] for f in r2 if f["event"] != "done"])
165	
166	    r3 = frames(c.get(f"{HARNESS}/runs/{RUN_ID}/events?last_event_id={n-3}").text)
167	    check("?last_event_id=<n> resumes", [f["id"] for f in r3 if f["event"] != "done"] == [str(n - 2), str(n - 1)],
168	          [f["id"] for f in r3 if f["event"] != "done"])
169	
170	    r4 = frames(c.get(f"{HARNESS}/runs/{RUN_ID}/events", headers={"Last-Event-ID": str(n - 3)}).text)
171	    check("Last-Event-ID header resumes", [f["id"] for f in r4 if f["event"] != "done"] == [str(n - 2), str(n - 1)],
172	          [f["id"] for f in r4 if f["event"] != "done"])
173	
174	    r5 = c.get(f"{HARNESS}/runs/does-not-exist/events")
175	    check("SSE 404s an unknown run", r5.status_code == 404, r5.status_code)
176	
177	    cors = c.request("OPTIONS", f"{HARNESS}/runs",
178	                     headers={"Origin": "https://example.org",
179	                              "Access-Control-Request-Method": "POST",
180	                              "Access-Control-Request-Headers": "content-type,x-faultline-user"})
181	    check("CORS preflight allows POST /runs from any origin",
182	          cors.status_code < 400 and cors.headers.get("access-control-allow-origin") == "*",
183	          {"status": cors.status_code, "allow-origin": cors.headers.get("access-control-allow-origin"),
184	           "allow-headers": cors.headers.get("access-control-allow-headers")})
185	
186	    bad = c.post(f"{HARNESS}/runs", json={"scenario_id": "nope"})
187	    check("POST /runs 400s an unknown scenario", bad.status_code == 400, bad.json())
188	
189	    badm = c.post(f"{HARNESS}/runs", json={"scenario_id": "lost-ack", "model": "gpt-4"})
190	    check("POST /runs 400s a model off the allowlist", badm.status_code == 400, badm.json())
191	
192	failed = [c_ for c_ in checks if not c_["ok"]]
193	OUT.write_text(json.dumps({"harness": HARNESS, "run_id": RUN_ID, "checks": checks,
194	                           "passed": len(checks) - len(failed), "failed": len(failed)}, indent=2))
195	print(f"\n{len(checks) - len(failed)}/{len(checks)} checks passed -> {OUT}")
196	sys.exit(1 if failed else 0)

1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
2	"""Live check of the harness Store, identity scoping and the conversation routes (PLAN.md §2.9).
3	
4	    services/agent-harness/.venv/bin/python services/agent-harness/tools/check_store.py \
5	        --user u_<uuid4> --out runs/<ts>_store/store_check.json
6	
7	Every assertion runs against the DEPLOYED harness over HTTP — nothing here imports the service.
8	Exit code 0 iff every check passes. Run it once before a redeploy and once after: the same command
9	then proves the database survived (`--expect-conversations N`, `--expect-run <run_id>`).
10	"""
11	
12	from __future__ import annotations
13	
14	import argparse
15	import json
16	import pathlib
17	import sys
18	import uuid
19	from typing import Any
20	
21	import httpx
22	
23	REPO_ROOT = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]
24	sys.path.insert(0, str(REPO_ROOT / "packages" / "common"))
25	
26	from faultline_common.schemas import (  # noqa: E402
27	    Conversation,
28	    ConversationDetail,
29	    ConversationSummary,
30	    RunRecord,
31	)
32	
33	DEFAULT_HARNESS = "https://appliedlabsai-local--faultline-harness-api.modal.run"
34	
35	
36	class Checks:
37	    def __init__(self) -> None:
38	        self.rows: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
39	
40	    def add(self, ok: bool, name: str, detail: Any = "") -> bool:
41	        self.rows.append({"name": name, "ok": bool(ok), "detail": str(detail)[:400]})
42	        print(f"[{'PASS' if ok else 'FAIL'}] {name}" + (f"  {str(detail)[:160]}" if detail else ""))
43	        return bool(ok)
44	
45	    @property
46	    def passed(self) -> int:
47	        return sum(1 for r in self.rows if r["ok"])
48	
49	    @property
50	    def failed(self) -> int:
51	        return sum(1 for r in self.rows if not r["ok"])
52	
53	
54	def main() -> int:
55	    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Check the harness Store against a deployed harness.")
56	    ap.add_argument("--harness", default=DEFAULT_HARNESS)
57	    ap.add_argument("--user", required=True, help="the browser identity whose data must be there")
58	    ap.add_argument("--expect-conversations", type=int, default=1)
59	    ap.add_argument("--expect-run", default=None, help="run id that must still be readable")
60	    ap.add_argument("--out", default=None, help="write the evidence JSON here")
61	    ap.add_argument("--label", default="store_check")
62	    args = ap.parse_args()
63	
64	    base = args.harness.rstrip("/")
65	    me = {"X-Faultline-User": args.user}
66	    stranger = {"X-Faultline-User": f"u_{uuid.uuid4()}"}
67	    c = Checks()
68	    evidence: dict[str, Any] = {"harness": base, "user": args.user, "label": args.label}
69	
70	    with httpx.Client(timeout=60.0, follow_redirects=True) as http:
71	        # ------------------------------------------------------------------ health
72	        health = http.get(f"{base}/health").json()
73	        evidence["health"] = health
74	        store = (health.get("detail") or {}).get("store") or {}
75	        c.add(store.get("ok") is True, "health.store.ok", store.get("error") or "")
76	        c.add("last_checkpoint_at" in store, "health.store.last_checkpoint_at",
77	              store.get("last_checkpoint_at"))
78	        c.add(health.get("has_provider_key") is False, "health.has_provider_key is false")
79	
80	        # ------------------------------------------------------------------ identity
81	        mine = http.get(f"{base}/me", headers=me).json()
82	        evidence["me"] = mine
83	        c.add(mine.get("user_id") == args.user, "GET /me returns this user", mine.get("user_id"))
84	        c.add(http.get(f"{base}/me").status_code == 400, "GET /me without an id is a 400")
85	        c.add(http.get(f"{base}/me", headers={"X-Faultline-User": "bogus"}).status_code == 400,
86	              "GET /me with a malformed id is a 400")
87	
88	        # ------------------------------------------------------------------ conversations
89	        listing = http.get(f"{base}/conversations", headers=me).json()
90	        rows = listing if isinstance(listing, list) else listing.get("conversations") or []
91	        evidence["conversations"] = rows
92	        c.add(len(rows) >= args.expect_conversations,
93	              f"GET /conversations has >= {args.expect_conversations} for this user", len(rows))
94	        for row in rows:
95	            ConversationSummary.model_validate(row)
96	        c.add(True, "every conversation row validates as ConversationSummary", len(rows))
97	        c.add(mine.get("conversations") == len(rows), "GET /me count matches the list",
98	              f"{mine.get('conversations')} vs {len(rows)}")
99	
100	        strangers = http.get(f"{base}/conversations", headers=stranger).json()
101	        stranger_rows = strangers if isinstance(strangers, list) else strangers.get("conversations") or []
102	        c.add(stranger_rows == [], "a fresh browser id sees an empty list", len(stranger_rows))
103	
104	        if rows:
105	            cid = rows[0]["id"]
106	            detail_raw = http.get(f"{base}/conversations/{cid}", headers=me).json()
107	            evidence["conversation_detail"] = detail_raw
108	            detail = ConversationDetail.model_validate(detail_raw)
109	            c.add(True, "GET /conversations/{id} validates as ConversationDetail",
110	                  f"{len(detail.messages)} messages / {len(detail.runs)} runs")
111	            c.add(len(detail.messages) > 0, "the persisted transcript is not empty",
112	                  len(detail.messages))
113	            blocks = [b for m in detail.messages for b in m.blocks]
114	            tool_uses = {b.tool_use_id for b in blocks if b.type == "tool_use"}
115	            results = {b.tool_use_id for b in blocks if b.type == "tool_result"}
116	            c.add(tool_uses and tool_uses == results,
117	                  "every tool_use block has its tool_result (joined by tool_use_id)",
118	                  f"{len(tool_uses)} calls / {len(results)} results")
119	            c.add(detail.messages[0].role == "user" and detail.messages[0].blocks[0].type == "text",
120	                  "the transcript starts with the task prompt as a user message",
121	                  (detail.messages[0].blocks[0].text or "")[:80])
122	            c.add([m.seq for m in detail.messages] == sorted(m.seq for m in detail.messages),
123	                  "messages are ordered by seq")
124	            c.add(http.get(f"{base}/conversations/{cid}", headers=stranger).status_code == 404,
125	                  "another browser id gets 404 (not 403) for this conversation")
126	
127	        # ------------------------------------------------------------------ the run itself
128	        if args.expect_run:
129	            got = http.get(f"{base}/runs/{args.expect_run}", headers=me)
130	            c.add(got.status_code == 200, f"GET /runs/{args.expect_run} still exists", got.status_code)
131	            if got.status_code == 200:
132	                record = RunRecord.model_validate(got.json())
133	                evidence["run"] = {"run_id": record.run_id, "status": record.status,
134	                                   "events": len(record.events),
135	                                   "score": (record.evaluation.score if record.evaluation else None),
136	                                   "conversation_id": record.conversation_id,
137	                                   "usage": record.usage.model_dump()}
138	                c.add(record.status in {"ok", "truncated"}, "the run is still terminal", record.status)
139	                c.add(len(record.events) > 0, "its events survived", len(record.events))
140	                c.add([e.id for e in record.events] == list(range(len(record.events))),
141	                      "event ids are contiguous and 0-based (SSE resume depends on it)")
142	                c.add(record.evaluation is not None and record.evaluation.score is not None,
143	                      "the evaluation survived",
144	                      record.evaluation.score if record.evaluation else None)
145	                c.add(record.user_id == args.user, "the run is still owned by this user",
146	                      record.user_id)
147	                c.add(http.get(f"{base}/runs/{args.expect_run}", headers=stranger).status_code == 404,
148	                      "another browser id gets 404 for this run")
149	                c.add(record.usage.cache_read_input_tokens >= 0, "usage carries the cache counters",
150	                      record.usage.model_dump())
151	
152	        scoped = http.get(f"{base}/runs", headers=me).json().get("runs") or []
153	        c.add(all(r.get("run_id") for r in scoped), "GET /runs rows carry run_id", len(scoped))
154	        c.add(len(http.get(f"{base}/runs", headers=stranger).json().get("runs") or []) == 0,
155	              "GET /runs is scoped: a fresh id sees nothing")
156	
157	        # ------------------------------------------------------------------ CRUD on a throwaway
158	        made = http.post(f"{base}/conversations", json={"scenario_id": "lost-ack"}, headers=me)
159	        c.add(made.status_code == 200, "POST /conversations", made.status_code)
160	        if made.status_code == 200:
161	            conv = Conversation.model_validate(made.json())
162	            patched = http.patch(f"{base}/conversations/{conv.id}", json={"title": "renamed by check"},
163	                                 headers=me).json()
164	            c.add(patched.get("title") == "renamed by check", "PATCH /conversations/{id} renames")
165	            c.add(http.delete(f"{base}/conversations/{conv.id}", headers=me).json() == {"archived": True},
166	                  "DELETE /conversations/{id} archives")
167	            after = http.get(f"{base}/conversations", headers=me).json()
168	            after_rows = after if isinstance(after, list) else after.get("conversations") or []
169	            c.add(all(r["id"] != conv.id for r in after_rows),
170	                  "the archived conversation is gone from the list", len(after_rows))
171	
172	    evidence["checks"] = c.rows
173	    evidence["summary"] = {"passed": c.passed, "failed": c.failed}
174	    if args.out:
175	        path = pathlib.Path(args.out)
176	        path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
177	        path.write_text(json.dumps(evidence, indent=2, default=str))
178	        print(f"\nevidence: {path}")
179	    print(f"STORE CHECK {'PASS' if c.failed == 0 else 'FAIL'} :: {c.passed} passed, {c.failed} failed")
180	    return 0 if c.failed == 0 else 1

1	# What the harness emits about failures (PLAN.md §2.11)
2	
3	Contract: `docs/error-taxonomy.md` (labels, statuses) · `services/sandbox-env/FAULTS.md` (what each
4	injector really does) · `packages/common/faultline_common/schemas.py` (wire types).
5	This file is the harness-side reader's guide: every field below is **additive**; no existing field
6	changed shape.
7	
8	## `tool.result.data`
9	
10	| field | always? | meaning |
11	|---|---|---|
12	| `outcome` | yes | `executed` · `failed` (ran and reported failure) · `not_executed` (refused before the sandbox saw it) · `unknown` (may or may not have run) |
13	| `error_class` | iff `is_error` | `ErrorClass` — `{origin, layer, code, kind?, label, outcome_known, side_effect_applied, detail}`. `label` is from the taxonomy table and is rendered verbatim. |
14	| `attempts` | yes | how many times the harness dispatched this call. `> 1` only ever happens for a failed **connect** (the request provably never left the worker). Timeouts, aborted streams and every other unknown-outcome failure are handed to the agent as-is. |
15	| `sandbox` | yes | `{id: <short sandbox id>, alive: bool}`. `alive: false` means this call found the sandbox gone. |
16	| `fault` | iff a fault fired | the `FaultFired` from `observe()`, with `origin`/`layer`/`description` filled in. |
17	| `synthetic` | only on a resume | `true` on the `EHARNESS` result a resuming worker writes for the call that was in flight. `reported_to_gym` says whether `POST /episodes/{id}/interruptions` succeeded. |
18	
19	The **agent** still sees only the OS/HTTP-style `output` and `error_code`. Everything above is for
20	the ledger, the events and the UI.
21	
22	## Classification rules (harness/classify.py)
23	
24	1. `EHARNESS` → `real/harness`, outcome `unknown` (a previous worker died mid-call).
25	2. our own transport failed (connect / client timeout / client abort) → `real/transport`,
26	   `ETRANSPORT` (or `ETIMEDOUT` for a client timeout), outcome `unknown`.
27	3. `ESANDBOX` → `real/sandbox`, outcome `not_executed`, `sandbox.alive: false`, plus an
28	   `episode.sandbox {status: terminated}` event. An `EINTERNAL` whose text names a dead sandbox
29	   ("Task has already finished", `NotFoundError`, `WorkspaceError`, …) is upgraded to `ESANDBOX`
30	   and says so in `detail` — that is what run `r_ccda8780cbee` was mislabelled as.
31	4. a fault fired for this call → the injector's own row: injected `missing_file`/`denied_write` are
32	   `not_executed` with no side effect, injected `ack_lost` is `unknown` **with** the side effect
33	   applied, staged (sticky) `missing_file` is a real OS error → `staged/filesystem`, `failed`.
34	5. no fault + `ENOENT`/`EACCES` → `real/filesystem`, `failed`; `EINVAL` → `not_executed`.
35	6. `EINTERNAL`/`ENOEPISODE` → `real/boundary`, `not_executed`.
36	
37	A server-sent `ETIMEDOUT` that `observe()` cannot explain is reported as `real/transport`, not as an
38	injected ack_lost: claiming an injection we cannot see would be a lie. (When `observe()` itself is
39	unreachable the code is all we have, and `detail` says so.)
40	
41	## Run-level
42	
43	- `run.finished.data` gained `evaluation_status` (`ok|failed|skipped`), `evaluation_error?`,
44	  `worker_generation`, `interruptions` (a count) and — whenever the status is
45	  `unevaluated|interrupted|error` — `error_class`, so a live SSE viewer can explain the ending
46	  without re-fetching `GET /runs/{id}`.
47	- Statuses: `ok` implies a score (a loop that finished but could not be graded is `unevaluated`);
48	  `interrupted` means a real interruption ended the run and nothing resumed it.
49	- `RunRecord` gained `error_class`, `interruptions[]` and `worker_generation`, all returned by
50	  `GET /runs/{id}` (defaulted to `null` / `[]` / `1` for runs that predate the taxonomy).
51	- Interruptions are written to the run row **as they happen**, not only at the end: the SSE stream
52	  closes the instant `run.finished` flips the status, so a client fetching the record on the `done`
53	  frame would otherwise race the final update.
54	
55	## Events
56	
57	- `interruption` — an `Interruption` (always real). `planned: true` = a scenario `harness_faults`
58	  entry (or a `POST /runs` `harness_faults`) triggered it deliberately.
59	- `run.resumed` — `{worker_generation, resumed_from_event_id, resumed_at, step, dangling_tool_use_id?}`.
60	- `episode.sandbox` — `{sandbox_id, status: alive|terminated, reason, step}`.
61	- `log` with `ev: "ledger.resolution"` — emitted once after grading. `data.resolutions` is
62	  `[{tool_use_id, step, ledger_step, tool, path, outcome, origin, error_code, interrupted,
63	  side_effect_applied}]`, which upgrades an `unknown` outcome to the truth the ledger holds
64	  (`ack_lost` → applied, `short_circuit` → not applied, an interrupted row → whether it completed).
65	  Earlier events are **never** rewritten; the stream stays append-only.
66	
67	## Real interruptions the harness inflicts on itself
68	
69	`Scenario.harness_faults[]` (from `GET /scenarios`, published by sandbox-env) or, for ops and
70	proofs, `POST /runs {"harness_faults": [...]}`:
71	
72	- `worker_crash` — dispatch the matching call on a worker thread, wait `after_ms`, then
73	  `os._exit(137)`. The `tool.call` event and a `harness_faults_fired` marker are persisted first, so
74	  the worker that takes over finds the dangling call and does **not** crash again on it.
75	  `run_episode` carries `modal.Retries(max_retries=2, initial_delay=1.0, backoff_coefficient=1.0)`,
76	  which re-invokes it with the same `(run_id, req)`. A run that already reached a terminal status
77	  returns immediately, so a retry can never double-run an episode.
78	- `transport_abort` — cancel the in-flight request after `after_ms` (`asyncio.wait_for` on that one
79	  call); the server completes it. `real/transport`, outcome `unknown`, no crash. The MCP session is
80	  rebuilt afterwards because a cancelled streamable-HTTP request can leave it unusable.
81	
82	## Proving it
83	
84	```bash
85	services/agent-harness/.venv/bin/python -m pytest -o addopts= -q      # 209 unit tests, no network
86	services/agent-harness/.venv/bin/python scripts/prove_interruptions.py  # 39 live checks, 4 episodes
87	services/agent-harness/.venv/bin/python scripts/prove_interruptions.py --case sandbox-loss
88	```
89	
90	`sandbox-loss` terminates exactly one Modal Sandbox — the one belonging to the episode it just
91	created — to reproduce the `r_ccda8780cbee` condition. It never enumerates or touches anything else;
92	that is what `reap` does, and `reap` kills other people's live episodes.

"""Event sink bookkeeping, the run index, and the system prompt's hard constraints."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from faultline_common.schemas import Event

from harness import config
from harness.events import EventSink, now_iso
from harness.prompts import SUBMIT_TOOL, SYSTEM_PROMPT, build_task_message, system_blocks
from harness.store import RunStore, memory_store


def sink_for(store: RunStore | None = None) -> tuple[EventSink, dict[str, Any], RunStore]:
    store = store or RunStore(memory_store())
    record: dict[str, Any] = {"run_id": "r_1", "status": "queued", "scenario_id": "lost-ack",
                              "model": "claude-haiku-4-5", "created_at": now_iso(), "events": []}
    store.create(record)
    return EventSink(store, record), record, store


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- events


def test_ids_are_contiguous_and_timestamps_are_iso_z() -> None:
    sink, record, _store = sink_for()
    for i in range(4):
        sink.emit("turn.text", {"text": f"t{i}"})
    assert [e["id"] for e in record["events"]] == [0, 1, 2, 3]
    sink.set_status("running", started_at=now_iso())
    assert record["status"] == "running"
    stored = store.get("r_1")
    assert stored["status"] == "running"
    assert stored["started_at"]


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- store index


def test_index_is_most_recent_first_and_capped() -> None:
    store = RunStore(memory_store())
    for i in range(3):
        store.create({"run_id": f"r_{i}", "status": "ok", "scenario_id": "lost-ack",
                      "model": "claude-haiku-4-5", "created_at": now_iso(), "events": [],
                      "evaluation": {"score": 60.0 + i}})
    listing = store.list_runs(limit=2)
    assert [r["run_id"] for r in listing] == ["r_2", "r_1"]
    assert listing[0]["score"] == 62.0


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- prompt


def test_system_prompt_is_short_enough_to_stay_cheap() -> None:
    assert len(SYSTEM_PROMPT.split()) <= 350


def test_system_prompt_never_names_the_fault_mechanism() -> None:
    """The agent must treat failures as ordinary; naming the mechanism would teach it the answer."""
    lowered = SYSTEM_PROMPT.lower()
    for banned in ("fault", "inject", "scenario", "grader", "harness", "ack_lost", "denied_write",
                   "missing_file", "on purpose", "deliberate"):
        assert banned not in lowered, f"system prompt leaks {banned!r}"

ACK_LOST_FAULT = {"step": 2, "kind": "ack_lost", "path": "CHANGELOG.md", "mode": "transient"}


def types_of(record: dict[str, Any], *, drop_logs: bool = True) -> list[str]:
    return [e["type"] for e in record["events"] if not (drop_logs and e["type"] == "log")]


def events_of(record: dict[str, Any], type_: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:


def test_new_faults_is_a_suffix_delta() -> None:
    seen = [ACK_LOST_FAULT]
    later = {"step": 5, "kind": "denied_write", "path": "src/limits.py", "mode": "transient"}
    assert new_faults([ACK_LOST_FAULT], seen) == []
    assert new_faults([ACK_LOST_FAULT, later], seen) == [later]


def test_inferred_fault_only_for_known_codes() -> None:
    assert inferred_fault(3, "ETIMEDOUT", {"path": "CHANGELOG.md"})["kind"] == "ack_lost"
    assert inferred_fault(3, "ETIMEDOUT", {})["inferred"] is True
    assert inferred_fault(3, "EINTERNAL", {}) is None
    assert inferred_fault(3, None, {}) is None

def test_lost_ack_episode_emits_the_expected_sequence() -> None:
    client, mcp, gym = build_lost_ack_run()
    record = run(client, mcp, gym)

    assert record["status"] == "ok"
    assert types_of(record) == [
        "run.started",
        "episode.reset",
        # one `llm.call` per messages.create, ahead of everything that turn produced
        "llm.call",
        "turn.text",
        "tool.call",
        "tool.result",
        "llm.call",
        "tool.call",
        "tool.result",
        "fault.fired",
        "workspace.diff",
        "llm.call",
        "turn.text",
        "tool.call",
        "tool.result",
        "llm.call",
        "tool.call",
        "tool.result",
        "episode.evaluated",
        "run.finished",
    ]
    assert record["usage"]["input_tokens"] == 400 and record["usage"]["output_tokens"] == 80
    # schemas.Usage declares both cache counters; the fake model reports neither.
    assert record["usage"]["cache_read_input_tokens"] == 0
    assert record["usage"]["cache_creation_input_tokens"] == 0
    assert record["evaluation"]["score"] == 100.0
    assert record["summary"].startswith("Bumped version")


def test_every_event_validates_against_the_shared_schema() -> None:
    client, mcp, gym = build_lost_ack_run()
    record = run(client, mcp, gym)
    ids = []
    for raw in record["events"]:
        event = Event.model_validate(raw)
        assert event.run_id == "r_test"
        assert event.ts.endswith("Z")
        ids.append(event.id)
    assert ids == list(range(len(ids)))  # contiguous, 0-based: SSE resume depends on this


def test_fault_fired_comes_from_the_observe_delta_not_the_tool_text() -> None:
    client, mcp, gym = build_lost_ack_run()
    record = run(client, mcp, gym)

    fired = events_of(record, "fault.fired")
    assert len(fired) == 1
    assert fired[0]["data"]["kind"] == "ack_lost"
    assert fired[0]["data"]["path"] == "CHANGELOG.md"
    assert "inferred" not in fired[0]["data"]  # ledger ground truth, not a guess from the error code

    timed_out = [e for e in events_of(record, "tool.result") if e["data"]["is_error"]]
    assert len(timed_out) == 1
    assert timed_out[0]["data"]["error_code"] == "ETIMEDOUT"
    assert timed_out[0]["data"]["fault"]["kind"] == "ack_lost"

def test_classification_table(case: str, kwargs: dict[str, Any], expected: Any) -> None:
    want_outcome, want_class = expected
    outcome, ec = ec_of(**kwargs)
    assert outcome == want_outcome, case
    if want_class is None:
        assert ec is None, case
        return
    origin, layer, code, known, side_effect = want_class
    assert (ec["origin"], ec["layer"], ec["code"]) == (origin, layer, code), case
    assert ec["outcome_known"] is known, case
    assert ec["side_effect_applied"] is side_effect, case
    assert ec["label"] == label_for(origin, layer, code), case

def test_worker_crash_kills_the_worker_only_after_the_request_is_dispatched() -> None:
    """The write must already be at sandbox-env when we die — that is the whole point."""
    gym = FakeGym(scenario=crash_scenario(WORKER_CRASH))
    order: list[str] = []
    store = RunStore(memory_store())
    persisted: dict[str, Any] = {}

    def handler(name: str, args: dict[str, Any]):
        order.append(f"dispatch:{name}")
        return ok_result({"path": args.get("path"), "bytes_written": 5, "sha256": "e" * 64})

    def crash(code: int) -> None:
        order.append(f"crash:{code}")
        persisted.update(copy.deepcopy(store.get("r_crash") or {}))
        raise WorkerDied()

    client = FakeAnthropic([
        response(tool_use("tu_1", "write_file", path="CHANGELOG.md", content="x")),
        response(tool_use("tu_2", "submit", summary="unreachable")),
    ])
    with pytest.raises(WorkerDied):
        run(client, FakeMCP(handler), gym, store=store, run_id="r_crash", crash=crash,
            scenario_id="worker-crash")

    assert order == ["dispatch:write_file", "crash:137"]

    # What the dying worker left behind is what the next one has to work with:
    types = [e["type"] for e in persisted["events"]]
    assert types.count("tool.call") == 1
    assert "tool.result" not in types                       # the dangling call
    assert persisted["harness_faults_fired"] == ["worker_crash:write_file:CHANGELOG.md:1"]
    assert persisted["status"] == "running"


def test_transport_abort_cancels_the_call_and_reports_etransport_unknown() -> None:
    gym = FakeGym(scenario=crash_scenario(TRANSPORT_ABORT))

    def handler(name: str, args: dict[str, Any], abort_after_ms: int | None = None):
        if abort_after_ms is not None:
            return transport_result("abort")
        return ok_result({"path": args.get("path"), "content": "x", "size": 1, "sha256": "f" * 64})

    mcp = FakeMCP(handler)
    client = FakeAnthropic([
        response(tool_use("tu_1", "write_file", path="CHANGELOG.md", content="x")),
        response(tool_use("tu_2", "read_file", path="CHANGELOG.md")),
        response(tool_use("tu_3", "submit", summary="verified before rewriting")),
    ])
    record = run(client, mcp, gym, scenario_id="worker-crash")

    assert mcp.aborts[0] == 10                       # the abort deadline reached the client
    assert mcp.reconnects == 1                       # a cancelled stream is rebuilt, not reused
    aborted = events_of(record, "tool.result")[0]["data"]
    assert aborted["error_code"] == "ETRANSPORT"
    assert aborted["outcome"] == "unknown"
    assert aborted["error_class"]["layer"] == "transport"
    assert aborted["error_class"]["outcome_known"] is False
    assert record["status"] == "ok"                  # no crash: the run continues and is graded

def test_get_run_and_list_runs(ctx) -> None:

# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- SSE


def parse_sse(text: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    frames = []
    for chunk in text.split("\n\n"):
        if not chunk.strip() or chunk.startswith(":") or chunk.startswith("retry:"):
            continue
        frame: dict[str, Any] = {}
        for line in chunk.splitlines():
            if line.startswith("id: "):
                frame["id"] = int(line[4:])
            elif line.startswith("event: "):
                frame["event"] = line[7:]
            elif line.startswith("data: "):
                frame["data"] = json.loads(line[6:])
        frames.append(frame)
    return frames

def test_runs_are_scoped_to_the_caller(ctx) -> None:
    client, _store, _gym, _spawn = ctx
    mine = client.post("/runs", json={"scenario_id": "lost-ack"}, headers=as_user()).json()["run_id"]
    theirs = client.post("/runs", json={"scenario_id": "lost-ack"}, headers=as_user(OTHER)).json()["run_id"]

    assert [r["run_id"] for r in client.get("/runs", headers=as_user()).json()["runs"]] == [mine]
    assert client.get(f"/runs/{mine}", headers=as_user()).json()["run_id"] == mine
    assert client.get(f"/runs/{theirs}", headers=as_user()).status_code == 404
    assert client.get(f"/runs/{theirs}/events", headers=as_user()).status_code == 404
    # No header at all = the CLI / evidence scripts: unscoped, so both runs are visible.
    assert len(client.get("/runs").json()["runs"]) == 2
    assert client.get(f"/runs/{theirs}").status_code == 200

Tool reply2026-09-13T00:40:59.871Z
"""Who caused a failure, and which layer really failed.

The agent only ever sees an OS/HTTP-style code (`ENOENT`, `EACCES`, `ETIMEDOUT`, `ESANDBOX`…) —
that is the experiment. Everything *else* in the system (the ledger, `observe`, the run events, the
browser) gets provenance, and this module is the single place where that provenance is decided, so
the ledger, the public scenario projection and the UI cannot drift apart.

Three origins (`faultline_common.schemas.ErrorOrigin`):

``injected``
    sandbox-env short-circuited the call at the tool boundary, or executed it fully and withheld the
    acknowledgement. Nothing real failed; the sandbox is healthy.
``staged``
    the scenario really changed the world at reset (a sticky ``missing_file`` leaves the file out of
    the workspace tar). The ENOENT the agent gets afterwards is a *genuine* OS error from the
    sandbox — there is no interception at all — but it is not an accident either, so it is neither
    "injected" nor "real".
``real``
    an unplanned failure of a real component: the Modal Sandbox, the transport, the harness worker,
    the model API, the gym control plane, or the sandbox's own filesystem.

The description strings are quoted verbatim from ``services/sandbox-env/FAULTS.md``; the layer
mapping is the one ``docs/error-taxonomy.md`` renders labels from. Keep all three in sync.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from faultline_common.schemas import (
    ErrorCode,
    ErrorLayer,
    ErrorOrigin,
    FaultPlan,
    FaultPublic,
    HarnessFault,
)

# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- descriptions
# One plain-language sentence per failure class, for the UI. FAULTS.md is the source text.

#: ack_lost — the only injected fault with a real side effect.
DESC_ACK_LOST = "the write was applied but the acknowledgement was withheld"
#: denied_write — refused at the boundary, nothing executed.
DESC_DENIED_WRITE = "the write was refused before reaching the sandbox; nothing was written"
#: missing_file, transient — refused at the boundary; the file never moved.
DESC_MISSING_TRANSIENT = "the read was refused before reaching the sandbox; the file is still on disk"
#: missing_file, sticky — realised at reset; every later ENOENT is the real OS talking.
DESC_MISSING_STICKY = (
    "the file was deleted when the episode was created; this ENOENT is a real OS error"
)
#: harness_faults (planned, but genuinely real failures of the harness process / the transport)
DESC_WORKER_CRASH = (
    "the harness worker process was killed while the call was in flight; the call reached the "
    "sandbox and a fresh worker resumed the run"
)
DESC_TRANSPORT_ABORT = (
    "the harness cancelled the in-flight request; the server completed the call anyway"
)

HARNESS_FAULT_DESCRIPTIONS: dict[str, str] = {
    "worker_crash": DESC_WORKER_CRASH,
    "transport_abort": DESC_TRANSPORT_ABORT,
}

#: which layer really failed, per harness fault kind
HARNESS_FAULT_LAYERS: dict[str, ErrorLayer] = {
    "worker_crash": "harness",
    "transport_abort": "transport",
}

#: the agent-facing code each injected fault kind produces
INJECTED_CODE: dict[str, ErrorCode] = {
    "missing_file": "ENOENT",
    "denied_write": "EACCES",
    "ack_lost": "ETIMEDOUT",
}


def is_staged(kind: str, mode: str) -> bool:
    """True for the one fault class that changes the world instead of intercepting calls."""
    return kind == "missing_file" and mode == "sticky"

# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- classification

#: GRADING.md "mutating" for run_command, verbatim.
MUTATING_RE = re.compile(
    r"(>>?|\btee\b|\bsed\s+-i|\brm\b|\bmv\b|\bcp\b|\btouch\b|\bmkdir\b|\btruncate\b|\bpatch\b"
    r"|\bgit\s+(apply|checkout|reset|restore))"
)
#: `python … -c` whose body contains open(...) in a write/append mode.
_PY_DASH_C_RE = re.compile(r"\bpython[0-9.]*\b[^|;&]*?\s-c\b", re.S)
_PY_WRITE_MODE_RE = re.compile(r"""['"][wax][bt+]*['"]""")

#: GRADING.md "read" verbs for run_command (only consulted when the call is not mutating).
READ_VERB_RE = re.compile(
    r"\b(cat|head|tail|grep|egrep|fgrep|rg|wc|diff|less|more|cmp|sha256sum|md5sum|ls|stat|find"
    r"|file|nl|od|awk|sed|pytest|python[0-9.]*)\b"
)
#: Commands that only *enumerate* a directory. These never consume a transient missing_file hit —
#: we filter the vanished name out of the output instead, so the illusion stays consistent.
LISTING_VERB_RE = re.compile(r"\b(ls|find|tree|dir)\b")
_CONTENT_READ_RE = re.compile(r"\b(cat|head|tail|grep|egrep|fgrep|rg|wc|diff|less|more|cmp|sha256sum|md5sum|nl|od|awk|sed|pytest)\b")

_QUOTES = "\"'`"
_SPLIT_CHARS = re.compile(r"""[=(),;|&<>\[\]{}'"`]+""")


def normalize_token(tok: str) -> str:
    """Strip quotes, a leading ./ and a leading /workspace/ — GRADING.md token normalisation."""
    t = str(tok).strip()
    while len(t) >= 2 and t[0] in _QUOTES and t[-1] == t[0]:
        t = t[1:-1]
    t = t.strip(_QUOTES)
    if t == "/workspace":
        return "."
    if t.startswith("/workspace/"):
        t = t[len("/workspace/") :]
    while t.startswith("./"):
        t = t[2:]
    if len(t) > 1 and t.endswith("/"):
        t = t.rstrip("/")
    return t


def command_tokens(command: str) -> list[str]:
    """Argv tokens of a shell command, plus path-ish fragments embedded in quoted arguments.

    shlex gives us the argv the agent meant. The extra pass exists for one real case:
    `python -c 'open("config/settings.json", "w")'` is a single argv token, and GRADING.md's
    `mutating` rule explicitly expects that form to be attributable to a path.
    """
    try:
        toks = shlex.split(command, posix=True)
    except ValueError:
        toks = [t for t in re.split(r"\s+", command) if t]
    out: list[str] = []
    for t in toks:
        out.append(t)
        if _SPLIT_CHARS.search(t):
            out.extend(p for p in _SPLIT_CHARS.split(t) if p)
    # quoted fragments of the *raw* command (shlex already consumed the outer quoting)
    out.extend(re.findall(r"""['"]([^'"\n]+)['"]""", command))
    return out


def is_mutating(tool: str, args: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
    """GRADING.md `mutating`: write_file always; run_command by verb; reads never."""
    if tool == "write_file":
        return True
    if tool != "run_command":
        return False
    cmd = str(args.get("command", "") or "")
    if MUTATING_RE.search(cmd):
        return True
    m = _PY_DASH_C_RE.search(cmd)
    if m:
        body = cmd[m.end() :]
        if "open(" in body and _PY_WRITE_MODE_RE.search(body):
            return True
    return False


def is_listing(tool: str, args: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:

# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- path matching


def normalize_path(token: str) -> str:
    """GRADING.md token normalisation: strip quotes, `./` and a `/workspace/` prefix."""
    t = (token or "").strip().strip("'\"")
    if t.startswith("/workspace/"):
        t = t[len("/workspace/"):]
    while t.startswith("./"):
        t = t[2:]
    return t.rstrip("/")
